	public class AesControlHelper : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsAutoSelectAllTextProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsAutoSelectAllText", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlHelper), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits, IsAutoSelectAllTextPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContextTooltipProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ContextTooltip", typeof(object), typeof(AesControlHelper), new PropertyMetadata(OnContextTooltipPropertyChanged));

		public static DependencyProperty IsContextTooltipVisibleProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsContextTooltipVisible", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesControlHelper), new PropertyMetadata(OnIsContextTooltipVisiblePropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsInResultFormProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsInResultForm", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesControlHelper), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));

		public static bool GetIsAutoSelectAllText(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (bool)obj.GetValue(IsAutoSelectAllTextProperty);
		}

		public static void SetIsAutoSelectAllText(DependencyObject obj, bool value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(IsAutoSelectAllTextProperty, value);
		}

		private static void IsAutoSelectAllTextPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			AesXamValueEdit valueEdit = d as AesXamValueEdit;
			if (valueEdit != null && (bool)e.NewValue)
			{
				valueEdit.GotFocus += delegate(object o, RoutedEventArgs arg)
				{
					if (!valueEdit.IsReadOnly)
					{
						Utilities.GetDescendantFromType<TextBox>(o as AesXamValueEdit, allowSubclassOfType: true, SetAllText);
						Utilities.GetDescendantFromType<AesXamMaskedEdit>(o as AesXamValueEdit, allowSubclassOfType: true, SetAllText);
					}
				};
			}
			AesTextBoxWithStatusBar textEdit = d as AesTextBoxWithStatusBar;
			if (textEdit == null || !(bool)e.NewValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			textEdit.GotFocus += delegate(object o, RoutedEventArgs arg)
			{
				if (!textEdit.IsReadOnly)
				{
					Utilities.GetDescendantFromType<TextBox>(o as AesTextBoxWithStatusBar, allowSubclassOfType: true, SetAllText);
				}
			};
		}

		private static bool SetAllText(TextBox target)
		{
			target?.SelectAll();
			return true;
		}

		private static bool SetAllText(AesXamMaskedEdit target)
		{
			if (target != null)
			{
				target.EditModeStarted += delegate
				{
					target.SelectAll();
				};
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static object GetContextTooltip(DependencyObject owner)
		{
			if (owner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("owner");
			}
			return owner.GetValue(ContextTooltipProperty);
		}

		public static void SetContextTooltip(DependencyObject owner, object flag)
		{
			if (owner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("owner");
			}
			owner.SetValue(ContextTooltipProperty, flag);
		}

		private static void OnContextTooltipPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateTooltip(d);
		}

		private static void UpdateTooltip(DependencyObject target)
		{
			if (!(target is FrameworkElement frameworkElement))
			{
				return;
			}
			frameworkElement.ToolTipOpening -= owner_ToolTipOpening;
			frameworkElement.ToolTipClosing -= owner_ToolTipClosing;
			bool? isContextTooltipVisible = GetIsContextTooltipVisible(frameworkElement);
			if (isContextTooltipVisible.HasValue && !isContextTooltipVisible.Value)
			{
				frameworkElement.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty);
				return;
			}
			object contextTooltip = GetContextTooltip(frameworkElement);
			if (contextTooltip == null)
			{
				frameworkElement.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty);
				return;
			}
			frameworkElement.ToolTip = contextTooltip;
			frameworkElement.ToolTipOpening += owner_ToolTipOpening;
			frameworkElement.ToolTipClosing += owner_ToolTipClosing;
		}

		private static void owner_ToolTipOpening(object sender, ToolTipEventArgs e)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = sender as FrameworkElement;
			if (GetContextTooltip(frameworkElement) is FrameworkElement frameworkElement2)
			{
				frameworkElement2.DataContext = frameworkElement;
			}
		}

		private static void owner_ToolTipClosing(object sender, ToolTipEventArgs e)
		{
			if (GetContextTooltip(sender as FrameworkElement) is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				if (frameworkElement.Parent is ToolTip toolTip)
				{
					toolTip.Content = null;
				}
				frameworkElement.DataContext = null;
			}
		}

		public static void SetIsContextTooltipVisible(DependencyObject target, bool? value)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			target.SetValue(IsContextTooltipVisibleProperty, value);
		}

		public static bool? GetIsContextTooltipVisible(DependencyObject target)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			return (bool?)target.GetValue(IsContextTooltipVisibleProperty);
		}

		private static void OnIsContextTooltipVisiblePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateTooltip(d);
		}

		public static bool? GetIsInResultForm(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (bool?)obj.GetValue(IsInResultFormProperty);
		}

		public static void SetIsInResultForm(DependencyObject obj, bool? value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(IsInResultFormProperty, value);
		}
	}
	public class TooltipContentTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string TooltipTemplatePropertyPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public DataTemplate FallbackTooltipTemplate { get; set; }

		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			if (container != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(TooltipTemplatePropertyPath))
			{
				UIElement uIElement = container.GetAncestor<ToolTip>()?.PlacementTarget;
				if (uIElement != null)
				{
					Binding binding = new Binding(TooltipTemplatePropertyPath)
					{
						Source = uIElement,
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					};
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = new FrameworkElement();
					frameworkElement.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.TagProperty, binding);
					if (frameworkElement.Tag is DataTemplate result)
					{
						return result;
					}
				}
			}
			return FallbackTooltipTemplate ?? base.SelectTemplate(item, container);
		}
	}
	public enum AesDisplayedCase
	{
		None,
		Uppercase,
		Lowercase
	}
	[ContentProperty("Content")]
	[Localizability(LocalizationCategory.CheckBox)]
	public class AesCheckBox : AesValueControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CommandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CommandParameterProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCheckedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentProperty;

		private ContentControl _bindingClearControl;

		private bool result;

		public ICommand Command
		{
			get
			{
				return (ICommand)GetValue(CommandProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CommandProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object CommandParameter
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(CommandParameterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CommandParameterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool? IsChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool?)GetValue(IsCheckedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsCheckedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object Content
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static AesCheckBox()
		{
			CommandProperty = ButtonBase.CommandProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesCheckBox));
			CommandParameterProperty = ButtonBase.CommandParameterProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesCheckBox));
			IsCheckedProperty = ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesCheckBox));
			ContentProperty = ContentControl.ContentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesCheckBox));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesCheckBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesCheckBox)));
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, IsCheckedProperty, "Value"));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCanClear(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsMMEnabled || base.IsReadOnly || !base.IsEntered || base.Variable == null)
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_bindingClearControl == null)
			{
				_bindingClearControl = new ContentControl();
			}
			Binding binding = new Binding
			{
				Source = base.Variable,
				Path = new PropertyPath("Value"),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWayToSource
			};
			_bindingClearControl.SetBinding(ContentControl.ContentProperty, binding);
			BindingOperations.ClearBinding(_bindingClearControl, ContentControl.ContentProperty);
			BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(this, IsCheckedProperty)?.UpdateTarget();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsMMEnabled && !base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				if (!IsChecked.HasValue || !IsChecked.Value)
				{
					IsChecked = true;
				}
				else
				{
					IsChecked = false;
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnCanPaste(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsEnabled && base.IsMMEnabled && !base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				string text = ClipboardWrapper.GetData(DataFormats.Text) as string;
				e.CanExecute = text != null && bool.TryParse(text, out result);
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPaste(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Value = result;
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Content")]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_BUTTON", Type = typeof(Button))]
	public class AesCmdButton : AesVariableControlBase
	{
		private const string TemplatePartOfButton = "PART_BUTTON";

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CommandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CommandParameterProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsDefaultProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCancelProperty;

		public ICommand Command
		{
			get
			{
				return (ICommand)GetValue(CommandProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CommandProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object CommandParameter
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(CommandParameterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CommandParameterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object Content
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public override string TextForCopy
		{
			get
			{
				if (Content != null)
				{
					return Content.ToString();
				}
				return base.TextForCopy;
			}
		}

		public bool IsDefault
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsDefaultProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsDefaultProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsCancel
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsCancelProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsCancelProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static AesCmdButton()
		{
			CommandProperty = ButtonBase.CommandProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesCmdButton));
			CommandParameterProperty = ButtonBase.CommandParameterProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesCmdButton));
			ContentProperty = ContentControl.ContentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesCmdButton));
			IsDefaultProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsDefault", typeof(bool), typeof(AesCmdButton), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			IsCancelProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsCancel", typeof(bool), typeof(AesCmdButton), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesCmdButton), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesCmdButton)));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (base.Template.FindName("PART_BUTTON", this) is Button button)
			{
				button.Click += templatedButton_Click;
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		private void templatedButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnClick();
		}

		protected virtual void OnClick()
		{
		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
			if (base.VariablePath == null)
			{
				BindingExpression bindingExpression = BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(this, CommandProperty);
				if (bindingExpression != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding.Path != null)
				{
					OnScriptingVariablePathDisplayed(bindingExpression.ParentBinding.Path.Path);
				}
			}
		}
	}

			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemTooltipTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static AesComboEdit()
		{
			ValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Value", typeof(object), typeof(AesComboEdit));
			ValueOptionsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueOptions", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(AesComboEdit));
			VariableDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariableDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(AesComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("SelectedItem"));
			PromptsDictionaryProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PromptsDictionary", typeof(Dictionary<string, string>), typeof(AesComboEdit));
			DisplayedCaseProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayedCase", typeof(AesDisplayedCase), typeof(AesComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesDisplayedCase.None));
			MaskProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Mask", typeof(AesMaskOption), typeof(AesComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesMaskOption.None));
			DisplayAsTextBoxProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayAsTextBox", typeof(bool), typeof(AesComboEdit));
			IsInScrollAreaProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollArea", typeof(bool), typeof(AesComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			IsInScrollAreaColumnHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollAreaColumnHeader", typeof(bool), typeof(AesComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			DisplayValueToRenderTextConverterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayValueToRenderTextConverter", typeof(IValueConverter), typeof(AesComboEdit));
			IsEditableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsEditable", typeof(bool), typeof(AesComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			UpdateSourceWhenValueChangedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UpdateSourceWhenValueChanged", typeof(bool), typeof(AesComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			AllowClearProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowClear", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(null));
			TooltipTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TooltipTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(AesComboEdit));
			ItemTooltipTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemTooltipTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(AesComboEdit));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesComboEdit)));
			FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(21.0));
			Control.IsTabStopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
		}

		public AesComboEdit()
		{
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Remove(base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.First((ExtraAutoBindingItem x) => x.TargetProperty == AesControlBase.PromptProperty));
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(AesControlBase.PromptProperty, AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CreateComboPromptBinding(this)));
		}

		internal static BindingBase CreateComboPromptBinding(UIElement element)
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.VariablePrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.DisabledPrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsMMEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.Value")
					{
						Source = element
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesComboPromptConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = string.Empty
			};
		}

		protected override BindingBase CreateIsMMEnabledBinding()
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.IsReadOnly")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding(AesVariableControlBase.ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = this,
						Path = new PropertyPath(ValueOptionsProperty)
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsEditable")
					{
						Source = this
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesComboEditIsMMEnabledConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			};
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				if (string.Compare(VariableDataMember, "SelectedItem", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ValueOptionsProperty, "Items")
					{
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					});
					items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ValueProperty, VariableDataMember));
				}
				else
				{
					items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ValueOptionsProperty, VariableDataMember + "Options")
					{
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					});
					items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ValueProperty, VariableDataMember));
				}
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, PromptsDictionaryProperty, "OptionPrompts")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected override void GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			if (properties != null)
			{
				properties.Add(VariableDataMemberProperty);
				base.GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(properties);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCanClear(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (AllowClear.HasValue && AllowClear.Value)
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
			else if ((AllowClear.HasValue && !AllowClear.Value) || !base.IsMMEnabled || base.IsReadOnly || (!base.IsEntered && !base.IsCalculated) || base.Variable == null || DisplayAsTextBox)
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!TryClearDynamically() && base.Template.FindName("PART_editControl", this) is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit)
			{
				aesXamComboEdit.Value = null;
				aesXamComboEdit.GetBindingExpression(ValueEditor.ValueProperty)?.UpdateSource();
			}
		}

		protected override void BuildDefaultContextMenu(ContextMenu menu)
		{
			base.BuildDefaultContextMenu(menu);
			AesXamComboEdit target = base.Template.FindName("PART_editControl", this) as AesXamComboEdit;
			CorrectCommandTarget(menu, target);
		}

		private bool TryClearDynamically()
		{
			if (base.Variable != null)
			{
				PropertyInfo property = base.Variable.GetType().GetProperty("Value");
				if (property != null)
				{
					try
					{
						property.SetValue(base.Variable, null);
						return true;
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public abstract class AesControlBase : Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsMMEnabledProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PromptProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent PromptDisplayedEvent;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsReadOnlyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCalculatedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsEnteredProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty;

		public bool IsMMEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsMMEnabledProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsMMEnabledProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string Prompt
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(PromptProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PromptProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool SuppressSimulatorStatusCheck
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsReadOnlyProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsReadOnlyProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsCalculated
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsCalculatedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsCalculatedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsEntered
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsEnteredProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsEnteredProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<RoutedEventArgs<string>> PromptDisplayed
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(PromptDisplayedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(PromptDisplayedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnPromptChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			AesControlBase aesControlBase = d as AesControlBase;
			if (e.NewValue != null && aesControlBase.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				aesControlBase.OnPromptDisplayed(e.NewValue.ToString());
			}
		}

		static AesControlBase()
		{
			IsMMEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsMMEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			PromptProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Prompt", typeof(string), typeof(AesControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnPromptChanged));
			PromptDisplayedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("PromptDisplayed", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<RoutedEventArgs<string>>), typeof(AesControlBase));
			IsReadOnlyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsReadOnly", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBase));
			IsCalculatedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsCalculated", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			IsEnteredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsEntered", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SuppressSimulatorStatusCheck", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			FrameworkElement.FocusVisualStyleProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			base.OnPreviewMouseDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				Focus();
			}
			base.OnMouseDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPromptDisplayed(string prompt)
		{
			RoutedEventArgs<string> e = new RoutedEventArgs<string>(prompt, PromptDisplayedEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new UIACustomControlAutomationPeer(this)
			{
				ClassName = GetType().Name,
				GetNameFunction = (FrameworkElement control) => (control as AesControlBase).Name
			};
		}
	}
	public abstract class AesAutoBindControlBase : AesControlBase, IAutoBindControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty AutoBindProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoBind", typeof(bool), typeof(AesAutoBindControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));

		protected AutoBindControlHelper AutoBindControlHelper { get; private set; }

		public bool AutoBind
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AutoBindProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AutoBindProperty, value);
			}
		}

		bool IAutoBindControl.AutoBind => AutoBind;

		protected AesAutoBindControlBase()
		{
			AutoBindControlHelper = new AutoBindControlHelper(this);
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			AutoBindControlHelper.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			try
			{
				base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (AutoBindControlHelper.IsInAutoBinding(dp))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		protected abstract void GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items);

		protected abstract void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items);

		protected virtual void GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			properties?.Add(AutoBindProperty);
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(properties);
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(items);
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
		}
	}
	public abstract class AesVariableControlBase : AesAutoBindControlBase, IDraggingDataProvider

		};

				},

					RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,

				{
					RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				},
				(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.Variable.Value")
				{
					RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				}
			}
		};

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VariableProperty;

		private Point _mouseDownPosition;

		private AesVariableControlBase _mousedragControl;

		public object Variable

		string IDraggingDataProvider.CxsMoniker => DataForClipboard.GetCxsItemMoniker(this);

		{

		{

				AddHandler(DefaultContextMenuInvokingEvent, value);

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)

			{
				OnScriptingVariablePathDisplayed(VariablePath);

			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
		}

		public virtual void OnScriptingVariablePathDisplayed(string path)
		{
			RoutedEventArgs<string> e = new RoutedEventArgs<string>(path, ScriptingDisplayVariablePathEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		static AesVariableControlBase()
		{
			VariableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Variable", typeof(object), typeof(AesVariableControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnVariablePropertyChanged));
			VariablePathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariablePath", typeof(string), typeof(AesVariableControlBase));
			ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnly", typeof(bool), typeof(AesVariableControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			DefaultVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(AesVariableControlBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Visibility.Visible));
			DefaultContextMenuInvokingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DefaultContextMenuInvoking", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<RoutedEventArgs<ContextMenu>>), typeof(AesVariableControlBase));
			_clearVariableCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.Clear, "Clear", typeof(AesVariableControlBase));
			ScriptingDisplayVariablePathEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ScriptingDisplayVariablePathEvent", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<RoutedEventArgs<string>>), typeof(AesVariableControlBase));
			isDraging = false;
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesVariableControlBase), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Copy, Copy_Executed, Copy_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesVariableControlBase), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Paste, Paste_Executed, Paste_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesVariableControlBase), new CommandBinding(ClearVariableCommand, Clear_Executed, Clear_CanExecute));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(AesVariableControlBase), DataObject.CopyingEvent, new DataObjectCopyingEventHandler(DataObjectCopyingEvent), handledEventsToo: true);
		}

		private static void DataObjectCopyingEvent(object source, DataObjectEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.IsDragDrop && !e.Handled && source is AesValueControlBase aesValueControlBase)
			{
				e.CancelCommand();
				aesValueControlBase.OnDrag(e);
			}
		}

		protected AesVariableControlBase()
		{
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.CaseFullFilePathProperty, _clipboardCaseFullFilePathBinding);
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.ApplicationNameProperty, _clipboardApplicationNameBinding);
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.CxsItemMonikerProperty, _clipboardItemMonikerBinding);
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.ValueProperty, _clipboardValueBinding);
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(AesControlBase.IsMMEnabledProperty, VariableProperty, CreateIsMMEnabledBinding()));
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(AesControlBase.PromptProperty, VariableProperty, CreatePromptBinding(this)));
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, CreateVisibilityBinding(this))
			{
				SourceProperties = { VariableProperty, DefaultVisibilityProperty }
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnVariableChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
		}

		internal static BindingBase CreateDefaultIsMMEnabledBinding(UIElement element, string shouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyPropertyName)
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.IsReadOnly")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)(string.IsNullOrEmpty(shouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyPropertyName) ? new Binding
					{
						Source = true
					} : new Binding(shouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyPropertyName)
					{
						Source = element
					})
				},
				Converter = new AesControlIsEnabledConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = true
			};
		}

		internal static BindingBase CreatePromptBinding(UIElement element)
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.VariablePrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.DisabledPrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsMMEnabled")
					{
						Source = element

					{
						Source = element

					{

		internal static BindingBase CreateVisibilityBinding(AesVariableControlBase control)
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.IsVisible")
					{

						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding

						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay

				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = control.DefaultVisibility
			};
		}

		protected virtual BindingBase CreateIsMMEnabledBinding()
		{
			return CreateDefaultIsMMEnabledBinding(this, ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyProperty.Name);
		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			InitContextMenu(e);
			base.OnContextMenuOpening(e);
		}

		internal void InitContextMenu(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ReadLocalValue(FrameworkElement.ContextMenuProperty) != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_defaultContextMenu == null)
			{
				_defaultContextMenu = new ContextMenu();
				BuildDefaultContextMenu(_defaultContextMenu);
			}
			if (_defaultContextMenu.Items.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			OnDefaultContextMenuInvoking(new RoutedEventArgs<ContextMenu>(_defaultContextMenu, DefaultContextMenuInvokingEvent, this));
			if (_defaultContextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
			{
				_defaultContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				if (e != null)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void BuildDefaultContextMenu(ContextMenu menu)
		{
			string text = "pack://application:,,,/" + typeof(AesControlBase).Assembly.FullName + ";component/Images/";
			if (menu != null)
			{
				Image image = new Image();
				image.Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Undo16.ico"));
				menu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderUndo,
					InputGestureText = "",
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Undo,
					CommandTarget = this,
					Icon = image
				});
				menu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				Image image2 = new Image();
				image2.Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Cut16.ico"));
				menu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderCut,
					InputGestureText = "",
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Cut,
					CommandTarget = this,
					Icon = image2
				});
				Image image3 = new Image();
				image3.Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Copy16.ico"));
				menu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderCopy,
					InputGestureText = "",
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Copy,
					CommandTarget = this,
					Icon = image3
				});
				Image image4 = new Image();
				image4.Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Paste16.ico"));
				menu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderPaste,
					InputGestureText = "",
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Paste,
					CommandTarget = this,
					Icon = image4
				});
				menu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				menu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderSelectAll,
					InputGestureText = "",
					Command = ApplicationCommands.SelectAll,
					CommandTarget = this
				});
				menu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				Image image5 = new Image();
				image5.Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Clear16.ico"));
				menu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Command = ClearVariableCommand,
					CommandTarget = this,
					Icon = image5
				});
			}
		}

		protected void CorrectCommandTarget(ContextMenu menu, Control target)
		{
			if (menu == null || target == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)menu.Items)
			{
				if (item is MenuItem)
				{
					MenuItem menuItem = item as MenuItem;
					if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Undo)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = target;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Copy)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = target;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Paste)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = target;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Cut)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = target;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.SelectAll)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = target;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefaultContextMenuInvoking(RoutedEventArgs<ContextMenu> e)
		{
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (Helpers.PropertyAndBindingMatch(this, dp, DataForClipboard.CaseFullFilePathProperty, _clipboardCaseFullFilePathBinding))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Helpers.PropertyAndBindingMatch(this, dp, DataForClipboard.ApplicationNameProperty, _clipboardApplicationNameBinding))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Helpers.PropertyAndBindingMatch(this, dp, DataForClipboard.CxsItemMonikerProperty, _clipboardItemMonikerBinding))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Helpers.PropertyAndBindingMatch(this, dp, DataForClipboard.ValueProperty, _clipboardValueBinding))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			items?.Add(new VariablePathToVariableBindingItem(VariablePathProperty, VariableProperty));
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
		}

		private static void Copy_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as AesVariableControlBase).OnCopy(e);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private static void Copy_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as AesVariableControlBase).OnCanCopy(e);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnCopy(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ClipboardHelper.CreateClipboard(new Collection<string>(new string[1] { DataForClipboard.GetCxsItemMoniker(this) }), TextForCopy, DataForClipboard.GetCaseFullFilePath(this), DataForClipboard.GetApplicationName(this));
		}

		protected virtual void OnCanCopy(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(TextForCopy) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(DataForClipboard.GetCxsItemMoniker(this));
		}

		private static void Paste_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as AesVariableControlBase).OnPaste(e);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private static void Paste_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as AesVariableControlBase).OnCanPaste(e);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnPaste(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnCanPaste(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				string text = ClipboardWrapper.GetData(DataFormats.Text) as string;
				e.CanExecute = text != null && base.IsEnabled && base.IsMMEnabled && !base.IsReadOnly;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		public void ClearValue()
		{
			OnClear(null);
		}

		protected virtual void OnCanClear(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = false;
		}

		internal void CanClear(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnCanClear(e);
		}

		private static void Clear_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as AesVariableControlBase).OnClear(e);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private static void Clear_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AesVariableControlBase aesVariableControlBase = sender as AesVariableControlBase;
			if (SimulatorStatusHelperForControls.Default.IsRunning || SimulatorStatusHelperForControls.Default.IsPausedByUser)
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				aesVariableControlBase.OnCanClear(e);
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject, typeof(AesVariableControlBase), allowSubclassOfType: true) is AesVariableControlBase aesVariableControlBase && Utilities.GetDescendantFromType(aesVariableControlBase, typeof(ValueEditor), allowSubclassOfType: true) is ValueEditor { IsInEditMode: false })
			{
				_mousedragControl = aesVariableControlBase;
				_mouseDownPosition = e.GetPosition(_mousedragControl);
				base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
			else
			{
				_mousedragControl = null;
				base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_mousedragControl != null)
			{
				_mousedragControl = null;
			}
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Mouse.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed && !e.Handled && _mousedragControl != null)
			{
				Vector vector = e.GetPosition(_mousedragControl) - _mouseDownPosition;
				if (Math.Abs(vector.X) > SystemParameters.MinimumHorizontalDragDistance || Math.Abs(vector.Y) > SystemParameters.MinimumVerticalDragDistance)
				{
					string cxsItemMoniker = DataForClipboard.GetCxsItemMoniker(_mousedragControl);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxsItemMoniker))
					{
						try
						{
							Collection<string> monikers = new Collection<string>(new string[1] { cxsItemMoniker });
							string caseFullFilePath = DataForClipboard.GetCaseFullFilePath(_mousedragControl);
							string applicationName = DataForClipboard.GetApplicationName(_mousedragControl);
							string textForCopy = _mousedragControl.TextForCopy;
							DataObject dataObject = new DataObject();
							MemoryStream asiFileXml = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiFileXml(caseFullFilePath, applicationName);
							if (asiFileXml != null)
							{
								dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI File Format").Name, asiFileXml);
							}
							string asiSelectionXml = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiSelectionXml(monikers, caseFullFilePath, applicationName);
							if (asiSelectionXml != null)
							{
								dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI Format").Name, asiSelectionXml);
							}
							dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.Text, (textForCopy != null) ? textForCopy : string.Empty);
							e.Handled = true;
							DragDrop.DoDragDrop(_mousedragControl, dataObject, DragDropEffects.Copy);
							_mousedragControl = null;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
				}
			}
			base.OnPreviewMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDrag(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (isDraging)
			{
				return;
			}
			string cxsItemMoniker = DataForClipboard.GetCxsItemMoniker(this);
			if (cxsItemMoniker != null)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
				isDraging = true;
				Collection<string> monikers = new Collection<string>(new string[1] { cxsItemMoniker });
				string caseFullFilePath = DataForClipboard.GetCaseFullFilePath(this);
				string applicationName = DataForClipboard.GetApplicationName(this);
				string textForCopy = TextForCopy;
				DataObject dataObject = new DataObject();
				MemoryStream asiFileXml = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiFileXml(caseFullFilePath, applicationName);
				if (asiFileXml != null)
				{
					dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI File Format").Name, asiFileXml);
				}
				string asiSelectionXml = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiSelectionXml(monikers, caseFullFilePath, applicationName);
				if (asiSelectionXml != null)
				{
					dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI Format").Name, asiSelectionXml);
				}
				dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.Text, (textForCopy != null) ? textForCopy : string.Empty);
				DragDrop.DoDragDrop(this, dataObject, DragDropEffects.Copy);
				isDraging = false;
			}
		}

		private static void OnVariablePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesVariableControlBase).OnVariableChanged(e.OldValue, e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public abstract class AesAttachedControlBase : AesVariableControlBase
	{
		private readonly Dictionary<DependencyProperty, BindingBase> _bindings = new Dictionary<DependencyProperty, BindingBase>();

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TargetControlProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TargetControl", typeof(string), typeof(AesAttachedControlBase));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TargetProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Target", typeof(AesVariableControlBase), typeof(AesAttachedControlBase));

		[Localizability(LocalizationCategory.NeverLocalize)]
		public string TargetControl
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(TargetControlProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TargetControlProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public AesVariableControlBase Target
		{
			get
			{
				return (AesVariableControlBase)GetValue(TargetProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(TargetProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public override string TextForCopy
		{
			get
			{
				if (Target != null)
				{
					return Target.TextForCopy;
				}
				return base.TextForCopy;
			}
		}

		protected AesAttachedControlBase()
		{
			base.Loaded += AesAttachedControlBase_Loaded;
			base.Unloaded += AesAttachedControlBase_Unloaded;
		}

		private void AesAttachedControlBase_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TargetControl))
			{
				BindToTargetControl(TargetControl);
			}
		}

		private void AesAttachedControlBase_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_bindings.Clear();
			ClearValue(TargetProperty);
			if (Target == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (AttachedBindingItem bindingItem in GetBindingItems())
			{
				ClearValue(bindingItem.TargetProperty);
			}
		}

		private void BindToTargetControl(string targetControl)
		{
			_bindings.Clear();
			Binding binding = new Binding
			{
				ElementName = targetControl
			};
			SetBinding(TargetProperty, binding);
			_bindings[TargetProperty] = binding;
			if (Target == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (AttachedBindingItem bindingItem in GetBindingItems())
			{
				BindingBase bindingBase = new Binding
				{
					ElementName = targetControl,
					Path = new PropertyPath(bindingItem.SourceProperty)
				};
				SetBinding(bindingItem.TargetProperty, bindingBase);
				_bindings[bindingItem.TargetProperty] = bindingBase;
			}
		}

		private List<AttachedBindingItem> GetBindingItems()
		{
			List<AttachedBindingItem> list = new List<AttachedBindingItem>();
			GenerateBindingItems(list);
			return list;
		}

		protected virtual void GenerateBindingItems(IList<AttachedBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				items.Add(new AttachedBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariablePathProperty, AesVariableControlBase.VariablePathProperty));
				items.Add(new AttachedBindingItem(AesControlBase.IsMMEnabledProperty, AesControlBase.IsMMEnabledProperty));
				items.Add(new AttachedBindingItem(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, UIElement.VisibilityProperty));
				items.Add(new AttachedBindingItem(AesControlBase.PromptProperty, AesControlBase.PromptProperty));
				items.Add(new AttachedBindingItem(AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty));
				items.Add(new AttachedBindingItem(AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty));
			}
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (IsPropertyInBinding(dp))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		private bool IsPropertyInBinding(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			BindingBase bindingBase = BindingOperations.GetBindingBase(this, dp);
			if (bindingBase != null && _bindings.ContainsKey(dp) && _bindings[dp] == bindingBase)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public abstract class AesValueControlBase : AesVariableControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Value", typeof(object), typeof(AesValueControlBase));

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ValueProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ValueProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public override string TextForCopy
		{
			get
			{
				if (Value != null)
				{
					return Value.ToString();
				}
				return base.TextForCopy;
			}
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ValueProperty, ValueProperty.Name));
			}
		}
	}
	public class AesControlBorder : ContentControl, ISimulatorStatusReceiver
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsMMEnabledProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty IsSimulatorRunningProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyProperty IsSimulatorPausedByUserProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsContentEnabledPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsContentEnabledProperty;

		public bool IsMMEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsMMEnabledProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsMMEnabledProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool SuppressSimulatorStatusCheck
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsContentEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsContentEnabledProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(IsContentEnabledPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public SimulatorStatusHelperForControls SimulatorStatus => SimulatorStatusHelperForControls.Default;

		static AesControlBorder()
		{
			IsMMEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsMMEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBorder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnIsMMEnabledPropertyChanged));
			SuppressSimulatorStatusCheckProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SuppressSimulatorStatusCheck", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBorder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnSuppressSimulatorStatusCheckPropertyChanged));
			IsSimulatorRunningProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsSimulatorRunning", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBorder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnIsSimulatorRunningPropertyChanged));
			IsSimulatorPausedByUserProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsSimulatorPausedByUser", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBorder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(IsSimulatorPausedByUserPropertyChanged));
			IsContentEnabledPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsContentEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(AesControlBorder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			IsContentEnabledProperty = IsContentEnabledPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesControlBorder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesControlBorder)));
			AesControlHelper.IsInResultFormProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesControlBorder), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnIsInResultFormPropertyChanged));
		}

		public AesControlBorder()
		{
			SetBinding(IsSimulatorRunningProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = SimulatorStatus,
				Path = new PropertyPath("IsRunning"),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			SetBinding(IsSimulatorPausedByUserProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = SimulatorStatus,
				Path = new PropertyPath("IsPausedByUser"),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		private void RefreshIsContentEnabled()
		{
			if (SuppressSimulatorStatusCheck)
			{
				IsContentEnabled = IsMMEnabled;
			}
			else if ((bool)GetValue(IsSimulatorRunningProperty))
			{
				IsContentEnabled = false;
			}
			else if (!(bool)GetValue(IsSimulatorPausedByUserProperty))
			{
				IsContentEnabled = IsMMEnabled;
			}
			else
			{
				IsContentEnabled = IsMMEnabled && AesControlHelper.GetIsInResultForm(this) == true;
			}
		}

		private static void OnIsMMEnabledPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesControlBorder).RefreshIsContentEnabled();
		}

		private static void OnSuppressSimulatorStatusCheckPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesControlBorder).RefreshIsContentEnabled();
		}

		private static void OnIsSimulatorRunningPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesControlBorder).RefreshIsContentEnabled();
		}

		private static void IsSimulatorPausedByUserPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesControlBorder).RefreshIsContentEnabled();
		}

		private static void OnIsInResultFormPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesControlBorder).RefreshIsContentEnabled();
		}
	}
	public class ObjectToDoubleConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && double.TryParse(value.ToString(), out var result))
			{
				return result;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value;
		}
	}
	public class AesVariableEditSizeConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values == null)
			{
				return GridLength.Auto;
			}
			if (values.Length != 2)
			{
				return GridLength.Auto;
			}
			if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return GridLength.Auto;
			}
			GridLength gridLength = (GridLength)values[0];
			if ((Visibility)values[1] == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				return gridLength;
			}
			return GridLength.Auto;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class AesVariableEditMarginConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values == null)
			{
				return new Thickness(0.0);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
			{
				if ((Visibility)values[i] == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return parameter;
				}
			}
			return new Thickness(0.0);
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class AesControlIsEnabledConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length == 3 && values[0] != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue && values[2] != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				bool? flag = values[0] as bool?;
				bool? flag2 = values[1] as bool?;
				bool flag3 = (bool)values[2];
				if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (flag3 && flag2.HasValue)
				{
					return !flag2.Value;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class AesControlVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter, IMultiValueConverter
	{
		object IValueConverter.Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				bool? flag = value as bool?;
				if (flag.HasValue)
				{
					return (!flag.Value) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
			return Visibility.Visible;
		}

		object IValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		object IMultiValueConverter.Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length == 2)
			{
				if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					return (Visibility)values[1];
				}
				bool? flag = values[0] as bool?;
				if (flag.HasValue)
				{
					return (!flag.Value) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
			return Visibility.Visible;
		}

		object[] IMultiValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class AesControlPromptConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string toolTip = GetToolTip(values);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(toolTip))
			{
				if (targetType == typeof(object))
				{
					return new TextBlock
					{
						Text = toolTip,
						MaxWidth = 500.0,
						TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap
					};
				}
				if (targetType == typeof(string))
				{
					return toolTip;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static string GetToolTip(object[] values)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length > 4)
			{
				string text = ((values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? null : (values[0] as string));
				string text2 = ((values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? null : (values[1] as string));
				bool? flag = ((values[2] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? ((bool?)null) : (values[2] as bool?));
				bool? flag2 = ((values[3] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? ((bool?)null) : (values[3] as bool?));
				double? num = null;
				double? num2 = null;
				bool? flag3 = null;
				object obj = ((values[4] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? null : values[4]);
				if (values.Length > 7)
				{
					num = ((values[5] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? ((double?)null) : (values[5] as double?));
					num2 = ((values[6] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? ((double?)null) : (values[6] as double?));
					flag3 = ((values[7] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? ((bool?)null) : (values[7] as bool?));
				}
				bool flag4 = (flag ?? true) && (flag2 ?? true);
				if (!(flag ?? true))
				{
					List<IAesControlContainer> list = new List<IAesControlContainer>();
					for (DependencyObject dependencyObject = obj as DependencyObject; dependencyObject != null; dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject))
					{
						if (dependencyObject is IAesControlContainer item)
						{
							list.Insert(0, item);
						}
					}
					foreach (IAesControlContainer item2 in list)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item2.DisabledPrompt))
						{
							return item2.DisabledPrompt;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag4 && text2 != null)
				{
					return text2;
				}
				if (values.Length == 4 || (!num.HasValue && !num2.HasValue) || !flag4 || (flag3 ?? false))
				{
					return text;
				}
				if (!num2.HasValue)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text.Trim()))
					{
						return text + "\n" + string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.LowerLimitPrompt, num);
					}
					return string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.LowerLimitPrompt, num);
				}
				if (!num.HasValue)

						return text + "\n" + string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.UpperLimitPrompt, num2);
					}
					return string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.UpperLimitPrompt, num2);
				}

				{

				}
				return string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.LimitsPrompt, num, num2);

		}

	public class AesControlFormatConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		private static string _defaultFormat = "g6";

		private static Dictionary<string, string> _formats = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		public static string DefaultFormat
		{
			get
			{
				return _defaultFormat;
			}
			set
			{
				_defaultFormat = value;
			}
		}

		private static void SetFormat(string dimension, string format)
		{
			if (_formats.Keys.Contains(dimension))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(format))
				{
					_formats.Remove(dimension);
				}
				else
				{
					_formats[dimension] = format;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_formats.Add(dimension, format);
			}
		}

		private static string GetFormat(string dimension)
		{
			if (dimension != null && _formats.Keys.Contains(dimension))
			{
				return _formats[dimension];
			}
			return _defaultFormat;
		}

		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length >= 2)
			{
				if (values[0] != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					if (values[0] as Type == typeof(double))
					{
						return GetFormat((values[1] == null || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) ? null : values[1].ToString());
					}
				}
				else if (values.Length == 3 && values[2] != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue && (AesMaskOption)values[2] == AesMaskOption.Double)
				{
					return _defaultFormat;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	public class OptionPromptToToolTipConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public static string ConvertToPrompt(object valueToConvert, object control)
		{
			object[] values = new object[2] { valueToConvert, control };
			if (values[0] == null || values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (values[1] != null && values[1] != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				if (values[1].GetType() == typeof(AesListPicker) || values[1].GetType().IsSubclassOf(typeof(AesListPicker)))
				{
					if (values[1] is AesListPicker aesListPicker)
					{
						if (aesListPicker.PromptsDictionary != null && aesListPicker.PromptsDictionary.Count((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(values[0].ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) > 0)
						{
							string value = aesListPicker.PromptsDictionary.FirstOrDefault((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(values[0].ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).Value;
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
							{
								return value;
							}
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(aesListPicker.Prompt))
						{
							return aesListPicker.Prompt;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (values[1].GetType() == typeof(AesXamComboEdit) && values[1] is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit)
				{
					if (aesXamComboEdit.PromptsDictionary != null && aesXamComboEdit.PromptsDictionary.Count((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(values[0].ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) > 0)
					{
						string value2 = aesXamComboEdit.PromptsDictionary.FirstOrDefault((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(values[0].ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).Value;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2))
						{
							return value2;
						}
					}
					if (aesXamComboEdit.Variable != null)
					{
						PropertyInfo property = aesXamComboEdit.Variable.GetType().GetProperty("VariablePrompt");
						if (property != null)
						{
							string text = property.GetValue(aesXamComboEdit.Variable, null) as string;
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								return text;
							}
						}
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(aesXamComboEdit.Prompt))
					{
						return aesXamComboEdit.Prompt;
					}
				}
			}
			return values[0].ToString();
		}

		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return ConvertToPrompt(values[0], values[1]);
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return true;
			}

			{
				return !result;
			}
			return false;
		}

	public class TextAlignmentConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values == null || values.Length != 2)
			{
				return TextAlignment.Left;
			}
			AesMaskOption? aesMaskOption = values[0] as AesMaskOption?;
			if (aesMaskOption.HasValue)
			{
				if (aesMaskOption.Value == AesMaskOption.Integer || aesMaskOption.Value == AesMaskOption.Double)
				{
					Type type = values[1] as Type;
					if (type == typeof(int) || type == typeof(double))
					{
						return TextAlignment.Right;
					}
				}
				else if (aesMaskOption.Value == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
				{
					return HorizontalAlignment.Right;
				}
			}
			return TextAlignment.Left;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

				{
					Type type = values[1] as Type;
					if (type == typeof(int) || type == typeof(double))
					{
						return HorizontalAlignment.Right;
					}
				}
				else if (aesMaskOption.Value == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
				{
					return HorizontalAlignment.Right;
				}

		}

	public class DisplayTextConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values.Length < 2 || !(values[0] is string) || !(values[1] is AesMaskOption))
			{
				return values[0];
			}
			AesMaskOption? aesMaskOption = values[1] as AesMaskOption?;
			bool flag = values.Length != 3 || (values[2] != null && values[2] is Type && (Type)values[2] == typeof(double));
			if (aesMaskOption.HasValue && ((aesMaskOption == AesMaskOption.Double && flag) || aesMaskOption == AesMaskOption.Percentage) && values[0] != null)
			{
				string text = values[0].ToString().Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol());
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return values[0];
				}
				if (aesMaskOption == AesMaskOption.Double)
				{
					if (double.TryParse(text, out var result))
					{
						return text = result.ToString("g6");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					bool flag2 = text.EndsWith("%");
					if (double.TryParse(flag2 ? text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1) : text, out var result2))
					{
						return text = result2.ToString("g6") + (flag2 ? "%" : string.Empty);
					}
				}
			}
			return values[0];
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class LocalizedRealConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value.GetType() == typeof(string))
			{
				string text = value.ToString().Trim();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					int num = text.IndexOf(' ');
					if (num > 0)
					{
						string text2 = text.Substring(0, num);
						string text3 = text.Substring(num);
						if (double.TryParse(text2, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result))
						{
							text2 = result.ToString("g12", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
						}
						return text2 + text3;
					}
					if (double.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result2))
					{
						text = result2.ToString("g12", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
					}
					return text;
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class NotNullNorEmptyConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null || value == DBNull.Value || value == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (value is string value2)
			{
				return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

		private static RoutedCommand _BrowseCommand = new RoutedCommand("BrowseCommand", typeof(AesControls));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VariableScopeProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("VariableScope", typeof(object), typeof(AesControls), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0)
		{
			Inherits = true
		});

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VariableScopeDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("VariableScopeDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(AesControls), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(string.Empty)
		{
			Inherits = true
		});

		public static RoutedCommand SpinUpCommand => _SpinUpCommand;

		public static RoutedCommand SpinDownCommand => _SpinDownCommand;

		public static RoutedCommand BrowseCommand => _BrowseCommand;

		public static object GetVariableScope(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return obj?.GetValue(VariableScopeProperty);
		}

		public static void SetVariableScope(DependencyObject obj, object value)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("obj", "DependencyObject is null");
			}
			obj.SetValue(VariableScopeProperty, value);
		}

		public static string GetVariableScopeDataMember(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return (string)obj.GetValue(VariableScopeDataMemberProperty);
		}

		public static void SetVariableScopeDataMember(DependencyObject obj, string value)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("obj", "DependencyObject is null");
			}
			obj.SetValue(VariableScopeDataMemberProperty, value);
		}
	}
	public class AesDoubleSlider : AesAutoBindControlBase

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TopVariableProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty BottomVariablePathProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TopPromptProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty BottomPromptProperty;

		public double Minimum
		{
			get

				return (double)GetValue(MinimumProperty);

		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MaximumProperty);

		public object TopValue

		public object TopVariable

		public object BottomVariable

			get

				return GetValue(BottomVariableProperty);

		[Localizability(LocalizationCategory.NeverLocalize)]
		public string TopVariablePath
		{

			}

			}

		[Localizability(LocalizationCategory.NeverLocalize)]
		public string BottomVariablePath
		{

			{

		{

		{

			}
