			}
		}

		static AesDoubleSlider()
		{
			MinimumProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Minimum", typeof(double), typeof(AesDoubleSlider), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0));
			MaximumProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Maximum", typeof(double), typeof(AesDoubleSlider), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(10.0));
			TopValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TopValue", typeof(object), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			BottomValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BottomValue", typeof(object), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			TopVariableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TopVariable", typeof(object), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			BottomVariableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BottomVariable", typeof(object), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			TopVariablePathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TopVariablePath", typeof(string), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			BottomVariablePathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BottomVariablePath", typeof(string), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			TopPromptProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TopPrompt", typeof(string), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			BottomPromptProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BottomPrompt", typeof(string), typeof(AesDoubleSlider));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesDoubleSlider), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesDoubleSlider)));
		}

		public AesDoubleSlider()
		{
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(TopPromptProperty, TopVariableProperty, CreatePromptBinding(this, TopVariableProperty.Name)));
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(BottomPromptProperty, BottomVariableProperty, CreatePromptBinding(this, BottomVariableProperty.Name)));
		}

		internal static BindingBase CreatePromptBinding(UIElement element, string variableProperty)
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding(variableProperty + ".VariablePrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding(variableProperty + ".DisabledPrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsMMEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = element
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesControlPromptConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = string.Empty
			};
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				items.Add(new VariablePathToVariableBindingItem(TopVariablePathProperty, TopVariableProperty));
				items.Add(new VariablePathToVariableBindingItem(BottomVariablePathProperty, BottomVariableProperty));
			}
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(TopVariableProperty, TopValueProperty, "Value"));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(BottomVariableProperty, BottomValueProperty, "Value"));
			}
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Content")]
	public class AesExpander : AesVariableControlBase, IAesControlContainer
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ExpandDirectionProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsExpandedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty EnteredProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CompleteProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AccessibleProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey AccessibleResolvedPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AccessibleResolvedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ExpanderStatusImagePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ExpanderStatusImageProperty;

		public object Header
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(HeaderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(HeaderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object Content
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ExpandDirection ExpandDirection
		{
			get
			{
				return (ExpandDirection)GetValue(ExpandDirectionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ExpandDirectionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsExpanded
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsExpandedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsExpandedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool? Entered
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool?)GetValue(EnteredProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(EnteredProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool? Complete
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool?)GetValue(CompleteProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CompleteProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool? Accessible
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool?)GetValue(AccessibleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AccessibleProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool AccessibleResolved => (bool)GetValue(AccessibleResolvedProperty);

		public ImageSource ExpanderStatusImage
		{
			get
			{
				return (ImageSource)GetValue(ExpanderStatusImageProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(ExpanderStatusImagePropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public string DisabledPrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(Accessible ?? true) && base.Prompt != null)
				{
					return base.Prompt;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		static AesExpander()
		{
			HeaderProperty = HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesExpander));
			ContentProperty = ContentControl.ContentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesExpander));
			ExpandDirectionProperty = Expander.ExpandDirectionProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesExpander));
			IsExpandedProperty = Expander.IsExpandedProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesExpander));
			EnteredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Entered", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesExpander), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, EnteredPropertyChanged));
			CompleteProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Complete", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesExpander), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, CompletePropertyChanged));
			AccessibleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Accessible", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesExpander), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, AccessiblePropertyChanged));
			AccessibleResolvedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("AccessibleResolved", typeof(bool), typeof(AesExpander), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			AccessibleResolvedProperty = AccessibleResolvedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			ExpanderStatusImagePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("ExpanderStatusImage", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(AesExpander), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ExpanderStatusImageProperty = ExpanderStatusImagePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesExpander), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesExpander)));
		}

		private static void EnteredPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is AesExpander aesExpander)
			{
				aesExpander.UpdateExpanderStatusImage();
			}
		}

		private static void CompletePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is AesExpander aesExpander)
			{
				aesExpander.UpdateExpanderStatusImage();
			}
		}

		private static void AccessiblePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is AesExpander aesExpander)
			{
				bool? flag = (bool?)args.NewValue;
				aesExpander.SetValue(AccessibleResolvedPropertyKey, !flag.HasValue || flag.Value);
				aesExpander.UpdateExpanderStatusImage();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateExpanderStatusImage()
		{
			ImageSource expanderStatusImage = null;
			if (AccessibleResolved && base.IsEnabled)
			{
				bool? complete = Complete;
				if (complete.HasValue)
				{
					if (complete.Value)
					{
						bool? entered = Entered;
						if (!entered.HasValue || entered.Value)
						{
							expanderStatusImage = ImageResources.InputComplete;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						expanderStatusImage = ImageResources.InputRequired;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					bool? entered2 = Entered;
					if (entered2.HasValue && entered2.Value)
					{
						expanderStatusImage = ImageResources.InputComplete;
					}
				}
			}
			ExpanderStatusImage = expanderStatusImage;
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
			if (items != null)
			{
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, EnteredProperty, EnteredProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CompleteProperty, CompleteProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AccessibleProperty, AccessibleProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected override BindingBase CreateIsMMEnabledBinding()
		{
			return new Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath("AccessibleResolved"),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = true
			};
		}
	}

	[ContentProperty("Content")]
	[Localizability(LocalizationCategory.Label)]
	public class AesLabel : AesAttachedControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentProperty;

		public object Content
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public override string TextForCopy
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Target != null)
				{
					return base.Target.TextForCopy;
				}
				if (Content != null && Content.GetType() == typeof(string))
				{
					return Content.ToString();
				}
				return base.TextForCopy;
			}
		}

		static AesLabel()
		{
			ContentProperty = ContentControl.ContentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesLabel));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesLabel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesLabel)));
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			items?.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ContentProperty, "Value")
			{
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		protected override void OnCanPaste(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = false;
		}
	}

			}

			{

				if (_selectedOption != null && PromptsDictionary != null && PromptsDictionary.ContainsKey(_selectedOption.ToString()))

					OnPromptDisplayed(PromptsDictionary[_selectedOption.ToString()]);

			}

				if (_selectedSelection != null && PromptsDictionary != null && PromptsDictionary.ContainsKey(_selectedSelection.ToString()))
				{
					OnPromptDisplayed(PromptsDictionary[_selectedSelection.ToString()]);
				}
			}
		}

		static AesListPicker()
		{
			PromptsDictionaryProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PromptsDictionary", typeof(Dictionary<string, string>), typeof(AesListPicker));
			NoRemoveAllProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NoRemoveAll", typeof(bool), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			NoAddAllProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NoAddAll", typeof(bool), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			OnlyAddOneProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("OnlyAddOne", typeof(bool), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnOnlyAddOnePropertyChanged));
			AllowDuplicatesProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowDuplicates", typeof(bool), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, AllowDuplicatesPropertyChanged));
			OptionsHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("OptionsHeader", typeof(string), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(string.Empty));
			SelectionHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectionHeader", typeof(string), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(string.Empty));
			OptionsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Options", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OptionsPropertyChanged));
			SelectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Selection", typeof(IList), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, SelectionPropertyChanged));
			SelectionViewPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("SelectionView", typeof(CollectionView), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			SelectionViewProperty = SelectionViewPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			OptionsViewPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("OptionsView", typeof(CollectionView), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			OptionsViewProperty = OptionsViewPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CanReorderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CanReorder", typeof(bool), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnReorderChanged));
			UserReorderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UserReorder", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnReorderChanged));
			ResolveCanReorderPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("ResolveCanReorder", typeof(bool), typeof(AesListPicker), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			ResolveCanReorderProperty = ResolveCanReorderPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CanDefineProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CanDefine", typeof(bool), typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			_MoveRightCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.MoveRight, "Move Right", typeof(AesListPicker));
			_MoveAllRightCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.MoveAllRight, "Move All Right", typeof(AesListPicker));
			_MoveLeftCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.MoveLeft, "Move Left", typeof(AesListPicker));
			_MoveAllLeftCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.MoveAllLeft, "Move All Left", typeof(AesListPicker));
			_MoveUpCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.MoveUp, "Move Up", typeof(AesListPicker));
			_MoveDownCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.MoveDown, "Move Down", typeof(AesListPicker));
			_DefineNewCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.DefineNew, "Define New", typeof(AesListPicker));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesListPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesListPicker)));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesListPicker), new CommandBinding(MoveLeftCommand, MoveLeftCommandExecuted, MoveLeftCommandCanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesListPicker), new CommandBinding(MoveRightCommand, MoveRightCommandExecuted, MoveRightCommandCanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesListPicker), new CommandBinding(MoveAllLeftCommand, MoveAllLeftCommandExecuted, MoveAllLeftCommandCanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesListPicker), new CommandBinding(MoveAllRightCommand, MoveAllRightCommandExecuted, MoveAllRightCommandCanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesListPicker), new CommandBinding(MoveUpCommand, MoveUpCommandExecuted, MoveUpCommandCanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesListPicker), new CommandBinding(MoveDownCommand, MoveDownCommandExecuted, MoveDownCommandCanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesListPicker), new CommandBinding(DefineNewCommand, DefineNewCommandExecuted, DefineNewCommandCanExecute));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(AesListPicker), Control.MouseDoubleClickEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(ListBox_MouseDoubleClick));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			_optionsListBox = base.Template.FindName("Part_OptionsBox", this) as ListBox;
			_selectionListBox = base.Template.FindName("Part_SelectionBox", this) as ListBox;
			_optionsListBox.IsEnabledChanged += delegate(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (!(bool)e.NewValue)
				{
					_optionsListBox.SelectedItems.Clear();
				}
			};
			_selectionListBox.IsEnabledChanged += delegate(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (!(bool)e.NewValue)
				{
					_selectionListBox.SelectedItems.Clear();
				}
			};
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		protected override void OnCanCopy(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = false;
		}

		private static void AllowDuplicatesPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is AesListPicker aesListPicker)
			{
				aesListPicker.RefreshOptionsView();
			}
		}

		private static void OptionsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is AesListPicker aesListPicker)
			{
				aesListPicker.OnOptionsChanged();
			}
		}

		private void OnOptionsChanged()
		{
			RefreshOptionsView();
		}

		private static void SelectionPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is AesListPicker aesListPicker)
			{
				aesListPicker.OnSelectionChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectionChanged()
		{
			RefreshSelectionView();
			if (!AllowDuplicates)
			{
				RefreshOptionsView();
			}
		}

		private void RefreshSelectionView(bool breakConnection = false)
		{
			CollectionView value = null;
			if (Selection != null)
			{
				value = (breakConnection ? new CollectionView(new ArrayList(Selection)) : new CollectionView(Selection));
			}
			SetValue(SelectionViewPropertyKey, value);
		}

		private void RefreshOptionsView()
		{
			CollectionView collectionView = null;
			IEnumerable options = Options;
			CollectionView selectionView = SelectionView;
			if (options == null)
			{
				collectionView = null;
			}
			else if (AllowDuplicates || selectionView == null)
			{
				collectionView = new CollectionView(options);
			}
			else
			{
				List<object> list = new List<object>();
				foreach (object option in Options)
				{
					if (selectionView.IndexOf(option) < 0)
					{
						list.Add(option);
					}
				}
				collectionView = new CollectionView(list);
			}
			SetValue(OptionsViewPropertyKey, collectionView);
		}

		private static void OnReorderChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is AesListPicker aesListPicker)
			{
				aesListPicker.ResolveCanReorderChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ResolveCanReorderChanged()
		{
			if (UserReorder.HasValue)
			{
				if (UserReorder == true)
				{
					ResolveCanReorder = true;
				}
				else
				{
					ResolveCanReorder = false;
				}
			}
			else if (CanReorder)
			{
				ResolveCanReorder = true;
			}
			else
			{
				ResolveCanReorder = false;
			}
		}

		private static void MoveRightCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			if (aesListPicker._optionsListBox == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<object> list = aesListPicker._optionsListBox.SelectedItems.Cast<object>().ToList();
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int lastSourceIndex = aesListPicker._optionsListBox.Items.IndexOf(list[list.Count - 1]);
			aesListPicker.RefreshSelectionView(breakConnection: true);
			foreach (object item in list)
			{
				aesListPicker.AddOptionIntoSelection(item);
			}
			aesListPicker.OnSelectionChanged();
			aesListPicker.UpdateSelectionStatusInListBoxes(aesListPicker._optionsListBox, aesListPicker._selectionListBox, list, lastSourceIndex);
			aesListPicker._selectionListBox.Focus();
		}

		private static void MoveRightCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			if (aesListPicker.OnlyAddOne)
			{
				args.CanExecute = aesListPicker._optionsListBox.SelectedItems.Count == 1 && aesListPicker._selectionListBox.Items.Count < 1 && aesListPicker.SelectedItemInOptions != null;
			}
			else
			{
				args.CanExecute = aesListPicker.SelectedItemInOptions != null;
			}
		}

		private static void MoveAllRightCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			aesListPicker.ExecuteMoveAllRight();
			if (aesListPicker._selectionListBox != null)
			{
				aesListPicker._selectionListBox.Focus();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ExecuteMoveAllRight()
		{
			List<object> list = _optionsListBox.Items.Cast<object>().ToList();
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)OptionsView)
			{
				AddOptionIntoSelection(item);
			}
			OnSelectionChanged();
			UpdateSelectionStatusInListBoxes(_optionsListBox, _selectionListBox, list, list.Count - 1);
		}

		private static void MoveAllRightCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			args.CanExecute = !aesListPicker.NoAddAll && aesListPicker.OptionsView != null && aesListPicker.OptionsView.Count > 0;
		}

		private static void MoveLeftCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			if (aesListPicker._selectionListBox == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<object> list = aesListPicker._selectionListBox.SelectedItems.Cast<object>().ToList();
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int lastSourceIndex = aesListPicker._selectionListBox.Items.IndexOf(list[list.Count - 1]);
			foreach (object item in list)
			{
				aesListPicker.RemoveOptionFromSelection(item);
			}
			aesListPicker.OnSelectionChanged();
			aesListPicker.UpdateSelectionStatusInListBoxes(aesListPicker._selectionListBox, aesListPicker._optionsListBox, list, lastSourceIndex);
			aesListPicker._optionsListBox.Focus();
		}

		private static void MoveLeftCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			args.CanExecute = aesListPicker.SelectedItemInSelection != null;
		}

		private static void MoveAllLeftCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			aesListPicker.ExecuteMoveAllLeft();
			if (aesListPicker._optionsListBox != null)
			{
				aesListPicker._optionsListBox.Focus();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ExecuteMoveAllLeft()
		{
			List<object> list = _selectionListBox.Items.Cast<object>().ToList();
			Selection.Clear();
			OnSelectionChanged();
			UpdateSelectionStatusInListBoxes(_selectionListBox, _optionsListBox, list, list.Count - 1);
		}

		private static void MoveAllLeftCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			args.CanExecute = !aesListPicker.NoRemoveAll && aesListPicker.Selection != null && aesListPicker.Selection.Count > 0;
		}

		private static void MoveUpCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			(sender as AesListPicker).ExecuteMoveUp();
		}

		protected virtual void ExecuteMoveUp()
		{
			moveExecute(IsMoveUp: true);
		}

		private static void MoveUpCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			if (aesListPicker.SelectedItemInSelection != null && aesListPicker.Selection.Count > 1)
			{
				if (aesListPicker._selectionListBox == null)
				{
					args.CanExecute = true;
				}
				else
				{
					args.CanExecute = aesListPicker.IsSelectedInSelectionCorrect(isMoveUp: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool IsSelectedInSelectionCorrect(bool isMoveUp)
		{
			int count = _selectionListBox.SelectedItems.Count;
			IEnumerable<object> source = _selectionListBox.Items.Cast<object>();
			object obj = source.FirstOrDefault((object dr) => _selectionListBox.SelectedItems.Contains(dr));
			object obj2 = source.LastOrDefault((object dr) => _selectionListBox.SelectedItems.Contains(dr));
			if (obj == null || obj2 == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = _selectionListBox.Items.IndexOf(obj);
			int num2 = _selectionListBox.Items.IndexOf(obj2);
			if (isMoveUp && num == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!isMoveUp && num + count == _selectionListBox.Items.Count)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (count != 0)
			{
				return num + count - 1 == num2;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void moveExecute(bool IsMoveUp)
		{
			List<object> list = null;
			if (_selectionListBox != null)
			{
				list = _selectionListBox.SelectedItems.Cast<object>().ToList();
			}
			List<int> list2 = new List<int>();
			foreach (object item in list)
			{
				list2.Add(_selectionListBox.Items.IndexOf(item));
			}
			int num = list2.Min();
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<object> list3 = _selectionListBox.Items.Cast<object>().ToList();
			IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<int, object>> indexedItems = list3.Select((object item, int index) => new KeyValuePair<int, object>(index, item));
			list = list.OrderBy((object t) => indexedItems.Single((KeyValuePair<int, object> keyValuePair) => keyValuePair.Value == t).Key).ToList();
			list3.RemoveRange(num, list.Count);
			int num2 = ((!IsMoveUp) ? 1 : (-1));
			list3.InsertRange(num + num2, list);
			Selection.Clear();
			foreach (object item2 in list3)
			{
				Selection.Add(item2);
			}
			OnSelectionChanged();
			if (_selectionListBox != null && list != null)
			{
				foreach (object item3 in list)
				{
					_selectionListBox.SelectedItems.Add(item3);
				}
			}
			_selectionListBox.ScrollIntoView(IsMoveUp ? list.FirstOrDefault() : list.LastOrDefault());
		}

		private static void MoveDownCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			(sender as AesListPicker).ExecuteMoveDown();
		}

		protected virtual void ExecuteMoveDown()
		{
			moveExecute(IsMoveUp: false);
		}

		private static void MoveDownCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			if (aesListPicker.SelectedItemInSelection != null && aesListPicker.Selection.Count > 1)
			{
				if (aesListPicker._selectionListBox == null)
				{
					args.CanExecute = true;
				}
				else
				{
					args.CanExecute = aesListPicker.IsSelectedInSelectionCorrect(isMoveUp: false);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void DefineNewCommandExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			(sender as AesListPicker).ExecuteDefineNew();
		}

		public virtual void ExecuteDefineNew()
		{
		}

		private static void DefineNewCommandCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			args.CanExecute = aesListPicker.CanDefine;
		}

		protected override void BuildDefaultContextMenu(ContextMenu menu)
		{
			base.BuildDefaultContextMenu(menu);
			if (menu.Items.Count > 0)
			{
				menu.Items.Add(new Separator());
			}
			string text = "pack://application:,,,/" + typeof(AesControlBase).Assembly.FullName + ";component/Images/";
			Image image = new Image();
			image.Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "New16.ico"));
			menu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
			{
				Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderNew,
				Icon = image,
				Command = DefineNewCommand,
				CommandTarget = this
			});
		}

		protected virtual void AddOptionIntoSelection(object option)
		{
			Selection.Add(option);
		}

		protected virtual void RemoveOptionFromSelection(object option)
		{
			Selection.Remove(option);
		}

		private void UpdateSelectionStatusInListBoxes(ListBox sourceBox, ListBox targetBox, IList<object> movingOptions, int lastSourceIndex)
		{
			List<object> list = movingOptions.Where((object x) => sourceBox.Items.Contains(x)).ToList();
			sourceBox.SelectedItems.Clear();
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (object item in list)
				{
					sourceBox.SelectedItems.Add(item);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int num = lastSourceIndex + 1 - movingOptions.Count;
				if (sourceBox.Items.Count > num && num >= 0)
				{
					sourceBox.SelectedIndex = num;
				}
				else
				{
					sourceBox.SelectedIndex = sourceBox.Items.Count - 1;
				}
			}
			List<object> list2 = movingOptions.Where((object x) => targetBox.Items.Contains(x)).ToList();
			if (list2.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			targetBox.SelectedItems.Clear();
			foreach (object item2 in list2)
			{
				targetBox.SelectedItems.Add(item2);
			}
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, OptionsProperty, OptionsProperty.Name));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, SelectionProperty, "Value"));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CanReorderProperty, CanReorderProperty.Name));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CanDefineProperty, CanDefineProperty.Name));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, PromptsDictionaryProperty, "OptionPrompts")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		private static void OnOnlyAddOnePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = d as AesListPicker;
			if ((bool)e.NewValue && aesListPicker._selectionListBox.Items.Cast<object>().ToList().Count > 0)
			{
				aesListPicker.ExecuteMoveAllLeft();
			}
		}

		private static void ListBox_MouseDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			AesListPicker aesListPicker = sender as AesListPicker;
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject;
			if (aesListPicker._optionsListBox == null || aesListPicker._selectionListBox == null || dependencyObject == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = dependencyObject;
			ListBoxItem listBoxItem = dependencyObject2 as ListBoxItem;
			while (listBoxItem == null && dependencyObject2 != null)
			{
				dependencyObject2 = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject2);
				listBoxItem = dependencyObject2 as ListBoxItem;
			}
			if (listBoxItem != null)
			{
				if (aesListPicker._optionsListBox.IsAncestorOf(listBoxItem))
				{
					MoveRightCommand.Execute(null, aesListPicker);
				}
				else if (aesListPicker._selectionListBox.IsAncestorOf(listBoxItem))
				{
					MoveLeftCommand.Execute(null, aesListPicker);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCanPaste(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = false;
		}
	}
	public enum AesMaskOption
	{
		None,
		Integer,
		Double,
		Id,
		FortranVariable,
		Subroutine,
		FortranExpression,
		ComponentId,
		Percentage,
		ComponentName,
		AsciiChar,
		Description
	}
	public class AesMessageBox : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private Dictionary<AesMessageBoxButton, string[]> Button_Captions = new Dictionary<AesMessageBoxButton, string[]>();

		private List<Button> btns = new List<Button>();

		private readonly int _maxBtnCnt = 6;

		private static readonly Assembly _ass;

		private AesMessageBoxButton _button;

		private AesMessageBoxImage _icon;

		private int _defaultButton = 1;

		private bool _showHelpButton;

		protected AesMessageBoxResult _result;

		internal Image HeaderImage;

		internal TextBlock TxtBlock;

		internal Grid buttonsPanel;

		internal Button btn0;

		internal Button btn1;

		internal Button btn2;

		internal Button btn3;

		internal Button btn4;

		internal Button btn5;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public string HelpFile { get; private set; }

		public int HelpId { get; private set; }

		public string MessageText
		{
			get
			{
				return TxtBlock.Text;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					value.Replace("\\n", "\n");
				}
				TxtBlock.Text = value;
			}
		}

		public AesMessageBoxButton Button
		{
			get
			{
				return _button;
			}
			set
			{
				_button = value;
				string[] array = Button_Captions[Button];
				if (array.Length < 1)
				{
					return;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < btns.Count; i++)
				{
					if (i >= array.Length)
					{
						btns[i].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
						continue;
					}
					btns[i].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					btns[i].Content = array[i];
				}
				if (_showHelpButton)
				{
					btns[array.Length].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					btns[array.Length].Content = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Help;
				}
			}
		}

		public AesMessageBoxImage MessageIcon
		{
			get
			{
				return _icon;
			}
			set
			{
				_icon = value;
				if (_icon == AesMessageBoxImage.None)
				{
					HeaderImage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					return;
				}
				HeaderImage.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				ImageSource imageSource = null;
				stringBuilder.Append("pack://application:,,,/");
				stringBuilder.Append(typeof(AesMessageBox).Assembly.FullName);
				stringBuilder.Append(";component/Images/");
				switch (_icon)
				{
				case AesMessageBoxImage.Error:
					stringBuilder.Append("Stop.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				case AesMessageBoxImage.Exclamation:
					stringBuilder.Append("Exclamation.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				case AesMessageBoxImage.Information:
					stringBuilder.Append("Asterisk.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				case AesMessageBoxImage.Question:
					stringBuilder.Append("Question.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				}
				if (imageSource != null)
				{
					HeaderImage.Source = imageSource;
				}
			}
		}

		protected int DefaultButton
		{
			get
			{
				return _defaultButton;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value > 0 && value <= _maxBtnCnt && btns[value - 1].Content != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(btns[value - 1].Content.ToString()))
				{
					btns[value - 1].IsDefault = true;
					btns[value - 1].Focus();
					_defaultButton = value;
				}
				else
				{
					btns[0].IsDefault = true;
					btns[0].Focus();
					_defaultButton = 1;
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool ShowHelpButton
		{
			get
			{
				return _showHelpButton;
			}
			set
			{
				_showHelpButton = value;
				if (_showHelpButton)
				{
					string[] array = Button_Captions[Button];
					if (array.Length >= 1)
					{
						btns[array.Length].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
						btns[array.Length].Content = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Help;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, ExactSpelling = true)]
		private static extern IntPtr GetActiveWindow();

		[DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, ExactSpelling = true)]
		private static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();

		static AesMessageBox()
		{
			try
			{
				_ass = Assembly.LoadFile(Path.GetDirectoryName(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().Location) + "\\AspenTech.AspenPlus.MMControls.dll");
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		protected AesMessageBox()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.KeyDown += AesMessageBox_KeyDown;
			Button_Captions.Clear();
			btns.Clear();
			Button_Captions.Add(AesMessageBoxButton.OK, new string[1] { AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Ok });
			Button_Captions.Add(AesMessageBoxButton.OKCancel, new string[2]
			{
				AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Ok,
				AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Cancel
			});
			Button_Captions.Add(AesMessageBoxButton.YesNoCancel, new string[3]
			{

		private void btn_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is Button { Content: not null } button)
			{
				if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Ok))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.OK;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Cancel))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.Cancel;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Yes))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.Yes;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._No))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.No;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Abort))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.Abort;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Retry))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.Retry;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Ignore))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.Ignore;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._YesAll))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.YesAll;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._NoAll))
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.NoAll;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Help))
				{
					ApplicationCommands.Help.Execute(this, button);
				}
				if (!button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources._Help))
				{
					Close();
				}
			}
		}

		private void AesMessageBox_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				if (Button == AesMessageBoxButton.OK)
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.OK;
				}
				else
				{
					_result = AesMessageBoxResult.Cancel;
				}
				Close();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClosed(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (_result == AesMessageBoxResult.None)
			{
				_result = AesMessageBoxResult.Cancel;
			}
			base.OnClosed(e);
		}

		private static AesMessageBoxResult InitializeAesMessageBox(Window owner, string messageBoxText, string caption, AesMessageBoxButton button, AesMessageBoxImage icon, bool showHelpButton, int defaultButton, string helpFile, int helpId)
		{
			AesMessageBox aesMessageBox = new AesMessageBox
			{
				MessageText = messageBoxText,
				Title = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(caption) ? "" : caption),
				Button = button,
				MessageIcon = icon,
				DefaultButton = defaultButton,
				ShowHelpButton = showHelpButton,
				HelpFile = helpFile,
				HelpId = helpId
			};
			if (owner != null)
			{
				aesMessageBox.Owner = owner;
			}
			else
			{
				WindowInteropHelper windowInteropHelper = new WindowInteropHelper(aesMessageBox);
				windowInteropHelper.Owner = GetActiveWindow();
				if (windowInteropHelper.Owner == IntPtr.Zero)
				{
					windowInteropHelper.Owner = GetForegroundWindow();
				}
			}
			aesMessageBox.ShowDialog();
			return aesMessageBox?._result ?? AesMessageBoxResult.OK;
		}

		public static AesMessageBoxResult Show(Window owner, string messageBoxText, string caption = "", AesMessageBoxButton button = AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage icon = AesMessageBoxImage.None, bool showHelpButton = false, int defaultButton = 1, string helpFile = "apwn.chm", int helpId = -1)
		{
			AesMessageBoxResult result = AesMessageBoxResult.OK;
			FieldInfo fieldInfo = null;
			FieldInfo fieldInfo2 = null;
			if (_ass != null)
			{
				Type type = _ass.GetType("AspenTech.AspenPlus.MMControls.Scripting.ScriptingSupport");
				fieldInfo2 = type.GetField("ScriptRunning", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
				fieldInfo = type.GetField("Enabled", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
			}
			if (fieldInfo != null && fieldInfo2 != null && (bool)fieldInfo.GetValue(null) && (bool)fieldInfo2.GetValue(null))
			{
				switch (button)
				{
				case AesMessageBoxButton.OK:
				case AesMessageBoxButton.OKCancel:
					result = AesMessageBoxResult.OK;
					break;
				case AesMessageBoxButton.YesAllNoAll:
				case AesMessageBoxButton.YesAllNo:
					result = AesMessageBoxResult.YesAll;
					break;
				case AesMessageBoxButton.YesNoCancel:
				case AesMessageBoxButton.YesNo:
				case AesMessageBoxButton.YesNoAll:
					result = AesMessageBoxResult.Yes;
					break;
				}
			}
			else if (Application.Current.Dispatcher != Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher)
			{
				Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(delegate
				{
					result = InitializeAesMessageBox(owner, messageBoxText, caption, button, icon, showHelpButton, defaultButton, helpFile, helpId);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				result = InitializeAesMessageBox(owner, messageBoxText, caption, button, icon, showHelpButton, defaultButton, helpFile, helpId);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static AesMessageBoxResult Show(string messageBoxText, string caption = "", AesMessageBoxButton button = AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage icon = AesMessageBoxImage.None, bool showHelpButton = false, int defaultResult = 1, string helpFile = "apwn.chm", int helpId = -1)
		{
			return Show(null, messageBoxText, caption, button, icon, showHelpButton, defaultResult, helpFile, helpId);
		}

	public class AesOptionGroup : AesVariableControlBase

		public event RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<object> OptionValueChanged
		{

			{
				AddHandler(OptionValueChangedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(OptionValueChangedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static AesOptionGroup()
		{
			HeaderProperty = HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesOptionGroup));
			ContentProperty = ContentControl.ContentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesOptionGroup));
			OptionValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("OptionValue", typeof(object), typeof(AesOptionGroup), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnOptionValuePropertyChanged));
			OptionValueChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("OptionValueChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<object>), typeof(AesOptionGroup));
			PromptsDictionaryProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PromptsDictionary", typeof(Dictionary<string, string>), typeof(AesOptionGroup));
			IsBorderVisibleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsBorderVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(AesOptionGroup), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesOptionGroup), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesOptionGroup)));
			KeyboardNavigation.TabNavigationProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesOptionGroup), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KeyboardNavigationMode.Local));
			KeyboardNavigation.DirectionalNavigationProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesOptionGroup), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(KeyboardNavigationMode.Cycle));
		}

		public AesOptionGroup()
		{
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Remove(base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.First((ExtraAutoBindingItem x) => x.TargetProperty == AesControlBase.PromptProperty));
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(AesControlBase.PromptProperty, AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CreateOptionGroupPromptBinding(this)));
		}

		internal static BindingBase CreateOptionGroupPromptBinding(UIElement element)
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.VariablePrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.DisabledPrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsMMEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.SelectedItem")
					{
						Source = element
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesOptionGroupPromptConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = string.Empty
			};
		}

		public void UpdateOptionValue(object newOptionValue)
		{
			object oldOptionValue = OptionValue;
			if (BindingOperations.GetBinding(this, OptionValueProperty) != null)
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding
				{
					Source = base.Variable,
					Path = new PropertyPath("Value"),
					Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
				};
				_bindingUsageControl.SetBinding(ContentControl.ContentProperty, binding);
				_bindingUsageControl.Content = newOptionValue;
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(_bindingUsageControl, ContentControl.ContentProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				OptionValue = newOptionValue;
			}
			if (OptionValue == newOptionValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (OptionValue != null && newOptionValue != null && OptionValue.GetType() == typeof(string) && newOptionValue.GetType() == typeof(string) && string.Equals(OptionValue.ToString(), newOptionValue.ToString()))
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					OnOptionValueChanged(new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<object>(oldOptionValue, newOptionValue, OptionValueChangedEvent));
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnOptionValueChanged(RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<object> e)
		{
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, OptionValueProperty, "Value"));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, PromptsDictionaryProperty, "OptionPrompts")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		private static void OnOptionValuePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs arg)
		{
			(d as AesOptionGroup).OnOptionValueChanged(new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<object>(arg.OldValue, arg.NewValue, OptionValueChangedEvent));
		}

		protected override void OnCanPaste(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = false;
		}
	}

	public class AesTextBoxWithStatusBar : AesTextEdit

			ColNumberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ColNumber", typeof(string), typeof(AesTextBoxWithStatusBar), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("1"));

			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesTextBoxWithStatusBar), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesTextBoxWithStatusBar)));
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
			items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, DefVarListProperty, "ValueOptions"));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			TextBox textBox = GetTemplateChild("PART_TextBoxControl") as TextBox;
			if (textBox != _richTextBox)
			{
				if (_richTextBox != null)
				{
					_richTextBox.TextChanged -= RichTextBox_TextChanged;
					_richTextBox.KeyUp -= RichTextBox_KeyUp;
					_richTextBox.KeyDown -= RichTextBox_KeyDown;
					_richTextBox.MouseDown -= richTextBox_MouseDown;
					_richTextBox.MouseLeftButtonDown -= richTextBox_MouseLeftButtonDown;
					_richTextBox.MouseLeftButtonUp -= richTextBox_MouseLeftButtonUp;
					_richTextBox.PreviewMouseDown -= richTextBox_MouseDown;
					_richTextBox.SelectionChanged -= RichTextBox_SelectionChanged;
					_richTextBox.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown -= RichTextBox_MouseRightButtonDown;
					_richTextBox.PreviewKeyDown -= RichTextBox_KeyDown;
				}
				_richTextBox = textBox;
				_richTextBox.TextChanged += RichTextBox_TextChanged;
				_richTextBox.KeyUp += RichTextBox_KeyUp;
				_richTextBox.KeyDown += RichTextBox_KeyDown;
				_richTextBox.MouseDown += richTextBox_MouseDown;
				_richTextBox.MouseLeftButtonDown += richTextBox_MouseLeftButtonDown;
				_richTextBox.MouseLeftButtonUp += richTextBox_MouseLeftButtonUp;
				_richTextBox.GotKeyboardFocus += richTextBox_GotKeyboardFocus;
				_richTextBox.LostKeyboardFocus += richTextBox_LostKeyboardFocus;
				_richTextBox.PreviewMouseDown += richTextBox_MouseDown;
				_richTextBox.SelectionChanged += RichTextBox_SelectionChanged;
				_richTextBox.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown += RichTextBox_MouseRightButtonDown;
				_richTextBox.PreviewKeyDown += RichTextBox_KeyDown;
			}
		}

		private void richTextBox_LostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_richTextBox.GetBindingExpression(TextBox.TextProperty)?.UpdateSource();
		}

		private void richTextBox_GotKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{

		}

			UpdateColRow();

			UpdateColRow();
		}

		{
			UpdateColRow();
		}

		private void RichTextBox_MouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			RichTextBox_DefVarsMenu();
		}

		protected override void BuildDefaultContextMenu(ContextMenu menu)
		{
			base.BuildDefaultContextMenu(menu);
			if (menu == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)menu.Items)
			{
				if (item is MenuItem)
				{
					MenuItem menuItem = item as MenuItem;
					if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Undo)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = _richTextBox;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Copy)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = _richTextBox;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Paste)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = _richTextBox;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.Cut)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = _richTextBox;
					}
					else if (menuItem.Command == ApplicationCommands.SelectAll)
					{
						menuItem.CommandTarget = _richTextBox;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void MenuItem_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			string sInsertText = ((MenuItem)e.OriginalSource).Header.ToString();
			InsertText(sInsertText);
		}

		private void RichTextBox_DefVarsMenu()
		{
			if (_richTextBox.ContextMenu != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ContextMenu contextMenu = new ContextMenu();
			string text = "pack://application:,,,/" + typeof(AesTextBoxWithStatusBar).Assembly.FullName + ";component/Images/";
			MenuItem menuItem = new MenuItem
			{
				Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderCopy,
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Copy,
				CommandTarget = _richTextBox
			};
			Image icon = new Image
			{
				Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Copy16.ico"))
			};
			menuItem.Icon = icon;
			contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem);
			MenuItem menuItem2 = new MenuItem
			{
				Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderCut,
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Cut,
				CommandTarget = _richTextBox
			};
			Image icon2 = new Image
			{
				Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Cut16.ico"))
			};
			menuItem2.Icon = icon2;
			contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem2);
			MenuItem menuItem3 = new MenuItem
			{
				Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderPaste,
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Paste,
				CommandTarget = _richTextBox
			};
			Image icon3 = new Image
			{
				Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Paste16.ico"))
			};
			menuItem3.Icon = icon3;
			contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem3);
			if (DefVarList != null)
			{
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				foreach (string defVar in DefVarList)
				{
					MenuItem menuItem4 = new MenuItem
					{
						Header = defVar
					};
					menuItem4.Click += MenuItem_Click;
					contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem4);
				}
			}
			_richTextBox.ContextMenu = contextMenu;
		}

		private void InsertText(string sInsertText)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sInsertText) && _richTextBox != null)
			{
				_richTextBox.SelectedText = sInsertText;
				Keyboard.Focus(_richTextBox);
			}
		}

		private int LineNumberCalc()
		{
			if (_richTextBox == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return _richTextBox.GetLineIndexFromCharacterIndex(_richTextBox.SelectionStart) + 1;
		}

		private int ColumnNumberCalc()
		{
			if (_richTextBox == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			int selectionStart = _richTextBox.SelectionStart;
			int lineIndexFromCharacterIndex = _richTextBox.GetLineIndexFromCharacterIndex(selectionStart);
			int num = ((lineIndexFromCharacterIndex >= 0) ? _richTextBox.GetCharacterIndexFromLineIndex(lineIndexFromCharacterIndex) : 0);
			return selectionStart - num + 1;
		}

		public void UpdateColRow()
		{
			if (!HideStatusBar)
			{
				ColNumber = ColumnNumberCalc().ToString();
				RowNumber = LineNumberCalc().ToString();
			}
		}

		private void SetEditorValue(object value, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.Template.FindName("PART_TextBoxControl", this) is TextBox textBox)
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					textBox.Text = null;
				}
				else
				{
					textBox.Text = value.ToString();
				}
				BindingExpression bindingExpression = BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(textBox, AesTextEdit.TextProperty);
				if (bindingExpression != null)
				{
					bindingExpression.UpdateSource();
					BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(this, AesTextEdit.TextProperty)?.UpdateSource();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetEditorValue(null, e);
		}

		protected override void OnPaste(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			object value = null;
			if (base.Template.FindName("PART_TextBoxControl", this) is TextBox { SelectionStart: var selectionStart } textBox)
			{
				string text = ClipboardWrapper.GetText();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(textBox.Text))
				{
					value = text;
				}
				else if (textBox.SelectionLength == 0)
				{
					value = textBox.Text.Insert(textBox.SelectionStart, text);
				}
				else if (textBox.SelectionLength > 0)
				{
					value = textBox.Text.Remove(selectionStart, textBox.SelectionLength).Insert(selectionStart, text);
				}
				SetEditorValue(value, e);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					textBox.SelectionStart = selectionStart + text.Length;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class AesSlider : AesValueControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinimumProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaximumProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TickPlacementProperty;

		private double result;

		public double Minimum
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MinimumProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MinimumProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double Maximum
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MaximumProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaximumProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public TickPlacement TickPlacement
		{
			get
			{
				return (TickPlacement)GetValue(TickPlacementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TickPlacementProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static AesSlider()
		{
			MinimumProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Minimum", typeof(double), typeof(AesSlider));
			MaximumProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Maximum", typeof(double), typeof(AesSlider));
			TickPlacementProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TickPlacement", typeof(TickPlacement), typeof(AesSlider), new UIPropertyMetadata(TickPlacement.BottomRight));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesSlider), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesSlider)));
		}

		public AesSlider()
		{
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Remove(base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.First((ExtraAutoBindingItem x) => x.TargetProperty == AesControlBase.PromptProperty));
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(AesControlBase.PromptProperty, AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CreatePromptBinding(this)));
		}

		internal new static BindingBase CreatePromptBinding(UIElement element)
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.VariablePrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.DisabledPrompt")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsMMEnabled")
					{
						Source = element
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = element
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesControlPromptConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = string.Empty
			};
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Value = null;
		}

		protected override void OnCanClear(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsMMEnabled || base.IsReadOnly || base.Variable == null)
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCanPaste(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsEnabled && base.IsMMEnabled && !base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				string text = ClipboardWrapper.GetData(DataFormats.Text) as string;
				e.CanExecute = text != null && double.TryParse(text, out result);
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPaste(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Value = result;
		}
	}
	public class AesSpinBox : AesValueControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty LowerLimitProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SpinStepSizeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaxLengthProperty;

		public double? LowerLimit
		{

			}
			set

				SetValue(LowerLimitProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double? UpperLimit
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(UpperLimitProperty);
			}
			set

				SetValue(UpperLimitProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public int SpinStepSize
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(SpinStepSizeProperty);
			}
			set

				SetValue(SpinStepSizeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public int MaxLength
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(MaxLengthProperty);
			}

			}

		{

			}

			}
		}

		static AesSpinBox()
		{
			LowerLimitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LowerLimit", typeof(double?), typeof(AesSpinBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			UpperLimitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UpperLimit", typeof(double?), typeof(AesSpinBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			SpinStepSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SpinStepSize", typeof(int), typeof(AesSpinBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1));
			MaxLengthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxLength", typeof(int), typeof(AesSpinBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0));
			IsInScrollAreaProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollArea", typeof(bool), typeof(AesSpinBox), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesSpinBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesSpinBox)));
			FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesSpinBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(21.0));
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesValueControlBase.ValueProperty, AesValueControlBase.ValueProperty.Name)
				{
					Trigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.LostFocus
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, UpperLimitProperty, UpperLimitProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, LowerLimitProperty, LowerLimitProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCanClear(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsMMEnabled || base.IsReadOnly || !base.IsEntered || base.Variable == null)
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		private void SetEditorValue(object value, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.Template.FindName("PART_editControl", this) is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				aesXamValueEdit.Value = value;
				bool isInEditMode = aesXamValueEdit.IsInEditMode;
				if (!isInEditMode)
				{
					aesXamValueEdit.StartEditMode();
				}
				else
				{
					aesXamValueEdit.Focus();
				}
				if (e != null)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (!isInEditMode)
				{
					base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<bool, bool>(aesXamValueEdit.EndEditMode), true, false);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetEditorValue(null, e);
		}

		protected override void OnPaste(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetEditorValue(ClipboardWrapper.GetText(), e);
		}
	}

			TextAlignmentProperty = TextBox.TextAlignmentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesTextEdit));
			TextWrappingProperty = TextBox.TextWrappingProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesTextEdit));

		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{

			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, TextProperty, string.IsNullOrEmpty(VariableDataMember) ? "Value" : VariableDataMember)
				{
					Trigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetEditorValue(null, e);
		}

		private void SetEditorValue(object value, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.Template.FindName("PART_editControl", this) is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				aesXamValueEdit.Value = value;
				bool isInEditMode = aesXamValueEdit.IsInEditMode;
				if (!isInEditMode)
				{
					aesXamValueEdit.StartEditMode();
				}
				else
				{
					aesXamValueEdit.Focus();
				}
				if (e != null)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (!isInEditMode)
				{
					base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<bool, bool>(aesXamValueEdit.EndEditMode), true, false);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPaste(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetEditorValue(ClipboardWrapper.GetText(), e);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetSelectedValue("SelectionLength", _selectionLength);
			SetSelectedValue("SelectionStart", _selectionStart);
			if (_selectedText != null)
			{
				SetSelectedValue("SelectedText", _selectedText);
			}
			if (Editor != null && Editor.IsInEditMode)
			{
				Editor.EndEditMode(acceptChanges: true, force: false);
			}
		}

		protected override void BuildDefaultContextMenu(ContextMenu menu)
		{
			base.BuildDefaultContextMenu(menu);
			CorrectCommandTarget(menu, Editor);
		}

		private object GetSelectedValue(string key)
		{
			if (Editor == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (Utilities.GetDescendantFromName(Editor, "PART_XamMaskedEditor") is XamMaskedEditor { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } xamMaskedEditor)
			{
				if (string.Compare(key, "SelectedText", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return xamMaskedEditor.SelectedText;
				}
				if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionStart", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart;
				}
				if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionLength", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return xamMaskedEditor.SelectionLength;
				}
			}
			else if (Utilities.GetDescendantFromName(Editor, "PART_TextBox") is TextBox { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } textBox)
			{
				if (string.Compare(key, "SelectedText", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return textBox.SelectedText;
				}
				if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionStart", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return textBox.SelectionStart;
				}
				if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionLength", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return textBox.SelectionLength;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void SetSelectedValue(string key, object value)
		{
			if (Editor == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!Editor.IsInEditMode)
			{
				Editor.StartEditMode();
			}
			if (Utilities.GetDescendantFromName(Editor, "PART_XamMaskedEditor") is XamMaskedEditor { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } xamMaskedEditor)
			{
				if (!xamMaskedEditor.IsInEditMode)
				{
					xamMaskedEditor.StartEditMode();
				}
				if (xamMaskedEditor.Text != null)
				{
					if (string.Compare(key, "SelectedText", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						xamMaskedEditor.SelectedText = (string)value;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionStart", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart = (int)value;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionLength", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						xamMaskedEditor.SelectionLength = (int)value;
					}
				}
				if (!xamMaskedEditor.IsFocusWithin)
				{
					xamMaskedEditor.Focus();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(Utilities.GetDescendantFromName(Editor, "PART_TextBox") is TextBox { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } textBox))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (!textBox.IsFocused)
				{
					textBox.Focus();
				}
				if (textBox.Text != null)
				{
					if (string.Compare(key, "SelectedText", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						textBox.SelectedText = (string)value;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionStart", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						textBox.SelectionStart = (int)value;
					}
					else if (string.Compare(key, "SelectionLength", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						textBox.SelectionLength = (int)value;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public class AesUnitsLabel : AesVariableControlBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty UnitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Unit", typeof(string), typeof(AesUnitsLabel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnUnitPropertyChanged));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ResolvedUnitToDisplayPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("ResolvedUnitToDisplay", typeof(string), typeof(AesUnitsLabel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ResolvedUnitToDisplayProperty = ResolvedUnitToDisplayPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty UnitRowProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UnitRow", typeof(int), typeof(AesUnitsLabel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnUnitRowPropertyChanged));

		public string Unit
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(UnitProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(UnitProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string ResolvedUnitToDisplay
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(ResolvedUnitToDisplayProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(ResolvedUnitToDisplayProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public int UnitRow
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(UnitRowProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(UnitRowProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected void RefreshResolvedUnitToDisplay()
		{
			ResolvedUnitToDisplay = CalculateResolvedUnitToDisplay();
		}

		protected virtual string CalculateResolvedUnitToDisplay()
		{
			return Unit;
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, UnitProperty, "Units"));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, UnitRowProperty, "UnitRow"));
			}
			base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
		}

		private static void OnUnitPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesUnitsLabel).RefreshResolvedUnitToDisplay();
		}

		private static void OnUnitRowPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesUnitsLabel).RefreshResolvedUnitToDisplay();
		}
	}
	public class AesValueEdit : AesValueControlBase

		static AesValueEdit()
		{
			FormatProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Format", typeof(string), typeof(AesValueEdit));
			ValueTypeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueType", typeof(Type), typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(string)));
			MaxLengthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxLength", typeof(int), typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0));
			DisplayedCaseProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayedCase", typeof(AesDisplayedCase), typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesDisplayedCase.None));
			MaskProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Mask", typeof(AesMaskOption), typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesMaskOption.None));
			IsInScrollAreaProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollArea", typeof(bool), typeof(AesValueEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			IsInScrollAreaColumnHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollAreaColumnHeader", typeof(bool), typeof(AesValueEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			CharacterCasingProperty = TextBox.CharacterCasingProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(TextBox.CharacterCasingProperty.DefaultMetadata.DefaultValue));
			LowerLimitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LowerLimit", typeof(double), typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.MinValue));
			UpperLimitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UpperLimit", typeof(double), typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.MaxValue));
			IsInnerValidationEnableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInnerValidationEnable", typeof(bool), typeof(AesValueEdit), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesValueEdit)));
			FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(21.0));
			Control.IsTabStopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			AesVariableControlBase.ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public AesValueEdit()
		{
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(FormatProperty, AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CreateFormatBinding()));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			editor = base.Template.FindName("PART_editControl", this) as AesXamValueEdit;
		}

		protected override void BuildDefaultContextMenu(ContextMenu menu)
		{
			base.BuildDefaultContextMenu(menu);
			menu.Opened += delegate
			{
				if (editor == null)
				{
					CorrectCommandTarget(menu, this);
				}
				else
				{
					CorrectCommandTarget(menu, editor);
				}
			};
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				TextOptions.SetTextFormattingMode(this, TextFormattingMode.Ideal);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ValueTypeProperty, ValueTypeProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesValueControlBase.ValueProperty, AesValueControlBase.ValueProperty.Name)
				{
					Trigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.LostFocus
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual BindingBase CreateFormatBinding()
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.ValueType")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.Dimension")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Mask")
					{
						Source = this
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesControlFormatConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = null
			};
		}

		protected override void OnCanClear(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (editor != null && editor.IsInEditMode)
			{
				flag = editor.ValuedChangedInEdit;
			}
			if (!base.IsMMEnabled || base.IsReadOnly || base.Variable == null)
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else if (flag)
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
			else if (!base.IsEntered)
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		private void SetEditorValue(object value, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (editor == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool isInEditMode = editor.IsInEditMode;
			if (!isInEditMode)
			{
				editor.StartEditMode();
			}
			else
			{
				editor.Focus();
			}
			if (IsValidValue(value))
			{
				editor.Value = value;
			}
			if (editor.IsValueValid)
			{
				if (e != null)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (!isInEditMode)
				{
					base.Dispatcher.Invoke(new Action<bool, bool>(editor.EndEditMode), true, false);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool IsValidValue(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (editor.Mask == AesMaskOption.Id)
			{
				return Regex.IsMatch(value.ToString(), "[a-z0-9A-Z][a-z0-9A-Z$+-:,]+");
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetEditorValue(null, e);
		}

		protected override void OnPaste(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				SetEditorValue(ClipboardWrapper.GetText().Replace("\\r", string.Empty).Replace(Environment.NewLine, string.Empty)
					.Trim(), e);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCanCopy(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnCanCopy(e);
		}
	}
