			BasisWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BasisWidth", typeof(GridLength), typeof(AesVariableEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Auto)));
			BasisOptionsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BasisOptions", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(AesVariableEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(AesVariableEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Orientation.Horizontal));
			MaxLengthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxLength", typeof(int), typeof(AesVariableEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0));
			FormatProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Format", typeof(string), typeof(AesVariableEdit));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesVariableEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesVariableEdit)));
			AesVariableControlBase.ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesVariableEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public AesVariableEdit()
		{
			base.AutoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(FormatProperty, AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, CreateFormatBinding()));
		}

		protected override void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				base.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
				TextOptions.SetTextFormattingMode(this, TextFormattingMode.Ideal);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, ValueTypeProperty, ValueTypeProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesValueControlBase.ValueProperty, AesValueControlBase.ValueProperty.Name)
				{
					Trigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.LostFocus
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, UnitsProperty, UnitsProperty.Name));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, UnitsOptionsProperty, UnitsOptionsProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, BasisProperty, BasisProperty.Name));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, BasisOptionsProperty, BasisOptionsProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty, AesControlBase.IsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty, AesControlBase.IsCalculatedProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(AesVariableControlBase.VariableProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty, AesControlBase.IsEnteredProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual BindingBase CreateFormatBinding()
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.ValueType")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.Dimension")
					{
						Source = this
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesControlFormatConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = null
			};
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.Template.FindName("textBox", this) is AesValueEdit aesValueEdit)
			{
				aesValueEdit.ClearValue();
			}
		}
	}

		public DataTemplate VisualContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(VisualContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VisualContentTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public TextBox VisiableTextBox
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.IsInEditMode)
				{
					if (editTextBox != null)
					{
						editTextBox.TextChanged -= editTextBox_TextChanged;
						editTextBox.Loaded -= editTextBox_Loaded;
					}
					editTextBox = Utilities.GetDescendantFromType(this, typeof(TextBox), allowSubclassOfType: true) as TextBox;
					if (editTextBox != null && base.IsEditable && (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage))
					{
						editTextBox.TextChanged += editTextBox_TextChanged;
						if (!editTextBox.IsLoaded)
						{
							editTextBox.Loaded += editTextBox_Loaded;
						}
					}
					return editTextBox;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		private void editTextBox_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateRenderText();
		}

		private void editTextBox_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateRenderText();
		}

		private void UpdateRenderText()
		{
			if (editTextBox != null && editTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				int caretIndex = editTextBox.CaretIndex;
				string text = ((editTextBox.Text == null) ? null : editTextBox.Text.Clone().ToString());
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = string.Empty;
				}
				editTextBox.Text = text.Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol());
				editTextBox.Select((caretIndex > text.Length) ? text.Length : caretIndex, 0);
			}
		}

		static AesXamComboEdit()
		{
			VisualContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VisualContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			AutoBindProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoBind", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			VariablePathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariablePath", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			VariableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Variable", typeof(object), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			VariableDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariableDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("SelectedItem"));
			PromptProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Prompt", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			PromptsDictionaryProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PromptsDictionary", typeof(Dictionary<string, string>), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnly", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			MaxLengthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxLength", typeof(int), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0, OnMaxLengthPropertyChanged));
			DisplayedCaseProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayedCase", typeof(AesDisplayedCase), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesDisplayedCase.None));
			MaskProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Mask", typeof(AesMaskOption), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesMaskOption.None, OnMaskChanged));
			DisplayAsTextBoxProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayAsTextBox", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			IsInScrollAreaProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollArea", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			RenderTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RenderText", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnRenderTextPropertyChanged));
			DisplayValueToRenderTextConverterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayValueToRenderTextConverter", typeof(IValueConverter), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			UpdateSourceWhenValueChangedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UpdateSourceWhenValueChanged", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			IsMMEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsMMEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			ValueUpdateTriggerProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueUpdateTrigger", typeof(UpdateSourceTrigger), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged));
			F4KeyDownEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("F4KeyDownEvent", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			DisallowClearProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisallowClear", typeof(bool?), typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new UIPropertyMetadata(null));
			TooltipTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TooltipTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			ItemTooltipTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemTooltipTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(AesXamComboEdit));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesXamComboEdit)));
			XamComboEditor.DropDownButtonDisplayModeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DropDownButtonDisplayMode.MouseOver));
			XamComboEditor.DropDownResizeModeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PopupResizeMode.None));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Delete, Delete_Executed, Delete_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Paste, Paste_Executed, Paste_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamComboEdit), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Copy, Copy_Executed, Copy_CanExecute));
		}

		public AesXamComboEdit()
		{
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.CaseFullFilePathProperty, _clipboardCaseFullFilePathBinding);
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.ApplicationNameProperty, _clipboardApplicationNameBinding);
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.CxsItemMonikerProperty, _clipboardItemMonikerBinding);
			_autoBindControlHelper = new AutoBindControlHelper(this);
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(IsMMEnabledProperty, VariableProperty, CreateIsMMEnabledBinding()));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty, VariableProperty, CreateIsMMEnabledBinding()));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(PromptProperty, VariableProperty, Helpers.CreatePromptBindingForXamCombo(this)));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(ValueEditor.FormatProperty, VariableProperty, CreateFormatBinding()));
			UpdateRenderTextBinding();
		}

		private void UpdateRenderTextBinding()
		{
			SetBinding(RenderTextProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(XamComboEditor.DisplayValueProperty),
				Converter = DisplayValueToRenderTextConverter,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		protected virtual BindingBase CreateIsMMEnabledBinding()
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.IsEnabled")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.IsReadOnly")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding(ShouldBindIsEnabledToIsReadOnlyProperty.Name)
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = this,
						Path = new PropertyPath(XamComboEditor.ItemsSourceProperty)
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("IsEditable")
					{
						Source = this
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesComboEditIsMMEnabledConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			};
		}

		protected virtual BindingBase CreateFormatBinding()
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.ValueType")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.Dimension")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Mask")
					{
						Source = this
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesControlFormatConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = null
			};
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				if (e.Property == ValueEditor.ValueProperty && e.NewValue != null)
				{
					if (e.NewValue.GetType() == typeof(double) && base.ValueType == typeof(string) && Variable == null)
					{
						base.ValueType = typeof(double);
						base.Format = AesControlFormatConverter.DefaultFormat;
					}
					if (e.NewValue.GetType() == typeof(string) && (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage))
					{
						string systemDecimalSymbol = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
						if (systemDecimalSymbol != "." && e.NewValue.ToString().Contains(systemDecimalSymbol))
						{
							e = new DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs(ValueEditor.ValueProperty, e.OldValue, e.NewValue.ToString().Replace(systemDecimalSymbol, "."));
						}
					}
				}
				else if (e.Property == ValueEditor.ValueTypeProperty && e.NewValue != null && e.NewValue is Type && Mask == AesMaskOption.None)
				{
					if ((Type)e.NewValue == typeof(double))
					{
						Mask = AesMaskOption.Double;
					}
					else if ((Type)e.NewValue == typeof(int))
					{
						Mask = AesMaskOption.Integer;
					}
				}
				_autoBindControlHelper.OnPropertyChanged(e);
				if (e.Property == MaskProperty || e.Property == MaxLengthProperty || e.Property == ValueEditor.ValueTypeProperty || e.Property == UIElement.IsEnabledProperty || e.Property == ValueEditor.IsReadOnlyProperty)
				{
					UpdateMaxLength();
				}
				base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (_autoBindControlHelper.IsInAutoBinding(dp))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
			if (PromptsDictionary != null && PromptsDictionary.Count > 0 && e.OriginalSource is ComboBoxItem { Content: not null } comboBoxItem)
			{
				string itemContent = comboBoxItem.Content.ToString();
				if (PromptsDictionary.Count((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(itemContent, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) > 0)
				{
					string value = PromptsDictionary.FirstOrDefault((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(itemContent, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).Value;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
					{
						if (_OriginalPrompt == null)
						{
							_OriginalPrompt = Prompt;
						}
						OnPromptDisplayed(value);
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!base.IsDropDownOpen || PromptsDictionary == null || PromptsDictionary.Count == 0)
			{
				OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			}
		}

		public void RaisePromptDisplayed()
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPromptDisplayed(string prompt)
		{
			RoutedEventArgs<string> e = new RoutedEventArgs<string>(prompt, AesControlBase.PromptDisplayedEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnMaxLengthChanged(int oldLength, int newLength)
		{
			if (base.IsEditable)
			{
				if (base.ValueConstraint == null)
				{
					base.ValueConstraint = new ValueConstraint();
				}
				base.ValueConstraint.MaxLength = newLength;
			}
		}

		private static void OnMaxLengthPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesXamComboEdit).OnMaxLengthChanged((int)e.OldValue, (int)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnRenderTextPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesXamComboEdit).UpdateRenderTextBinding();
		}

		private static void OnMaskChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesXamComboEdit).OnMaskChanged(e.NewValue as AesMaskOption?);
		}

		private void OnMaskChanged(AesMaskOption? newMask)
		{
			if (newMask.HasValue && (newMask == AesMaskOption.Double || newMask == AesMaskOption.Percentage) && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol() != ".")
			{
				UpdateComboBoxStyle();
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if ((Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage) && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol() != ".")
			{
				UpdateComboBoxStyle();
			}
			if (base.ComboBox != null && base.IsEditable)
			{
				base.ComboBox.IsTextSearchEnabled = false;
			}
			if (base.ComboBox != null)
			{
				base.ComboBox.ItemContainerGenerator.StatusChanged += ItemContainerGenerator_StatusChanged;
			}
		}

		private void ItemContainerGenerator_StatusChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.ComboBox.ItemContainerGenerator.Status != GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (cbiItems != null && cbiItems.Count != 0)
			{
				foreach (ComboBoxItem cbiItem in cbiItems)
				{
					cbiItem.GotFocus -= ComboBoxItem_GotFocus;
					cbiItem.LostFocus -= ComboBoxItem_LostFocus;
					if (base.IsEditable)
					{
						cbiItem.ToolTipOpening -= ComboBoxItem_ToolTipOpening;
					}
				}
			}
			cbiItems = new List<ComboBoxItem>();
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)base.ComboBox.Items)
			{
				if (base.ComboBox.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(item) is ComboBoxItem comboBoxItem)
				{
					comboBoxItem.GotFocus += ComboBoxItem_GotFocus;
					comboBoxItem.LostFocus += ComboBoxItem_LostFocus;
					if (base.IsEditable)
					{
						comboBoxItem.ToolTipOpening += ComboBoxItem_ToolTipOpening;
					}
					cbiItems.Add(comboBoxItem);
				}
			}
			if (cbiItems.Count != 0)
			{
				if (itemsPanel != null)
				{
					itemsPanel.MouseLeave -= itemsPanel_MouseLeave;
				}
				itemsPanel = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(cbiItems[0]) as Panel;
				if (itemsPanel != null)
				{
					itemsPanel.MouseLeave += itemsPanel_MouseLeave;
				}
			}
		}

		private void itemsPanel_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (cbiItems == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ComboBoxItem cbiItem in cbiItems)
			{
				if (cbiItem.ToolTip != null && cbiItem.ToolTip is ToolTip)
				{
					(cbiItem.ToolTip as ToolTip).IsOpen = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ComboBoxItem_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBoxItem comboBoxItem = sender as ComboBoxItem;
			UIElement uIElement = null;
			uIElement = (UIElement)(comboBoxItem.ToolTip = ((ItemTooltipTemplate != null) ? ((object)new ContentControl
			{
				Content = comboBoxItem.Content,
				ContentTemplate = ItemTooltipTemplate
			}) : ((object)new TextBlock
			{
				Text = OptionPromptToToolTipConverter.ConvertToPrompt(comboBoxItem.Content, this),
				TextWrapping = TextWrapping.WrapWithOverflow,
				MaxWidth = 250.0
			})));
			Point point = comboBoxItem.TranslatePoint(new Point(0.0, 0.0), base.ComboBox);
			ToolTipService.SetPlacementTarget(comboBoxItem, base.ComboBox);
			ToolTipService.SetPlacement(comboBoxItem, PlacementMode.RelativePoint);
			ToolTipService.SetVerticalOffset(comboBoxItem, point.Y);
			ToolTipService.SetHorizontalOffset(comboBoxItem, point.X + comboBoxItem.RenderSize.Width);
			ToolTipService.SetInitialShowDelay(comboBoxItem, 0);
			if (comboBoxItem.PointToScreen(new Point(uIElement.RenderSize.Width + comboBoxItem.RenderSize.Width, 0.0)).X > SystemParameters.VirtualScreenWidth)
			{
				ToolTipService.SetHorizontalOffset(comboBoxItem, point.X - uIElement.RenderSize.Width);
			}
			HighlightedItem = ((comboBoxItem.DataContext == null) ? null : comboBoxItem.DataContext.ToString());
		}

		private void ComboBoxItem_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((sender as ComboBoxItem).ToolTip is ToolTip toolTip)
			{
				toolTip.IsOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void ComboBoxItem_ToolTipOpening(object sender, ToolTipEventArgs e)
		{
			RoutedEventArgs<string> e2 = new RoutedEventArgs<string>(OptionPromptToToolTipConverter.ConvertToPrompt((sender as ComboBoxItem).Content, this), AesControlBase.PromptDisplayedEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e2);
		}

		private void UpdateComboBoxStyle()
		{
			if (base.ComboBoxStyle != null)
			{
				Style style = new Style(typeof(ComboBox), base.ComboBoxStyle);
				ComponentResourceKey resourceKey = new ComponentResourceKey(typeof(AesXamComboEdit), "realLocalizerTemplate");
				style.Setters.Add(new Setter(ItemsControl.ItemTemplateProperty, FindResource(resourceKey)));
				base.ComboBoxStyle = style;
			}
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(properties);
		}

		protected static void GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			if (properties != null)
			{
				properties.Add(AutoBindProperty);
				properties.Add(VariableDataMemberProperty);
				properties.Add(ValueUpdateTriggerProperty);
			}
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(items);
		}

		protected static void GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			items?.Add(new VariablePathToVariableBindingItem(VariablePathProperty, VariableProperty));
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
		}

		protected void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (string.Equals(VariableDataMember, "SelectedItem", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, XamComboEditor.ItemsSourceProperty, "Items")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, ValueEditor.ValueProperty, "SelectedItem")
				{
					Trigger = ValueUpdateTrigger
				});
			}
			else
			{
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, XamComboEditor.ItemsSourceProperty, VariableDataMember + "Options")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
				{
					items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, ValueEditor.ValueProperty, VariableDataMember)
					{
						Trigger = ValueUpdateTrigger,
						Converter = new DoubleValueDisplayedAsStringConverter()
					});
				}
				else
				{
					items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, ValueEditor.ValueProperty, VariableDataMember)
					{
						Trigger = ValueUpdateTrigger
					});
				}
			}
			items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, "IsVisible")
			{
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Converter = new AesControlVisibilityConverter()
			});
			items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, PromptsDictionaryProperty, "OptionPrompts")
			{
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		private static void Delete_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(sender is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (aesXamComboEdit.Value != null && aesXamComboEdit.Value.GetType() == typeof(string) && aesXamComboEdit.VariableDataMember != "Units")
			{
				aesXamComboEdit.Value = null;
				BindingExpression bindingExpression = aesXamComboEdit.GetBindingExpression(ValueEditor.ValueProperty);
				if (bindingExpression != null)
				{
					aesXamComboEdit.IsUpdatingSource = true;
					bindingExpression.UpdateSource();
					aesXamComboEdit.IsUpdatingSource = false;
				}
				if (aesXamComboEdit.IsDropDownOpen)
				{
					aesXamComboEdit.IsDropDownOpen = false;
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private static void Delete_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamComboEdit { DisallowClear: var disallowClear } aesXamComboEdit)
			{
				if (disallowClear.HasValue && aesXamComboEdit.DisallowClear.Value)
				{
					e.CanExecute = false;
				}
				else if (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(aesXamComboEdit, typeof(AesVariableControlBase), allowSubclassOfType: true) is AesVariableControlBase aesVariableControlBase)
				{
					aesVariableControlBase.CanClear(e);
				}
				else
				{
					e.CanExecute = !aesXamComboEdit.IsReadOnly && aesXamComboEdit.IsEnabled;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void Copy_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit)
			{
				aesXamComboEdit.OnCopy();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void Copy_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit)
			{
				e.CanExecute = aesXamComboEdit.CanCopy();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnCopy()
		{
			if (base.Value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = null;
			if (base.IsInEditMode && base.IsEditable)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox != null)
				{
					text = VisiableTextBox.SelectedText;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text = base.Text;
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage || (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && base.ValueType == typeof(string)))
				{
					text = text.Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol());
				}
			}
			if (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(this, typeof(AesVariableControlBase), allowSubclassOfType: true) is AesVariableControlBase obj)
			{
				ClipboardHelper.CreateClipboard(new Collection<string>(new string[1] { DataForClipboard.GetCxsItemMoniker(obj) }), text, DataForClipboard.GetCaseFullFilePath(obj), DataForClipboard.GetApplicationName(obj));
			}
			else
			{
				ClipboardWrapper.SetText(text);
			}
		}

		private bool CanCopy()
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode && base.IsEditable)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(VisiableTextBox.SelectedText);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text))
			{
				return base.IsEnabled;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static void Paste_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit)
			{
				aesXamComboEdit.OnPaste(e.Parameter);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void Paste_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit)
			{
				e.CanExecute = aesXamComboEdit.CanPaste(e.Parameter);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnPaste(object parameter)
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode && base.IsEditable && VisiableTextBox != null)
			{
				int num = VisiableTextBox.SelectionStart + pasteStringLength;
				base.Text = stringToPaste;
				if (stringToPaste.Length >= num)
				{
					VisiableTextBox.Select(num, 0);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.Dispatcher.Invoke(delegate
				{
					StartEditMode();
					base.Text = stringToPaste;
					EndEditMode(acceptChanges: true, force: true);
				});
			}
		}

		private bool CanPaste(object parameter)
		{
			if (parameter == null)
			{
				try
				{
					stringToPaste = ClipboardWrapper.GetText().Replace("\r", string.Empty).Replace("\n", string.Empty);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			else
			{
				stringToPaste = parameter.ToString();
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stringToPaste))
			{
				return false;
			}
			pasteStringLength = stringToPaste.Length;
			if (base.IsInEditMode && base.IsEditable)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				stringToPaste = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, stringToPaste);
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && double.TryParse(stringToPaste, out var result))
				{
					stringToPaste = result.ToString();
				}
				return MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextMaskable(stringToPaste, MaxLength, Mask);
			}
			if (base.IsReadOnly || !base.IsEnabled)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (base.IsEditable && NeedToMask(stringToPaste))
			{
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && double.TryParse(stringToPaste, out var result2))
				{
					stringToPaste = result2.ToString();
				}
				if (MaxLength != 0 && stringToPaste.Length > MaxLength)
				{
					stringToPaste = stringToPaste.Substring(0, MaxLength);
				}
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
				{
					if (!stringToPaste.EndsWith("%"))
					{
						_ = stringToPaste;
					}
					else
					{
						stringToPaste.Substring(0, stringToPaste.Length - 1);
					}
					if (double.TryParse(stringToPaste, out var result3))
					{
						stringToPaste = (stringToPaste.EndsWith("%") ? (result3 + "%") : result3.ToString());
					}
				}
				return MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(stringToPaste, MaxLength, Mask);
			}
			bool result4 = true;
			if (base.ItemsSource != null)
			{
				IEnumerable<object> source = base.ItemsSource.Cast<object>();
				int num = ((MaxLength != 0) ? MaxLength : 8);
				if (!source.Contains(stringToPaste) && (!source.Contains(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.NewCommandInComboBox) || stringToPaste.Length > num))
				{
					result4 = false;
				}
			}
			return result4;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewLostKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Point position = Mouse.GetPosition(this);
			if (base.IsEditable && ((e.NewFocus is ContextMenu && position.X <= base.ActualWidth && position.Y <= base.ActualHeight && position.X >= 0.0 && position.Y >= 0.0) || e.NewFocus is MenuItem))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (_OriginalPrompt != null && _OriginalPrompt != Prompt)
			{
				OnPromptDisplayed(_OriginalPrompt);
			}
			base.OnPreviewLostKeyboardFocus(e);
		}

		protected override void OnSelectedItemChanged(object previousValue, object currentValue)
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (base.Value == null && base.SelectedIndex >= 0)
				{
					base.SelectedIndex = -1;
				}
			}, DispatcherPriority.Normal);
			base.OnSelectedItemChanged(previousValue, currentValue);
		}

		protected override void OnValueChanged(object previousValue, object currentValue)
		{
			base.OnValueChanged(previousValue, currentValue);
			if (UpdateSourceWhenValueChanged)
			{
				BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(this, ValueEditor.ValueProperty).UpdateSource();
			}
			else if (currentValue != null && base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && PromptsDictionary != null && PromptsDictionary.Count > 0 && PromptsDictionary.Count((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(currentValue.ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) > 0)
			{
				string value = PromptsDictionary.FirstOrDefault((KeyValuePair<string, string> p) => p.Key.Equals(currentValue.ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).Value;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					OnPromptDisplayed(value);
					return;
				}
			}
			if (previousValue == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string testResult = string.Empty;
			ConvertValueToDisplayText(currentValue, out testResult, out var error);
			if (!base.IsInEditMode && testResult != base.DisplayText && error == null)
			{
				ClearValue(ValueEditor.ValueProperty);
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					SetValue(ValueEditor.ValueProperty, currentValue);
				}, null);
			}
			if (!base.IsInEditMode || !(testResult != base.SelectedItem as string) || error != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (!base.IsEditable && base.ItemsSource is IEnumerable<string> source && !source.Contains(testResult))
				{
					base.SelectedIndex = -1;
				}
				else
				{
					SetValue(XamComboEditor.SelectedItemProperty, testResult);
				}
			}, null);
		}

		protected override void OnTextChanged(string previousValue, string currentValue)
		{
			base.OnTextChanged(previousValue, currentValue);
			if (base.IsInEditMode && ((base.Value == null && currentValue != "") || (base.Value != null && base.Value.GetType() == typeof(string) && currentValue != base.Value as string)))
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<bool, bool>(base.EndEditMode), true, false);
			}
			else if (base.Value != null && base.Value.GetType() != typeof(string) && currentValue != base.Value.ToString())
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<bool, bool>(base.EndEditMode), true, false);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewKeyDown(e);
			if (e.Key == Key.F4)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
				RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(F4KeyDownEvent));
			}
			else if (e.Key == Key.Delete && base.IsDropDownOpen)
			{
				base.IsDropDownOpen = false;
			}
			if (e.Key == Key.Back || e.Key == Key.Delete || e.Key == Key.Space || e.Key == Key.Decimal)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					string empty = string.Empty;
					empty = ((e.Key == Key.Space) ? MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, " ") : ((e.Key != Key.Decimal) ? MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenDeleteText(VisiableTextBox, e.Key) : MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol())));
					if (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
					{
						if (!MaskingAndValidatingHelper.maskIntWithExponentValue(empty, MaxLength))
						{
							e.Handled = true;
						}
					}
					else if (NeedToMask(empty) && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextMaskable(empty, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.Space)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					SystemSounds.Asterisk.Play();
				}
			}
			else if (e.Key == Key.OemPeriod)
			{
				if ((Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage) && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol() != ".")
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					SystemSounds.Asterisk.Play();
				}
			}
			else if (e.Key == Key.OemComma)
			{
				if ((Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage) && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol() != ",")
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					SystemSounds.Asterisk.Play();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeEnded(EditModeEndedEventArgs args)
		{
			base.OnEditModeEnded(args);
			BindingExpression bindingExpression = GetBindingExpression(ValueEditor.ValueProperty);
			if (bindingExpression != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding.UpdateSourceTrigger == UpdateSourceTrigger.Explicit)
			{
				bindingExpression.UpdateSource();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnDropDownClosed(RoutedEventArgs args)
		{
			base.OnDropDownClosed(args);
			BindingExpression bindingExpression = GetBindingExpression(ValueEditor.ValueProperty);
			if (bindingExpression != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding != null && bindingExpression.ParentBinding.UpdateSourceTrigger == UpdateSourceTrigger.Explicit && !IsUpdatingSource)
			{
				bindingExpression.UpdateSource();
			}
			HighlightedItem = null;
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeEnding(EditModeEndingEventArgs args)
		{
			if (VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.ContextMenu != null && VisiableTextBox.ContextMenu.IsOpen)
			{
				args.Handled = true;
				args.Cancel = true;
				return;
			}
			string text = base.Text.Clone().ToString();
			if (NeedToMask(text) && (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage || (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && base.ValueType == typeof(string))))
			{
				if (MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(text, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly))
				{
					if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
					{
						base.Value = text.Replace(MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
					}
					else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
					{
						base.Value = text.Replace(MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					args.AcceptChanges = false;
					MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupInvalidValueWarningMsg(text);
				}
			}
			base.OnEditModeEnding(args);
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeStarted(EditModeStartedEventArgs args)
		{
			base.OnEditModeStarted(args);
			_ = VisiableTextBox;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			if (VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				string previewInputStringWhenAddText = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, e.Text);
				if (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
				{
					if (!MaskingAndValidatingHelper.maskIntWithExponentValue(previewInputStringWhenAddText, MaxLength))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				else if (NeedToMask(previewInputStringWhenAddText) && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextMaskable(previewInputStringWhenAddText, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					SystemSounds.Asterisk.Play();
				}
			}
			base.OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}

		private void UpdateMaxLength()
		{
			if (!base.IsEnabled || base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				ClearValue(XamMaskedEditor.MaskProperty);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength < 16) ? 16 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Subroutine)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 16 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranVariable)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 8 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentId)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 8 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentName)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 10 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Id)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 8 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength < 16) ? 16 : MaxLength);
				if (base.ValueConstraint != null)
				{
					base.ValueConstraint.MaxLength = 0;
				}
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 16 : MaxLength);
			}
		}

		protected override object OnCoerceValue(object val)
		{
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				return val;
			}
			return base.OnCoerceValue(val);
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeValidationError(EditModeValidationErrorEventArgs args)
		{
			string text = base.Text.Clone().ToString();
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer)
			{
				if (((base.ValueType == typeof(double) || (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer)) && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(text, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly)) || (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.validIntWithExponentValue(text, MaxLength)))
				{
					RevertValueBackToOriginalValue();
					MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupInvalidValueWarningMsg(text);
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text))
				{
					if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
					{
						double num = double.Parse(double.Parse(base.Text).ToString("g12"));
						if (base.ValueType == typeof(double))
						{
							base.Value = num;
						}
						else if (base.ValueType == typeof(int))
						{
							base.Value = (int)num;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						string s = int.Parse(text).ToString("g12");
						base.Value = int.Parse(s);
					}
					InitializeOriginalValueFromValue();
				}
				args.InvalidValueBehavior = InvalidValueBehavior.RevertValue;
				args.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnEditModeValidationError(args);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseMove(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		private bool NeedToMask(string testValue)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (base.ItemsSource != null)
			{
				try
				{
					string value = testValue.Replace(MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
					foreach (object item in base.ItemsSource)
					{
						if (item != null && item.ToString().Equals(value, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
						{
							result = false;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class AesXamMaskedEdit : XamMaskedEditor
	{
		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Property == ValueEditor.ValueTypeProperty && e.NewValue as Type == typeof(double))
			{
				base.Format = "g12";
			}
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode && base.ContextMenu == null)
			{
				ContextMenu contextMenu = new ContextMenu();
				string text = "pack://application:,,,/" + typeof(AesXamMaskedEdit).Assembly.FullName + ";component/Images/";
				Image icon = new Image
				{
					Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Undo16.ico"))
				};
				Image icon2 = new Image
				{
					Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Cut16.ico"))
				};
				Image icon3 = new Image
				{
					Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Copy16.ico"))
				};
				Image icon4 = new Image
				{
					Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Paste16.ico"))
				};
				Image icon5 = new Image
				{
					Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + "Clear16.ico"))
				};
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderUndo,
					Icon = icon,
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Undo,
					CommandTarget = this
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = icon2,
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderCut,
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Cut,
					CommandTarget = this,
					IsEnabled = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.SelectedText)
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = icon3,
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderCopy,
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Copy,
					CommandTarget = this,
					IsEnabled = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.SelectedText)
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = icon4,
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderPaste,
					Command = ApplicationCommands.Paste,
					CommandTarget = this
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ContextMenuHeaderSelectAll,
					Command = ApplicationCommands.SelectAll,
					CommandTarget = this,
					IsEnabled = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text)
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = icon5,
					Command = AesVariableControlBase.ClearVariableCommand,
					CommandTarget = (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(this, typeof(AesXamValueEdit), allowSubclassOfType: false) as IInputElement)
				});
				contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				base.OnContextMenuOpening(e);
			}
		}
	}
