		private static void OnPromptChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit = d as AesXamValueEdit;
			if (e.NewValue != null && aesXamValueEdit.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				aesXamValueEdit.OnPromptDisplayed(e.NewValue.ToString());
			}
		}

		static AesXamValueEdit()
		{
			AutoBindProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoBind", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			VariablePathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariablePath", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamValueEdit));
			VariableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Variable", typeof(object), typeof(AesXamValueEdit));
			VariableDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariableDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("SelectedItem"));
			LowerLimitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LowerLimit", typeof(double?), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			UpperLimitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("UpperLimit", typeof(double?), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			SpinStepSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SpinStepSize", typeof(object), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1));
			SpinVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SpinVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed));
			BrowseVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BrowseVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed));
			BrowseCommandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BrowseCommand", typeof(ICommand), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), null);
			PromptProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Prompt", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnPromptChanged));
			MaxLengthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaxLength", typeof(int), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0, OnMaxLengthPropertyChanged, OnMaxLengthPropertyCoerced));
			DisplayedCaseProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayedCase", typeof(AesDisplayedCase), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesDisplayedCase.None));
			MaskProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Mask", typeof(AesMaskOption), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(AesMaskOption.None));
			IsMMEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsMMEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			IsInScrollAreaProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollArea", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			IsInScrollAreaColumnHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInScrollAreaColumnHeader", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			GreaterThanLowerLimitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GreaterThanLowerLimit", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			AcceptsReturnProperty = TextBoxBase.AcceptsReturnProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			MaxLinesProperty = TextBox.MaxLinesProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(TextBox.MaxLinesProperty.DefaultMetadata.DefaultValue));
			InnerMaskPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("InnerMask", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			InnerMaskProperty = InnerMaskPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CharacterCasingProperty = TextBox.CharacterCasingProperty.AddOwner(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(TextBox.CharacterCasingProperty.DefaultMetadata.DefaultValue));
			ValueUpdateTriggerProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueUpdateTrigger", typeof(UpdateSourceTrigger), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged));
			IsInnerValidationEnableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsInnerValidationEnable", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			bCanBrowseCommand = true;
			objLocker = new object();
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesXamValueEdit)));
			XamTextEditor.HorizontalScrollBarVisibilityProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ScrollBarVisibility.Hidden));
			XamTextEditor.VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ScrollBarVisibility.Hidden));
			ValueEditor.TextProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(string.Empty, TextPropertyChanged));
			UIElement.AllowDropProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new CommandBinding(AesControls.SpinUpCommand, OnSpinUpCommand));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new CommandBinding(AesControls.SpinDownCommand, OnSpinDownCommand));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new CommandBinding(AesControls.BrowseCommand, OnBrowseCommand, OnBrowseCanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Copy, Copy_Executed, Copy_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Paste, Paste_Executed, Paste_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(AesXamValueEdit), new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Cut, Cut_Executed, Cut_CanExecute));
		}

		public AesXamValueEdit()
		{
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.CaseFullFilePathProperty, _clipboardCaseFullFilePathBinding);
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.ApplicationNameProperty, _clipboardApplicationNameBinding);
			SetBinding(DataForClipboard.CxsItemMonikerProperty, _clipboardItemMonikerBinding);
			_autoBindControlHelper = new AutoBindControlHelper(this);
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(IsMMEnabledProperty, VariableProperty, AesVariableControlBase.CreateDefaultIsMMEnabledBinding(this, null)));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(ValueEditor.IsReadOnlyProperty, VariableProperty, new Binding("Variable.IsReadOnly")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = false
			}));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(PromptProperty, VariableProperty, Helpers.CreatePromptBindingForXam(this)));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(ValueEditor.FormatProperty, VariableProperty, CreateFormatBinding()));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			xamMaskedEditor = GetTemplateChild("PART_XamMaskedEditor") as XamMaskedEditor;
			TextBox textBox = GetTemplateChild("PART_TextBox") as TextBox;
			if (xamMaskedEditor != null && !xamMaskedEditor.IsLoaded)
			{
				xamMaskedEditor.Loaded += delegate
				{
					xamMaskedEditor.ExecutedCommand += delegate(object o, ExecutedCommandEventArgs e)
					{
						if (xamMaskedEditor != null)
						{
							if (xamMaskedEditor.Text != null && xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart > xamMaskedEditor.Text.Length)
							{
								xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart = xamMaskedEditor.Text.Length;
								e.Handled = true;
							}
							if (e.Command == MaskedEditorCommands.SetPivot && xamMaskedEditor.DisplayText != null && xamMaskedEditor.SelectionLength > xamMaskedEditor.DisplayText.Length)
							{
								xamMaskedEditor.SelectionLength = xamMaskedEditor.DisplayText.Length;
							}
						}
					};
				};
			}
			if (textBox != null)
			{
				if (base.IsInEditMode)
				{
					editTextBox = textBox;
				}
				else
				{
					normalTextBox = textBox;
				}
			}
			if (VisiableTextBox != null)
			{
				BuildTextBoxContextMenu();
				VisiableTextBox.ContextMenu = _textBoxContextMenu;
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual BindingBase CreateFormatBinding()
		{
			return new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.ValueType")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Variable.Dimension")
					{
						Source = this
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding("Mask")
					{
						Source = this
					}
				},
				Converter = new AesControlFormatConverter(),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = null
			};
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Property == VariableProperty && e.NewValue == null && base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				EndEditMode(acceptChanges: false, force: true);
				if (xamMaskedEditor != null && xamMaskedEditor.IsInEditMode)
				{
					xamMaskedEditor.EndEditMode(acceptChanges: false, force: true);
				}
			}
			else if (e.Property == ValueEditor.ValueProperty && e.NewValue != null)
			{
				if (e.NewValue.GetType() == typeof(double) && base.ValueType == typeof(string) && Variable == null)
				{
					base.ValueType = typeof(double);
					base.Format = AesControlFormatConverter.DefaultFormat;
				}
				if (base.IsInEditMode)
				{
					ValuedChangedInEdit = true;
				}
			}
			else if (e.Property == ValueEditor.ValueTypeProperty && e.NewValue != null && e.NewValue is Type && Mask == AesMaskOption.None)
			{
				if ((Type)e.NewValue == typeof(double))
				{
					Mask = AesMaskOption.Double;
				}
				else if ((Type)e.NewValue == typeof(int))
				{
					Mask = AesMaskOption.Integer;
				}
			}
			_autoBindControlHelper.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			if (e.Property == MaskProperty || e.Property == LowerLimitProperty || e.Property == UpperLimitProperty || e.Property == MaxLengthProperty || e.Property == ValueEditor.ValueTypeProperty || e.Property == UIElement.IsEnabledProperty || e.Property == ValueEditor.IsReadOnlyProperty)
			{
				UpdateBaseMask();
			}
			if (e.Property == LowerLimitProperty || e.Property == UpperLimitProperty)
			{
				UpdateValueConstraint();
			}
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeEnding(EditModeEndingEventArgs args)
		{
			OnEditModeEnding(args);
			if (VisiableTextBox != null && _textBoxContextMenu.IsOpen)
			{
				args.Handled = true;
				args.Cancel = true;
			}
			else
			{
				bool acceptChanges = args.AcceptChanges;
				ForceUpdateForEndingEditMode(ref acceptChanges);
				args.AcceptChanges = acceptChanges;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(e);
			if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && IsInScrollArea)
			{
				bool acceptChanges = false;
				ForceUpdateForEndingEditMode(ref acceptChanges);
			}
		}

		private void ForceUpdateForEndingEditMode(ref bool acceptChanges)
		{
			if (base.IsReadOnly || _isEndingEditMode)
			{
				return;
			}
			_isEndingEditMode = true;
			string text = base.Text.Clone().ToString();
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage || Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentId || Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentName || Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer || Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranExpression || (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && base.ValueType == typeof(double)))
			{
				if (MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(text, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly))
				{
					if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
					{
						bool flag = text.EndsWith("%");
						if (double.TryParse(text.Substring(0, flag ? (text.Length - 1) : text.Length), out var result))
						{
							if (base.ValueType == typeof(double))
							{
								base.Value = result.ToString().Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol()) + (flag ? "%" : string.Empty);
							}
							else
							{
								base.Value = result.ToString().Replace(MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".") + (flag ? "%" : string.Empty);
							}
						}
						else if (base.ValueType == typeof(double))
						{
							base.Value = text.Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol()) + (flag ? "%" : string.Empty);
						}
						else
						{
							base.Value = result.ToString().Replace(MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".") + (flag ? "%" : string.Empty);
						}
					}
					else if (IsInnerValidationEnable && Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						double num = double.Parse(text);
						if ((UpperLimit.HasValue && num > UpperLimit.Value) || (!UpperLimit.HasValue && num > 2147483647.0) || (LowerLimit.HasValue && (GreaterThanLowerLimit ? (num <= LowerLimit.Value) : (num < LowerLimit.Value))) || (!LowerLimit.HasValue && num < -2147483648.0))
						{
							acceptChanges = false;
							MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupOutOfWarningMsg(UpperLimit, LowerLimit, GreaterThanLowerLimit);
						}
					}
					else if (IsInnerValidationEnable && Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						double num2 = double.Parse(text);
						if ((UpperLimit.HasValue && num2 > UpperLimit.Value) || (!UpperLimit.HasValue && num2 > double.MaxValue) || (LowerLimit.HasValue && (GreaterThanLowerLimit ? (num2 <= LowerLimit.Value) : (num2 < LowerLimit.Value))) || (!LowerLimit.HasValue && num2 < double.MinValue))
						{
							acceptChanges = false;
							MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupOutOfWarningMsg(UpperLimit, LowerLimit, GreaterThanLowerLimit);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					acceptChanges = false;
					MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupInvalidValueWarningMsg(text, Mask);
				}
			}
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.AsciiChar && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.ArePureAsciiCharacters(text))
			{
				acceptChanges = false;
				MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupNoneAsciiCharMsg();
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranVariable)
			{
				if (!MaskingAndValidatingHelper.validFortranVariableValue(text, MaxLength))
				{
					acceptChanges = false;
					MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupInvalidValue(text);
				}
				else if (!MaskingAndValidatingHelper.ArePureAsciiCharacters(text))
				{
					acceptChanges = false;
					MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupNoneAsciiCharMsg();
				}
			}
			_isEndingEditMode = false;
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (_autoBindControlHelper.IsInAutoBinding(dp))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			base.OnPreviewMouseDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseDoubleClick(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseDoubleClick(e);
			if (base.IsInEditMode && editTextBox != null)
			{
				editTextBox.SelectAll();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			this.TriggerCommands(e, onlyWhenReadonly: false);
			if (!base.IsEmbedded && e.Key != Key.Escape && e.Key != Key.Tab && e.Key != Key.Return && (e.Key < Key.F1 || e.Key > Key.F24) && e.SystemKey == Key.None && e.Key != Key.Left && e.Key != Key.Up && e.Key != Key.Right && e.Key != Key.Down && e.Key != Key.Insert && e.Key != Key.Home && e.Key != Key.End && e.Key != Key.Prior && e.Key != Key.Next && e.Key != Key.NumLock && e.Key != Key.Space && (Keyboard.Modifiers & (ModifierKeys.Alt | ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift)) == 0)
			{
				if (!base.IsInEditMode)
				{
					StartEditMode();
					if (xamMaskedEditor.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						if (xamMaskedEditor.Text != null)
						{
							xamMaskedEditor.Text = string.Empty;
						}
					}
					else if (VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && VisiableTextBox.Text != null)
					{
						VisiableTextBox.Text = string.Empty;
					}
				}
				else if (xamMaskedEditor.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					if (!xamMaskedEditor.IsInEditMode)
					{
						xamMaskedEditor.StartEditMode();
					}
					if (!xamMaskedEditor.IsFocusWithin)
					{
						xamMaskedEditor.Focus();
					}
				}
				else if (VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && !VisiableTextBox.IsFocused)
				{
					VisiableTextBox.Focus();
				}
			}
			if (base.IsInEditMode && SpinVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				if (e.Key == Key.Up)
				{
					AesControls.SpinUpCommand.Execute(null, this);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (e.Key == Key.Down)
				{
					AesControls.SpinDownCommand.Execute(null, this);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			if (base.IsReadOnly && e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!base.IsReadOnly && e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!base.IsReadOnly && base.IsInEditMode && e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				EndEditMode(acceptChanges: false, force: true);
			}
			if (!base.IsReadOnly && base.IsInEditMode && e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				EndEditMode(acceptChanges: true, force: true);
			}
			if (((Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage || Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer) && base.IsInEditMode) || Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentId || Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentName || Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranExpression)
			{
				if (e.Key == Key.Back || e.Key == Key.Delete || e.Key == Key.Space || e.Key == Key.Decimal)
				{
					if (VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						string empty = string.Empty;
						empty = ((e.Key == Key.Space) ? MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, " ") : ((e.Key != Key.Decimal) ? MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenDeleteText(VisiableTextBox, e.Key) : MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol())));
						if (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.maskIntWithExponentValue(empty, MaxLength))
						{
							e.Handled = true;
						}
						else if (!MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextMaskable(empty, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly))
						{
							e.Handled = true;
						}
					}
					else if (e.Key == Key.Space)
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.OemPeriod)
				{
					if ((Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage) && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol() != ".")
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.OemComma && (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage) && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol() != ",")
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					SystemSounds.Asterisk.Play();
				}
			}
			base.OnPreviewKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
		}

		public void RaisePromptDisplayed()
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPromptDisplayed(string prompt)
		{
			RoutedEventArgs<string> e = new RoutedEventArgs<string>(prompt, AesControlBase.PromptDisplayedEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnMaxLengthChanged(int oldLength, int newLength)
		{
			UpdateValueConstraint();
		}

		private void UpdateValueConstraint()
		{
			if (InnerMask == null || (Mask != AesMaskOption.Double && !(base.ValueType == typeof(double))))
			{
				if (base.ValueConstraint == null)
				{
					base.ValueConstraint = new ValueConstraint();
				}
				base.ValueConstraint.MaxLength = MaxLength;
			}
		}

		private static bool AreAllValidDoubleChars(string chars)
		{
			NumberFormatInfo currentInfo = NumberFormatInfo.CurrentInfo;
			foreach (char c in chars)
			{
				if (!char.IsNumber(c) && c != currentInfo.NegativeSign[0] && c != currentInfo.NumberDecimalSeparator[0] && c != currentInfo.NumberGroupSeparator[0] && c != currentInfo.PositiveSign[0])
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static bool IsAlphaKey(Key key)
		{
			if ((uint)(key - 44) <= 25u)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static bool IsOtherValidChar(char test)
		{
			switch (test)
			{
			case '$':
			case '&':
			case '(':
			case ')':
			case '*':
			case '+':
			case ',':
			case '-':
			case '.':
			case '/':
			case ':':
			case '<':
			case '=':
			case '>':
			case '[':
			case ']':
			case '{':
			case '}':
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected override void ProcessKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				Type valueTypeResolved = ValueTypeResolved;
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
				{
					if (e.Key == Key.Up)
					{
						AesControls.SpinUpCommand.Execute(null, this);
					}
					else if (e.Key == Key.Down)
					{
						AesControls.SpinDownCommand.Execute(null, this);
					}
				}
				else if (valueTypeResolved == typeof(string) && Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranVariable)
				{
					if (e.Key == Key.I)
					{
						if (xamMaskedEditor != null)
						{
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(xamMaskedEditor.Text) && xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart == 0 && xamMaskedEditor.Text[0] == 'Z')
							{
								e.Handled = true;
							}
						}
						else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text) && base.SelectionStart == 0 && base.Text[0] == 'Z')
						{
							e.Handled = true;
						}
					}
					else if (e.Key == Key.Z)
					{
						if (xamMaskedEditor != null)
						{
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(xamMaskedEditor.Text) && xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart == 1 && (xamMaskedEditor.Text[0] == 'I' || xamMaskedEditor.Text[0] == 'Z'))
							{
								e.Handled = true;
							}
						}
						else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text) && base.SelectionStart == 1 && (base.Text[0] == 'I' || base.Text[0] == 'Z'))
						{
							e.Handled = true;
						}
					}
					else if (e.Key >= Key.D0 && e.Key <= Key.D9)
					{
						if (xamMaskedEditor != null)
						{
							if (xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart == 0)
							{
								e.Handled = true;
							}
						}
						else if (base.SelectionStart == 0)
						{
							e.Handled = true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			base.ProcessKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsEmbedded || string.IsNullOrEmpty(InnerMask) || AcceptsReturn)
			{
				Helpers.ProcessKeyInput(this, e);
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnTextChanged(string previousValue, string currentValue)
		{
			if (base.Text == string.Empty)
			{
				base.Value = null;
			}
			if (IsInnerValidationEnable && (SpinVisibility == Visibility.Visible || Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer || base.ValueType == typeof(int)) && int.TryParse(currentValue, out var result))
			{
				if (LowerLimit.HasValue && LowerLimit.Value > (double)result)
				{
					base.Text = Convert.ToInt32(LowerLimit.Value).ToString();
					return;
				}
				if (UpperLimit.HasValue && UpperLimit.Value < (double)result)
				{
					base.Text = Convert.ToInt32(UpperLimit.Value).ToString();
					return;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text))
			{
				if (DisplayedCase == AesDisplayedCase.Uppercase)
				{
					string text = base.Text.ToUpperInvariant();
					if (base.Text != text)
					{
						base.Text = text;
					}
				}
				else if (DisplayedCase == AesDisplayedCase.Lowercase)
				{
					string text2 = base.Text.ToLowerInvariant();
					if (base.Text != text2)
					{
						base.Text = text2;
					}
				}
			}
			base.OnTextChanged(previousValue, currentValue);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Subroutine || Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranVariable)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Text) && !char.IsLetter(e.Text[0]) && xamMaskedEditor != null && xamMaskedEditor.SelectionStart == 0)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.None && base.ValueType == typeof(string))
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Text) && e.Text[0] == '"')
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else if ((Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage || Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer || Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentId || Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentName || Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranExpression) && VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				string previewInputStringWhenAddText = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, e.Text);
				if (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.maskIntWithExponentValue(previewInputStringWhenAddText, MaxLength))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				else if (!MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextMaskable(previewInputStringWhenAddText, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			if (e.Handled)
			{
				SystemSounds.Asterisk.Play();
			}
			((UIElement)this).OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}

		private static void TextPropertyChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (o is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit && e.NewValue != null && ((aesXamValueEdit.Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && aesXamValueEdit.ValueType != typeof(string)) || aesXamValueEdit.Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage))
			{
				string text = e.NewValue.ToString().Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol()).Replace("E", "e");
				if (!aesXamValueEdit.Text.Equals(text, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					aesXamValueEdit.Text = text;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override object OnCoerceValue(object val)
		{
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				return val;
			}
			return base.OnCoerceValue(val);
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeValidationError(EditModeValidationErrorEventArgs args)
		{
			string text = base.Text.Clone().ToString();
			bool flag = false;
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double || Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer)
			{
				if (((base.ValueType == typeof(double) || (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer)) && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(text, MaxLength, Mask, base.IsReadOnly)) || (base.ValueType == typeof(int) && Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && !MaskingAndValidatingHelper.validIntWithExponentValue(text, MaxLength)))
				{
					RevertValueBackToOriginalValue();
					MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupInvalidValueWarningMsg(text);
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text))
				{
					int result2;
					if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
					{
						if (double.TryParse(base.Text, out var result))
						{
							double num = double.Parse(result.ToString("g12"));
							if (base.ValueType == typeof(double))
							{
								base.Value = num;
							}
							else if (base.ValueType == typeof(int))
							{
								base.Value = (int)num;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (int.TryParse(text, out result2))
					{
						string s = result2.ToString("g12");
						base.Value = int.Parse(s);
					}
					InitializeOriginalValueFromValue();
				}
				args.InvalidValueBehavior = InvalidValueBehavior.RevertValue;
				args.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!flag && Mask != AesMaskOption.Double && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && MaxLength > 0 && text.Length > MaxLength && !base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				RevertValueBackToOriginalValue();
				MaskingAndValidatingHelper.PopupExceedMaxLengthWarningMsg(MaxLength);
				args.InvalidValueBehavior = InvalidValueBehavior.RevertValue;
				args.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnEditModeValidationError(args);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseWheel(MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseWheel(e);
			if (!base.IsInEditMode || SpinVisibility != Visibility.Visible)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = e.Delta / 120;
			if (num > 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					OnSpinUp();
				}
			}
			else if (num < 0)
			{
				for (int num2 = 0; num2 > num; num2--)
				{
					OnSpinDown();
				}
			}
		}

		public static void LockCommand(bool bLock)
		{
			lock (objLocker)
			{
				bCanBrowseCommand = !bLock;
			}
		}

		private static void OnBrowseCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				aesXamValueEdit.OnBrowse();
			}
		}

		private static void OnBrowseCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				lock (objLocker)
				{
					e.CanExecute = bCanBrowseCommand && aesXamValueEdit.CanBrowse();
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void OnBrowse()
		{
			if (BrowseCommand == null)
			{
				OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new OpenFileDialog();
				openFileDialog.RestoreDirectory = true;
				if (base.Value != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Value.ToString()))
				{
					openFileDialog.FileName = base.Value.ToString();
				}
				if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog().Value)
				{
					base.Value = openFileDialog.FileName;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				AesXamValueEditBrowse parameter = new AesXamValueEditBrowse(this);
				if (BrowseCommand.CanExecute(parameter))
				{
					BrowseCommand.Execute(parameter);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual bool CanBrowse()
		{
			if (BrowseCommand == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			AesXamValueEditBrowse parameter = new AesXamValueEditBrowse(this);
			return BrowseCommand.CanExecute(parameter);
		}

		private static void OnSpinUpCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AesXamValueEdit control = sender as AesXamValueEdit;
			if (control == null || control.IsReadOnly)
			{
				return;
			}
			Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
			{
				if (!control.IsInEditMode)
				{
					control.StartEditMode();
				}
				control.OnSpinUp();
			});
		}

		public virtual bool OnSpinUp()
		{
			bool result = false;
			int? currentValue = null;
			if (IsSpinAvailable(out currentValue))
			{
				result = true;
				int num = 1;
				if (SpinStepSize != null)
				{
					num = (int)SpinStepSize;
				}
				int? newValue = currentValue;
				if (currentValue.HasValue && currentValue != int.MaxValue)
				{
					newValue = currentValue.Value + num;
					if (UpperLimit.HasValue && (double)newValue.Value > UpperLimit.Value)
					{
						newValue = Convert.ToInt32(UpperLimit.Value);
					}
					else if (MaxLength < 16 && newValue.ToString().Length > MaxLength)
					{
						newValue = currentValue;
					}
				}
				else if (!currentValue.HasValue)
				{
					newValue = ((!LowerLimit.HasValue || LowerLimit.Value == -2147483648.0 || LowerLimit.Value == -2147483647.0 || LowerLimit.Value == double.MinValue) ? new int?(2) : ((UpperLimit.HasValue && UpperLimit.Value == LowerLimit.Value) ? new int?(Convert.ToInt32(LowerLimit.Value)) : ((!(LowerLimit.Value < 0.0)) ? new int?(Convert.ToInt32(LowerLimit.Value) + 1) : new int?(1))));
				}
				SetNewValueWhenSpin(currentValue, newValue);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static void OnSpinDownCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AesXamValueEdit control = sender as AesXamValueEdit;
			if (control == null || control.IsReadOnly)
			{
				return;
			}
			Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
			{
				if (!control.IsInEditMode)
				{
					control.StartEditMode();
				}
				control.OnSpinDown();
			});
		}

		public virtual bool OnSpinDown()
		{
			bool result = false;
			int? currentValue = null;
			if (IsSpinAvailable(out currentValue))
			{
				result = true;
				int num = 1;
				if (SpinStepSize != null)
				{
					num = (int)SpinStepSize;
				}
				int? newValue = currentValue;
				if (currentValue.HasValue && currentValue.Value != int.MinValue)
				{
					newValue = currentValue.Value - num;
					if (LowerLimit.HasValue && (double)newValue.Value < LowerLimit.Value)
					{
						newValue = Convert.ToInt32(LowerLimit.Value);
					}
				}
				else if (!currentValue.HasValue)
				{
					newValue = ((!LowerLimit.HasValue || LowerLimit.Value == -2147483648.0 || LowerLimit.Value == -2147483647.0 || LowerLimit.Value == double.MinValue) ? new int?(1) : ((UpperLimit.HasValue && UpperLimit.Value == LowerLimit.Value) ? new int?(Convert.ToInt32(LowerLimit.Value)) : ((!UpperLimit.HasValue || !(UpperLimit.Value > 0.0) || !LowerLimit.HasValue || !(LowerLimit.Value <= -1.0)) ? new int?(Convert.ToInt32(LowerLimit.Value)) : new int?(-1))));
				}
				SetNewValueWhenSpin(currentValue, newValue);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static void Copy_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				aesXamValueEdit.OnCopy();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void Copy_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				e.CanExecute = aesXamValueEdit.CanCopy();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnCopy()
		{
			if (base.Value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = null;
			if (base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox != null)
				{
					text = VisiableTextBox.SelectedText;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text = base.Text;
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage || (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && base.ValueType == typeof(string)))
				{
					text = text.Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol());
				}
			}
			if (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(this, typeof(AesVariableControlBase), allowSubclassOfType: true) is AesVariableControlBase obj)
			{
				ClipboardHelper.CreateClipboard(new Collection<string>(new string[1] { DataForClipboard.GetCxsItemMoniker(obj) }), text, DataForClipboard.GetCaseFullFilePath(obj), DataForClipboard.GetApplicationName(obj));
			}
			else
			{
				ClipboardWrapper.SetText(text);
			}
		}

		private bool CanCopy()
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(VisiableTextBox.SelectedText);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Text))
			{
				return base.IsEnabled;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static void Paste_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				aesXamValueEdit.OnPaste(e.Parameter);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void Paste_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				e.CanExecute = aesXamValueEdit.CanPaste(e.Parameter);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnPaste(object parameter)
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode && VisiableTextBox != null)
			{
				int num = VisiableTextBox.SelectionStart + pasteStringLength;
				base.Text = stringToPaste;
				if (stringToPaste.Length >= num)
				{
					VisiableTextBox.Select(num, 0);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.Dispatcher.Invoke(delegate
				{
					StartEditMode();
					base.Text = stringToPaste;
					EndEditMode(acceptChanges: true, force: true);
				});
			}
		}

		private bool CanPaste(object parameter)
		{
			if (base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (parameter == null)
			{
				try
				{
					stringToPaste = null;
					if (ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("AP_Text") && ClipboardWrapper.GetData("AP_Text") is MemoryStream stream)
					{
						StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(stream);
						stringToPaste = streamReader.ReadToEnd()?.Replace("\r", string.Empty).Replace("\n", string.Empty);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringToPaste))
					{
						stringToPaste = ClipboardWrapper.GetText().Replace("\r", string.Empty).Replace("\n", string.Empty);
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			else
			{
				stringToPaste = parameter.ToString();
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stringToPaste))
			{
				return false;
			}
			pasteStringLength = stringToPaste.Length;
			if (base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				stringToPaste = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(VisiableTextBox, stringToPaste);
				if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && double.TryParse(stringToPaste, out var result))
				{
					stringToPaste = result.ToString();
					if (stringToPaste.Length > MaxLength && stringToPaste.Contains("E"))
					{
						int num = stringToPaste.Length - MaxLength;
						int num2 = stringToPaste.IndexOf('E');
						if (num2 - num > 0)
						{
							stringToPaste = stringToPaste.Substring(0, num2 - num) + stringToPaste.Substring(num2, stringToPaste.Length - num2);
						}
					}
				}
				return MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextMaskable(stringToPaste, MaxLength, Mask);
			}
			if (base.IsReadOnly || !base.IsEnabled || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stringToPaste))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double && double.TryParse(stringToPaste, out var result2))
			{
				stringToPaste = result2.ToString();
				if (stringToPaste.Length > MaxLength && stringToPaste.Contains("E"))
				{
					int num3 = stringToPaste.Length - MaxLength;
					int num4 = stringToPaste.IndexOf('E');
					if (num4 - num3 > 0)
					{
						stringToPaste = stringToPaste.Substring(0, num4 - num3) + stringToPaste.Substring(num4, stringToPaste.Length - num4);
					}
				}
			}
			if (MaxLength != 0 && stringToPaste.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				stringToPaste = stringToPaste.Substring(0, MaxLength);
			}
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
			{
				if (!stringToPaste.EndsWith("%"))
				{
					_ = stringToPaste;
				}
				else
				{
					stringToPaste.Substring(0, stringToPaste.Length - 1);
				}
				if (double.TryParse(stringToPaste, out var result3))
				{
					stringToPaste = (stringToPaste.EndsWith("%") ? (result3 + "%") : result3.ToString());
				}
			}
			return MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(stringToPaste, MaxLength, Mask);
		}

		private static void Cut_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				aesXamValueEdit.OnCut();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void Cut_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit)
			{
				e.CanExecute = aesXamValueEdit.CanCut();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnCut()
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode && VisiableTextBox != null)
			{
				int selectionStart = VisiableTextBox.SelectionStart;
				base.Text = stringAfterCut;
				VisiableTextBox.Select(selectionStart, 0);
				ClipboardWrapper.SetText(stringToCut);
			}
		}

		private bool CanCut()
		{
			if (base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (base.IsInEditMode)
			{
				if (VisiableTextBox == null || VisiableTextBox.SelectionLength == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				stringAfterCut = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetPreviewInputStringWhenDeleteText(VisiableTextBox, Key.Delete);
				stringToCut = VisiableTextBox.SelectedText;
				return MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextMaskable(stringAfterCut, MaxLength, Mask);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void SetNewValueWhenSpin(int? curValue, int? newValue)
		{
			if (newValue == curValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				if (newValue.HasValue)
				{
					VisiableTextBox.Text = newValue.Value.ToString();
				}
				else
				{
					VisiableTextBox.Text = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.Value = newValue;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsInEditMode && VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				_textBoxContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (!base.IsEmbedded && !IsInScrollArea)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
				if (Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(this, typeof(AesVariableControlBase), allowSubclassOfType: true) is AesVariableControlBase aesVariableControlBase)
				{
					aesVariableControlBase.InitContextMenu(null);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeStarted(EditModeStartedEventArgs args)
		{
			base.OnEditModeStarted(args);
			if (base.ValueType == typeof(double) && base.Text.Length >= 7)
			{
				string text = ((double)base.Value).ToString(base.Format);
				if (double.Parse(text) == (double)base.Value)
				{
					base.Text = text;
				}
				else
				{
					int length = base.Text.Length;
					int num = 0;
					num = length - 1;
					while (num > -1 && (base.Text[num] == '0' || base.Text[num] == MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol()[0]))
					{
						num--;
					}
					base.Text = ((double)base.Value).ToString("g" + (num + 1));
				}
			}
			if (xamMaskedEditor != null)
			{
				if (xamMaskedEditor.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					xamMaskedEditor.Focus();
				}
				else if (VisiableTextBox != null && VisiableTextBox.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					VisiableTextBox.Focus();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool IsSpinAvailable(out int? currentValue)
		{
			Type valueTypeResolved = ValueTypeResolved;
			if (valueTypeResolved == typeof(int) || valueTypeResolved == typeof(int?))
			{
				if (base.Value == null)
				{
					currentValue = null;
				}
				else if (base.Value.GetType() == typeof(string))
				{
					currentValue = Convert.ToInt32(base.Value);
				}
				else
				{
					currentValue = ((base.Value == null) ? ((int?)null) : ((int?)base.Value));
				}
				return true;
			}
			if (valueTypeResolved == typeof(string) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Value as string))
			{
				currentValue = null;
				return true;
			}
			if (base.Value != null)
			{
				int result = 0;
				if (int.TryParse(base.Value.ToString(), out result))
				{
					currentValue = result;
					return true;
				}
			}
			currentValue = null;
			return false;
		}

		private void UpdateBaseMask()
		{
			if (!base.IsEnabled || base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				ClearValue(XamMaskedEditor.MaskProperty);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Percentage)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength < 16) ? 16 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Subroutine)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 16 : MaxLength);
				string text = "{char:" + MaxLength + ":-_a-zA-Z0-9}";
				if (CharacterCasing == CharacterCasing.Upper)
				{
					text = ">" + text;
				}
				InnerMask = text;
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranVariable)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 8 : MaxLength);
				string text2 = ">";
				for (int i = 0; i < MaxLength; i++)
				{
					text2 += "a";
				}
				InnerMask = text2;
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentId)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 8 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentName)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 10 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Id)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 8 : MaxLength);
				string text3 = ">";
				text3 = text3 + "{char:1:a-zA-Z0-9}{char:" + (MaxLength - 1) + ":a-zA-Z0-9\\,\\$\\+\\-\\:}";
				InnerMask = text3;
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Double)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength < 16) ? 16 : MaxLength);
				if (base.ValueConstraint != null)
				{
					base.ValueConstraint.MaxLength = 0;
				}
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.Integer)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 16 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (Mask == AesMaskOption.FortranExpression)
			{
				MaxLength = ((MaxLength <= 0) ? 20 : MaxLength);
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(InnerMask))
			{
				InnerMask = null;
			}
		}

		private static void OnMaxLengthPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AesXamValueEdit).OnMaxLengthChanged((int)e.OldValue, (int)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static object OnMaxLengthPropertyCoerced(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit = d as AesXamValueEdit;
			int? num = value as int?;
			if (num.HasValue && aesXamValueEdit.SpinVisibility != Visibility.Visible)
			{
				return Math.Max(0, num.Value);
			}
			if (aesXamValueEdit.SpinVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				if (num.HasValue && num.Value > 0 && num.Value < 10)
				{
					return num.Value;
				}
				return 10;
			}
			return 0;
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(properties);
		}

		protected static void GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			if (properties != null)
			{
				properties.Add(AutoBindProperty);
				properties.Add(VariableDataMemberProperty);
				properties.Add(ValueUpdateTriggerProperty);
			}
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(items);
		}

		protected static void GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			items?.Add(new VariablePathToVariableBindingItem(VariablePathProperty, VariableProperty));
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
		}

		protected void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				string text = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(VariableDataMember) ? "Value" : VariableDataMember);
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, ValueEditor.ValueTypeProperty, text + "Type")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
					IgnoreLocalValue = true
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, ValueEditor.ValueProperty, text)
				{
					Trigger = ValueUpdateTrigger
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, "IsVisible")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
					Converter = new AesControlVisibilityConverter()
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, UpperLimitProperty, UpperLimitProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, LowerLimitProperty, LowerLimitProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeStarting(EditModeStartingEventArgs args)
		{
			base.OnEditModeStarting(args);
			args.Cancel = false;
			ValuedChangedInEdit = false;
		}

		protected override void OnEditModeEnded(EditModeEndedEventArgs args)
		{
			base.OnEditModeEnded(args);
			ValuedChangedInEdit = false;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewDragOver(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			string draggingString = GetDraggingString(e.Data);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(draggingString) && !base.IsReadOnly && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(draggingString, MaxLength, Mask))
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Copy;
			}
			else
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.None;
			}
			e.Handled = true;
			base.OnPreviewDragOver(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewDragEnter(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.None;
			}
			base.OnPreviewDragEnter(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewDrop(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsMMEnabled && !base.IsReadOnly)
			{
				string draggingText = GetDraggingString(e.Data);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(draggingText) && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.CanTextValiable(draggingText, MaxLength, Mask))
				{
					if (!base.IsFocused && !base.IsFocusWithin)
					{
						Focus();
					}
					base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
					{
						StartEditMode();
						base.Text = draggingText;
						if (base.IsInEditMode)
						{
							EndEditMode(acceptChanges: true, force: true);
						}
					});
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
			base.OnPreviewDrop(e);
		}

		private static string GetDraggingString(IDataObject dataObject)
		{
			if (dataObject != null)
			{
				string text = null;
				if (dataObject.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.UnicodeText))
				{
					text = dataObject.GetData(DataFormats.UnicodeText) as string;
				}
				if (text == null && dataObject.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.Text))
				{
					text = dataObject.GetData(DataFormats.Text) as string;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					int num = text.IndexOf('\t');
					if (num > 0)
					{
						text = text.Substring(0, num);
					}
					int num2 = text.IndexOf(Environment.NewLine);
					if (num2 > 0)
					{
						text = text.Substring(0, num2);
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
					{
						return text;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		{
			Helper.DoMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseMove(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}
	}

	public class AesXamCheckEdit : XamCheckEditor, IAutoBindControl, IPromptControl
	{
		private readonly AutoBindControlHelper _autoBindControlHelper;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AutoBindProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VariablePathProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VariableProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VariableDataMemberProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PromptProperty;

		public bool AutoBind
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AutoBindProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AutoBindProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Localizability(LocalizationCategory.NeverLocalize)]
		public string VariablePath
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(VariablePathProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VariablePathProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object Variable
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(VariableProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VariableProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string VariableDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(VariableDataMemberProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VariableDataMemberProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string Prompt
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(PromptProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PromptProperty, value);
			}
		}

		bool IAutoBindControl.AutoBind => AutoBind;

		static AesXamCheckEdit()
		{
			AutoBindProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoBind", typeof(bool), typeof(AesXamCheckEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			VariablePathProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariablePath", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamCheckEdit));
			VariableProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Variable", typeof(object), typeof(AesXamCheckEdit));
			VariableDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VariableDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamCheckEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("SelectedItem"));
			PromptProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Prompt", typeof(string), typeof(AesXamCheckEdit));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AesXamCheckEdit), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(AesXamCheckEdit)));
		}

		public AesXamCheckEdit()
		{
			_autoBindControlHelper = new AutoBindControlHelper(this);
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty, VariableProperty, AesVariableControlBase.CreateDefaultIsMMEnabledBinding(this, null)));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(PromptProperty, VariableProperty, AesVariableControlBase.CreatePromptBinding(this)));
			_autoBindControlHelper.ExtraAutoBindingItems.Add(new ExtraAutoBindingItem(ValueEditor.IsReadOnlyProperty, VariableProperty, new Binding("Variable.IsReadOnly")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = false
			}));
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_autoBindControlHelper.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (_autoBindControlHelper.IsInAutoBinding(dp))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
			base.OnPreviewGotKeyboardFocus(e);
		}

		public void RaisePromptDisplayed()
		{
			OnPromptDisplayed(Prompt);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPromptDisplayed(string prompt)
		{
			RoutedEventArgs<string> e = new RoutedEventArgs<string>(prompt, AesControlBase.PromptDisplayedEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(properties);
		}

		protected static void GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties)
		{
			if (properties != null)
			{
				properties.Add(AutoBindProperty);
				properties.Add(VariableDataMemberProperty);
			}
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(items);
		}

		protected static void GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items)
		{
			items?.Add(new VariablePathToVariableBindingItem(VariablePathProperty, VariableProperty));
		}

		void IAutoBindControl.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(items);
		}

		protected void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, ValueEditor.ValueProperty, string.IsNullOrEmpty(VariableDataMember) ? "Value" : VariableDataMember));
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, "IsVisible")
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
					Converter = new AesControlVisibilityConverter()
				});
				items.Add(new VariableToValueBindingItem(VariableProperty, PromptProperty, PromptProperty.Name)
				{
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public static class ClipboardWrapper
	{
		private const int tryTime = 2;

		private const int tryInterval = 0;

		public static void Clear()
		{
			for (int i = 1; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					Clipboard.Clear();
					break;
				}
				catch
				{
					if (i < 2)
					{
						Thread.Sleep(0);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public static bool ContainsText()
		{
			bool result = false;
			for (int i = 1; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					result = Clipboard.ContainsText();
				}
				catch
				{
					if (i < 2)
					{
						Thread.Sleep(0);
					}
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

	public static class ClipboardHelper
	{
		public const string AsiFileFormat = "ASI File Format";

		public const string AsiFormat = "ASI Format";

		public const string CsvFormat = "CSV";

		public const string DatagridMetadata = "DatagridMetadata";

		public static DataObject GetDataObject(List<IGridCellData> cellInfo, string value, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			ClipboardWrapper.Clear();
			DataObject dataObject = new DataObject();
			MemoryStream asiFileXml = GetAsiFileXml(fullCasePath, applicationName);
			if (asiFileXml != null)
			{
				dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI File Format").Name, asiFileXml);
			}
			MemoryStream asiSelectionXmlGridFormatStream = GetAsiSelectionXmlGridFormatStream(cellInfo, fullCasePath, applicationName);
			if (asiSelectionXmlGridFormatStream != null)
			{
				dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI Format").Name, asiSelectionXmlGridFormatStream);
			}
			dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.Text, (value != null) ? value : string.Empty);
			return dataObject;
		}

		public static void CreateClipboard(List<IGridCellData> cellInfo, string value, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			ClipboardWrapper.SetDataObject(GetDataObject(cellInfo, value, fullCasePath, applicationName), copy: true);
		}

		public static DataObject GetDataObject(ICollection<string> monikers, string value, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			ClipboardWrapper.Clear();
			DataObject dataObject = new DataObject();
			MemoryStream asiFileXml = GetAsiFileXml(fullCasePath, applicationName);
			if (asiFileXml != null)
			{
				dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI File Format").Name, asiFileXml);
			}
			MemoryStream asiSelectionXmlStream = GetAsiSelectionXmlStream(monikers, fullCasePath, applicationName);
			if (asiSelectionXmlStream != null)
			{
				dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI Format").Name, asiSelectionXmlStream);
			}
			dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.Text, (value != null) ? value : string.Empty);
			return dataObject;
		}

		public static void CreateClipboard(ICollection<string> monikers, string value, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			ClipboardWrapper.SetDataObject(GetDataObject(monikers, value, fullCasePath, applicationName), copy: true);
		}

		public static MemoryStream GetAsiFileXml(string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
			{
				return null;
			}
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.OmitXmlDeclaration = true;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			using (XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder, xmlWriterSettings))
			{
				xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
				xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("SelectionFile");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(applicationName))
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Product", applicationName);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteElementString("FileMoniker", fullCasePath);
				}
				xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
				xmlWriter.WriteEndDocument();
				xmlWriter.Flush();
			}
			return new MemoryStream(new UTF8Encoding().GetBytes(stringBuilder.ToString()));
		}

		public static MemoryStream GetAsiSelectionXmlGridFormatStream(List<IGridCellData> cellInfo, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			return new MemoryStream(new UTF8Encoding().GetBytes(GetAsiSelectionGridXml(cellInfo, fullCasePath, applicationName)));
		}

		public static string GetAsiSelectionGridXml(List<IGridCellData> cellInfo, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			if ((cellInfo == null || cellInfo.Count == 0) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
			{
				return null;
			}
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.OmitXmlDeclaration = true;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			using (XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder, xmlWriterSettings))
			{
				xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
				xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Selection");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteElementString("FileMoniker", fullCasePath);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(applicationName))
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Product", applicationName);
				}
				xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Format", "Grid");
				if (cellInfo != null && cellInfo.Count > 0)
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Items");
					foreach (IGridCellData item in cellInfo)
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.Moniker))
						{
							xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Text");
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Value", item.TextValue);
						}
						else
						{
							xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Item");
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Path", item.Moniker);
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Value", item.TextValue);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.Name))
						{
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Name", item.Name);
						}
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Row", item.RowIndex.ToString());
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Col", item.ColumnIndex.ToString());
						xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
					}
					xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
				}
				xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
				xmlWriter.WriteEndDocument();
				xmlWriter.Flush();
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static MemoryStream GetAsiSelectionXmlStream(ICollection<string> monikers, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			return new MemoryStream(new UTF8Encoding().GetBytes(GetAsiSelectionXml(monikers, fullCasePath, applicationName)));
		}

		public static string GetAsiSelectionXml(ICollection<string> monikers, string fullCasePath, string applicationName)
		{
			if ((monikers == null || monikers.Count == 0) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
			{
				return null;
			}
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.OmitXmlDeclaration = true;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			using (XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder, xmlWriterSettings))
			{
				xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
				xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Selection");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullCasePath))
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteElementString("FileMoniker", fullCasePath);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(applicationName))
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Product", applicationName);
				}
				if (monikers != null && monikers.Count > 0)
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Items");
					foreach (string moniker in monikers)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(moniker))
						{
							xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Item");
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Path", moniker);
							xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
						}
					}
					xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
				}
				xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
				xmlWriter.WriteEndDocument();
				xmlWriter.Flush();
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string GetAsiFormatTextFromClipboard()
		{
			if (Clipboard.ContainsData("ASI Format"))
			{
				object obj = null;
				try
				{
					obj = Clipboard.GetData("ASI Format");
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				if (obj is string)
				{
					return obj as string;
				}
				if (obj is Stream)
				{
					return new StreamReader(obj as Stream).ReadToEnd();
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string GetAsiFormatTextFromDataObject(IDataObject dObject)
		{
			if (dObject == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				obj = dObject.GetData("ASI Format");
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (obj is string)
			{
				return obj as string;
			}
			if (obj is Stream)
			{
				return new StreamReader(obj as Stream).ReadToEnd();
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static IList<string> ParseCxsPathFromAsiFormat(string asiFormatText, string baseCaseFilePath, int maxPaths = 1000000)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(asiFormatText))
			{
				try
				{
					asiFormatText = asiFormatText.TrimEnd(default(char));
					XDocument xDocument = XDocument.Parse(asiFormatText);
					if (xDocument != null)
					{
						XElement xElement = xDocument.Root.Element("FileMoniker");
						if (xElement != null && string.Equals(xElement.Value, baseCaseFilePath, StringComparison.CurrentCulture))
						{
							List<string> list = new List<string>();
							IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable = xDocument.Root.XPathSelectElements("Items/Item");
							if (enumerable != null)
							{
								foreach (XElement item in enumerable)
								{
									XElement xElement2 = item.XPathSelectElement("Path");
									if (xElement2 != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(xElement2.Value) && !list.Contains(xElement2.Value))
									{
										list.Add(xElement2.Value);
										if (list.Count == maxPaths)
										{
											return list;
										}
									}
								}
							}
							return list;
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	public class DataDraggingAnchor : Control, INotifyDraggingStarted
	{
		public static readonly RoutedEvent DataDraggingStartedEvent;

		private CommonDraggingHelper _helper;

		private CommonDraggingHelper Helper
		{
			get
			{
				if (_helper == null)
				{
					_helper = new CommonDraggingHelper(this)
					{
						CaptureMouseWhenMouseDown = true
					};
				}
				return _helper;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs> DataDraggingStarted
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(DataDraggingStartedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(DataDraggingStartedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static DataDraggingAnchor()
		{
			DataDraggingStartedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DataDraggingStarted", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs>), typeof(DataDraggingAnchor));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataDraggingAnchor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(DataDraggingAnchor)));
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseMove(e);
			base.OnMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Helper.DoMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataDraggingStarted(DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs e)
		{
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected virtual DependencyObject GetStartingTargetToCollectDraggingData()
		{
			return VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(this);
		}

		void INotifyDraggingStarted.OnDataDraggingStarted(DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs e)
		{
			e.RoutedEvent = DataDraggingStartedEvent;
			e.Source = this;
			OnDataDraggingStarted(e);
		}
	}
	internal class CommonDraggingHelper
	{
		private const double ValidInstance = 290.0;

		private readonly IInputElement _element;

		private Point? _mouseDownPoint;

		public bool CaptureMouseWhenMouseDown { get; set; }

		public CommonDraggingHelper(IInputElement element)
		{
			_element = element;
		}

		public void DoMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsDragReady(_element))
			{
				_mouseDownPoint = e.GetPosition(_element);
				if (CaptureMouseWhenMouseDown)
				{
					_element.CaptureMouse();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_mouseDownPoint = null;
			}
		}

		public void DoMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_mouseDownPoint.HasValue && e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed && (e.GetPosition(_element) - _mouseDownPoint.Value).Length >= 290.0)
			{
				_mouseDownPoint = null;
				if (CaptureMouseWhenMouseDown && _element.IsMouseCaptured)
				{
					_element.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
				StartDataDragging();
			}
		}

		public void DoMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_mouseDownPoint.HasValue)
			{
				_mouseDownPoint = null;
				if (CaptureMouseWhenMouseDown && _element.IsMouseCaptured)
				{
					_element.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
			}
		}

		private void StartDataDragging()
		{
			IDataObject dataObject = GenerateDraggingData();
			if (_element is INotifyDraggingStarted notifyDraggingStarted)
			{
				DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs e = new DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs(dataObject);
				notifyDraggingStarted.OnDataDraggingStarted(e);
				if (!e.Cancel)
				{
					DragDrop.DoDragDrop(e.DragSource ?? (_element as DependencyObject), dataObject, DragDropEffects.All);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DragDrop.DoDragDrop(_element as DependencyObject, dataObject, DragDropEffects.All);
			}
		}

		private IDataObject GenerateDraggingData()
		{
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			string text3 = null;
			string text4 = null;
			for (DependencyObject dependencyObject = _element as DependencyObject; dependencyObject != null; dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject))
			{
				if (text4 == null)
				{
					text4 = GetPlainTextToDrag(dependencyObject);
				}
				if (dependencyObject is IDraggingDataProvider draggingDataProvider)
				{
					if (text == null)
					{
						text = draggingDataProvider.CaseFullFilePath;
					}
					if (text2 == null)
					{
						text2 = draggingDataProvider.ApplicationName;
					}
					if (text3 == null)
					{
						text3 = draggingDataProvider.CxsMoniker;
					}
					if (text != null || text2 != null)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			DataObject dataObject = new DataObject();
			MemoryStream asiFileXml = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiFileXml(text, text2);
			if (asiFileXml != null)
			{
				dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI File Format").Name, asiFileXml);
			}
			string asiSelectionXml = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiSelectionXml(new Collection<string>(new string[1] { text3 }), text, text2);
			if (asiSelectionXml != null)
			{
				dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat("ASI Format").Name, asiSelectionXml);
			}
			dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.Text, text4 ?? string.Empty);
			return dataObject;
		}

		private static bool IsDragReady(object control)
		{
			if (control is DataDraggingAnchor)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (GetSelectedLength(control) > 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (control is AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit)
			{
				return new Rect(aesXamComboEdit.RenderSize).Contains(Mouse.GetPosition(aesXamComboEdit));
			}
			if (control is ValueEditor)
			{
				return !((ValueEditor)control).IsInEditMode;
			}
			if (control is TextBox)
			{
				return ((TextBox)control).IsReadOnly;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static int GetSelectedLength(object control)
		{
			if (control is AesXamComboEdit)
			{
				return ((AesXamComboEdit)control).SelectionLength;
			}
			if (control is AesXamValueEdit)
			{
				return ((AesXamValueEdit)control).SelectionLength;
			}
			if (control is TextBox)
			{
				return ((TextBox)control).SelectionLength;
			}
			return 0;
		}

		private static string GetPlainTextToDrag(object control)
		{
			if (control is AesXamComboEdit)
			{
				AesXamComboEdit aesXamComboEdit = control as AesXamComboEdit;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(aesXamComboEdit.SelectedText))
				{
					return aesXamComboEdit.SelectedText;
				}
				return aesXamComboEdit.Text;
			}
			if (control is AesXamValueEdit)
			{
				AesXamValueEdit aesXamValueEdit = control as AesXamValueEdit;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(aesXamValueEdit.SelectedText))
				{
					return aesXamValueEdit.SelectedText;
				}
				return aesXamValueEdit.Text;
			}
			if (control is ValueEditor)
			{
				return ((ValueEditor)control).Text;
			}
			if (control is TextBox)
			{
				TextBox textBox = control as TextBox;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textBox.SelectedText))
				{
					return textBox.SelectedText;
				}
				return textBox.Text;
			}
			if (control is AesVariableControlBase)
			{
				return ((AesVariableControlBase)control).TextForCopy;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public interface INotifyDraggingStarted
	{
		void OnDataDraggingStarted(DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs e);
	}
	public class DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public IDataObject DataObject { get; private set; }

		public bool Cancel { get; set; }

		public DependencyObject DragSource { get; set; }

		public DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs(IDataObject dataObject)
		{
			if (dataObject == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataObject");
			}
			DataObject = dataObject;
		}

		public DataDraggingAnchorEventArgs(IDataObject dataObject, RoutedEvent routedEvent, object source)
			: base(routedEvent, source)
		{
			if (dataObject == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataObject");
			}
			DataObject = dataObject;
		}
	}

		}

	public interface IAesControlContainer
