	public class AesPerformanceTotal : IDisposable
	{
		private static int _numRunning;

		private static AesPerformancTimer totalTimer;

		private AesPerformancTimer tm;

		private static int _voidCount;

		private static AesPerformancTimer _voidTimer;

		private static AesPerformancTimer _spanTimer;

		private static bool _spanStarted;

		public static bool Running
		{
			get
			{
				return AesPerformancTimer.Running;
			}
			set
			{
				AesPerformancTimer.Running = value;
			}
		}

		public static string Logfile
		{
			get
			{
				return AesPerformancTimer.Logfile;
			}
			set
			{
				AesPerformancTimer.Logfile = value;
			}
		}

		public int TraceStart
		{
			set
			{
				if (AesPerformancTimer.Running)
				{
					tm.TraceStart = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public int TraceEnd
		{
			set
			{
				if (AesPerformancTimer.Running)
				{
					tm.TraceEnd = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public static void Report()
		{
			AesPerformancTimer.Report();
		}

		public AesPerformanceTotal(string name)
		{
			if (!AesPerformancTimer.Running)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!_spanStarted)
			{
				_spanTimer = new AesPerformancTimer("Total Span");
				_spanStarted = true;
			}
			string text;
			if (_numRunning == 0)
			{
				text = ",top";
				totalTimer = new AesPerformancTimer("Total");
				if (_voidTimer != null)
				{
					_voidTimer.Stop();
					_voidTimer = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text = "";
			}
			tm = new AesPerformancTimer(name + text);
			_numRunning++;
		}

		public static void StopSpan()
		{
			_voidTimer.Stop();
			_spanTimer.Stop();
		}

		public void Stop()
		{
			if (AesPerformancTimer.Running)
			{
				if (tm != null)
				{
					tm.Stop();
				}
				_numRunning--;
				if (_numRunning == 0)
				{
					totalTimer.Stop();
					totalTimer = null;
					_voidTimer = new AesPerformancTimer("voidtimer,top");
					_voidCount++;
				}
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Stop();
		}
	}
	public class AesPerformancTimer : IDisposable
	{
		protected class TraceElement
		{
			public string Name { get; set; }

			public TimeSpan Time { get; set; }

			public bool Start { get; set; }

			public double TimeSpent { get; set; }

			public TraceElement(string n, TimeSpan t, bool s)
			{
				Name = n;
				Time = t;
				Start = s;
			}
		}

		private static bool _running;

		private static int _tracing = 0;

		private static Stack<TraceElement> _traceStack = new Stack<TraceElement>();

		private static Dictionary<string, double> elapsed = new Dictionary<string, double>();

		private static Dictionary<string, int> hits = new Dictionary<string, int>();

		private static Stopwatch sw = null;

		private TimeSpan? _start;

		private string _section = "unspecificed";

		public int TraceStart
		{
			set
			{
				if (hits[_section] == value)
				{
					Tracing = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public int TraceEnd
		{
			set
			{
				if (hits[_section] == value)
				{
					Tracing = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public static string Logfile { get; set; }

		public static bool Tracing
		{
			get
			{
				return _tracing > 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					_tracing++;
					if (_tracing == 1)
					{
						_traceStack.Push(new TraceElement("*************TRACE ON **************", sw.Elapsed, s: true));
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_tracing--;
					if (_tracing == 0)
					{
						_traceStack.Push(new TraceElement("*************TRACE OFF**************", sw.Elapsed, s: false));
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public static bool Running
		{
			get
			{
				return _running;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _running)
				{
					if (sw == null)
					{
						sw = new Stopwatch();
						sw.Start();
					}
					if (!value)
					{
						AesPerformanceTotal.StopSpan();
					}
					_running = value;
					if (_running)
					{
						new AesPerformanceTotal("fake starter").Stop();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public static void Reset()
		{
			elapsed.Clear();
			hits.Clear();
			_traceStack.Clear();
		}

		public static void Report()
		{
			string text = ((Logfile == null) ? "CxsPerformancTimer.log" : Logfile);
			TextWriter textWriter = null;
			try
			{
				textWriter = new StreamWriter(text);
				textWriter.WriteLine($"Performace Timer log: {DateTime.Now.ToLongDateString()} {DateTime.Now.ToLongTimeString()}");
				foreach (string key in elapsed.Keys)
				{
					if (hits.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						textWriter.WriteLine($"{key}: hits {hits[key].ToString()} total {elapsed[key].ToString()}");
					}
				}
				if (_traceStack.Count > 2)
				{
					string text2 = "     ";
					foreach (TraceElement item in _traceStack.Reverse())
					{
						if (!item.Start)
						{
							text2 = text2.Substring(4);
						}
						string text3 = "          ";
						text3 = text3.Substring(text3.Length + item.Time.TotalMilliseconds.ToString().Length - 9);
						textWriter.WriteLine(item.Time.TotalMilliseconds + text3 + text2 + item.Name + " " + (item.Start ? "" : item.TimeSpent.ToString()));
						if (item.Start)
						{
							text2 += "    ";
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (textWriter != null)
				{
					textWriter.Flush();
					textWriter.Close();
				}
			}
			text.ToLower();
			text = ((!text.Contains(".log")) ? "CxsPerformancTimer.csv" : text.Replace(".log", ".csv"));
			textWriter = null;
			try
			{
				textWriter = new StreamWriter(text);
				textWriter.WriteLine($",,,,,{DateTime.Now.ToLongDateString()},{DateTime.Now.ToLongTimeString()}");
				textWriter.WriteLine(",Section,top?,Hits,Total Time");
				foreach (string key2 in elapsed.Keys)
				{
					if (hits.ContainsKey(key2))
					{
						string text4 = key2;
						if (!text4.EndsWith(",top"))
						{
							text4 += ",";
						}
						textWriter.WriteLine($",{text4},{hits[key2].ToString()},{elapsed[key2].ToString()}");
					}
				}
				if (_traceStack.Count <= 2)
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (TraceElement item2 in _traceStack.Reverse())
				{
					string text5 = item2.Name;
					if (!text5.EndsWith(",top"))
					{
						text5 = "  " + text5 + ",";
					}
					textWriter.WriteLine(item2.Time.TotalMilliseconds + "," + text5 + "," + (item2.Start ? "" : ((text5.StartsWith("voidtimer") ? "0.0," : "") + item2.TimeSpent)));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (textWriter != null)
				{
					textWriter.Flush();
					textWriter.Close();
				}
			}
		}

		public AesPerformancTimer(string section)
		{
			if (Running)
			{
				if (section != null)
				{
					_section = section.ToLower();
				}
				if (!elapsed.ContainsKey(_section))
				{
					elapsed.Add(_section, 0.0);
				}
				if (!hits.ContainsKey(_section))
				{
					hits.Add(_section, 0);
				}
				hits[_section] += 1;
				_start = sw.Elapsed;
				if (Tracing && string.Compare(_section, "total") != 0)
				{
					_traceStack.Push(new TraceElement(_section, _start.Value, s: true));
				}
			}
		}

		public void Stop()
		{
			if (Running && _start.HasValue)
			{
				TimeSpan timeSpan = sw.Elapsed;
				TimeSpan value = timeSpan;
				TimeSpan? start = _start;
				double totalMilliseconds = (value - start).Value.TotalMilliseconds;
				elapsed[_section] += totalMilliseconds;
				_start = null;
				if (Tracing && string.Compare(_section, "total") != 0)
				{
					_traceStack.Push(new TraceElement(_section, timeSpan, s: false)
					{
						TimeSpent = totalMilliseconds
					});
				}
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Stop();
		}
	}
	public interface IAutoBindControl
	{
		bool AutoBind { get; }

		void GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(IList<DependencyProperty> properties);

		void GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(IList<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> items);

		void GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(IList<VariableToValueBindingItem> items);
	}
	public class AutoBindControlHelper
	{
		private readonly IAutoBindControl _control;

		private readonly UIElement _element;

		private readonly List<DependencyProperty> _updateProperties = new List<DependencyProperty>();

		private readonly Dictionary<DependencyProperty, BindingBase> _bindings = new Dictionary<DependencyProperty, BindingBase>();

		private readonly List<ExtraAutoBindingItem> _extraAutoBindingItems = new List<ExtraAutoBindingItem>();

		public IList<ExtraAutoBindingItem> ExtraAutoBindingItems => _extraAutoBindingItems;

		public AutoBindControlHelper(IAutoBindControl control)
		{
			if (control == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("control");
			}
			if (!typeof(UIElement).IsAssignableFrom(control.GetType()))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("control should be an UIElement");
			}
			_control = control;
			_element = control as UIElement;
			_control.GetPropertiesToUpdateAutoBindings(_updateProperties);
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_updateProperties.Contains(e.Property))
			{
				UpdateAutoBindings();
				return;
			}
			foreach (ExtraAutoBindingItem extraAutoBindingItem in ExtraAutoBindingItems)
			{
				if (extraAutoBindingItem.SourceProperties.Contains(e.Property))
				{
					UpdateExtraAutoBindings(extraAutoBindingItem);
				}
			}
			List<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> list = GetVariablePathToVariableBindingItems().FindAll((VariablePathToVariableBindingItem x) => x.VariablePathProperty == e.Property);
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (VariablePathToVariableBindingItem item in list)
				{
					UpdateVariablePathToVariableBindings(item.VariableProperty, e.OldValue as string, e.NewValue as string);
				}
				return;
			}
			List<VariableToValueBindingItem> list2 = GetVariableToValueBindingItems().FindAll((VariableToValueBindingItem x) => x.VariableProperty == e.Property);
			if (list2.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (VariableToValueBindingItem item2 in list2)
			{
				UpdateVariableToValueBindings(item2);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateAutoBindings()
		{
			List<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> variablePathToVariableBindingItems = GetVariablePathToVariableBindingItems();
			List<VariableToValueBindingItem> variableToValueBindingItems = GetVariableToValueBindingItems();
			foreach (VariablePathToVariableBindingItem item in variablePathToVariableBindingItems)
			{
				string text = _element.GetValue(item.VariablePathProperty) as string;
				UpdateVariablePathToVariableBindings(item.VariableProperty, text, text);
			}
			foreach (VariableToValueBindingItem item2 in variableToValueBindingItems)
			{
				UpdateVariableToValueBindings(item2);
			}
			foreach (ExtraAutoBindingItem extraAutoBindingItem in ExtraAutoBindingItems)
			{
				UpdateExtraAutoBindings(extraAutoBindingItem);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateVariablePathToVariableBindings(DependencyProperty variableProperty, string oldVariablePath, string newVairablePath)
		{
			BindingBase bindingBase = FindCachedBinding(variableProperty);
			BindingBase bindingBase2 = BindingOperations.GetBindingBase(_element, variableProperty);
			bool flag = bindingBase != null && bindingBase == bindingBase2;
			if (!_control.AutoBind || string.IsNullOrEmpty(newVairablePath))
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(_element, variableProperty);
				}
				if (bindingBase != null)
				{
					_bindings.Remove(variableProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (!flag || !(oldVariablePath == newVairablePath))
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding(newVairablePath);
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(_element, variableProperty, binding);
				_bindings[variableProperty] = binding;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateVariableToValueBindings(VariableToValueBindingItem item)
		{
			BindingBase bindingBase = FindCachedBinding(item.ValueProperty);
			BindingBase bindingBase2 = BindingOperations.GetBindingBase(_element, item.ValueProperty);
			if ((bindingBase2 != null || item.IgnoreLocalValue || _element.ReadLocalValue(item.ValueProperty) == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) && bindingBase2 == bindingBase)
			{
				object value = _element.GetValue(item.VariableProperty);
				PropertyDescriptorCollection sourceProperties = null;
				if (_control.AutoBind && value != null)
				{
					sourceProperties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(value);
				}
				Binding binding = Helpers.UpdateVariableRelativeBinding(sourceProperties, _element, item.ValueProperty, item.VariableProperty.Name, item.SubPropertyPath, item.Mode, item.Trigger, bindingBase as Binding, item.Converter);
				if (binding == null)
				{
					_bindings.Remove(item.ValueProperty);
				}
				else
				{
					_bindings[item.ValueProperty] = binding;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateExtraAutoBindings(ExtraAutoBindingItem item)
		{
			BindingBase bindingBase = FindCachedBinding(item.TargetProperty);
			BindingBase bindingBase2 = BindingOperations.GetBindingBase(_element, item.TargetProperty);
			if ((bindingBase2 != null || _element.ReadLocalValue(item.TargetProperty) == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue) && bindingBase2 == bindingBase && bindingBase2 != item.Binding)
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(_element, item.TargetProperty);
				if (_bindings.ContainsKey(item.TargetProperty))
				{
					_bindings.Remove(item.TargetProperty);
				}
				if (_control.AutoBind)
				{
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(_element, item.TargetProperty, item.Binding);
					_bindings[item.TargetProperty] = item.Binding;
				}
			}
		}

		private List<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> GetVariablePathToVariableBindingItems()
		{
			List<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem> list = new List<VariablePathToVariableBindingItem>();
			_control.GenerateVariablePathToVariableBindingItems(list);
			return list;
		}

		private List<VariableToValueBindingItem> GetVariableToValueBindingItems()
		{
			List<VariableToValueBindingItem> list = new List<VariableToValueBindingItem>();
			_control.GenerateVariableToValueBindingItems(list);
			return list;
		}

		private BindingBase FindCachedBinding(DependencyProperty targetProperty)
		{
			if (_bindings.ContainsKey(targetProperty))
			{
				return _bindings[targetProperty];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public bool IsInAutoBinding(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			foreach (VariablePathToVariableBindingItem variablePathToVariableBindingItem in GetVariablePathToVariableBindingItems())
			{
				if (Helpers.PropertyAndBindingMatch(_element, dp, variablePathToVariableBindingItem.VariableProperty, FindCachedBinding(variablePathToVariableBindingItem.VariableProperty)))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			foreach (VariableToValueBindingItem variableToValueBindingItem in GetVariableToValueBindingItems())
			{
				if (Helpers.PropertyAndBindingMatch(_element, dp, variableToValueBindingItem.ValueProperty, FindCachedBinding(variableToValueBindingItem.ValueProperty)))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			foreach (ExtraAutoBindingItem extraAutoBindingItem in ExtraAutoBindingItems)
			{
				if (Helpers.PropertyAndBindingMatch(_element, dp, extraAutoBindingItem.TargetProperty, FindCachedBinding(extraAutoBindingItem.TargetProperty)))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class VariablePathToVariableBindingItem
	{
		public DependencyProperty VariablePathProperty { get; private set; }

		public DependencyProperty VariableProperty { get; private set; }

		public VariablePathToVariableBindingItem(DependencyProperty variablePathProperty, DependencyProperty variableProperty)
		{
			if (variablePathProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("variablePathProperty");
			}
			if (variableProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("variableProperty");
			}
			VariablePathProperty = variablePathProperty;
			VariableProperty = variableProperty;
		}
	}
	public class VariableToValueBindingItem
	{
		public DependencyProperty VariableProperty { get; private set; }

		public DependencyProperty ValueProperty { get; private set; }

		public string SubPropertyPath { get; private set; }

		public BindingMode Mode { get; set; }

		public UpdateSourceTrigger Trigger { get; set; }

		public IValueConverter Converter { get; set; }

		public bool IgnoreLocalValue { get; set; }

		public VariableToValueBindingItem(DependencyProperty variableProperty, DependencyProperty valueProperty, string subPropertyPath)
		{
			if (variableProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("variableProperty");
			}
			if (valueProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("valueProperty");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subPropertyPath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("subPropertyPath is null or empty");
			}
			VariableProperty = variableProperty;
			ValueProperty = valueProperty;
			SubPropertyPath = subPropertyPath;
			Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay;
			Trigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged;
			IgnoreLocalValue = false;
		}
	}
	public class ExtraAutoBindingItem
	{
		public DependencyProperty TargetProperty { get; private set; }

		public IList<DependencyProperty> SourceProperties { get; private set; }

		public BindingBase Binding { get; private set; }

		public ExtraAutoBindingItem(DependencyProperty targetProperty, BindingBase binding)
		{
			if (targetProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("targetProperty");
			}
			if (binding == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("binding");
			}
			TargetProperty = targetProperty;
			Binding = binding;
			SourceProperties = new List<DependencyProperty>();
		}

		public ExtraAutoBindingItem(DependencyProperty targetProperty, DependencyProperty sourceProperty, BindingBase binding)
			: this(targetProperty, binding)
		{
			if (sourceProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("sourceProperty");
			}
			SourceProperties.Add(sourceProperty);
		}
	}
	public class AttachedBindingItem
	{
		public DependencyProperty TargetProperty { get; private set; }

		public DependencyProperty SourceProperty { get; private set; }

		public AttachedBindingItem(DependencyProperty targetProperty, DependencyProperty sourceProperty)
		{
			if (targetProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("targetProperty");
			}
			if (sourceProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("sourceProperty");
			}
			TargetProperty = targetProperty;
			SourceProperty = sourceProperty;
		}
	}

	internal static class ImageResourcesService
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, ImageSource> _imageSources = new Dictionary<string, ImageSource>();

		public static ImageSource GetImageSource(string imageFileName)
		{
			if (!_imageSources.ContainsKey(imageFileName))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				string value = "pack://application:,,,/";
				string value2 = ";component/Images/";
				stringBuilder.Append(value);
				stringBuilder.Append(typeof(ImageResourcesService).Assembly.FullName);
				stringBuilder.Append(value2);
				stringBuilder.Append(imageFileName);
				BitmapImage bitmapImage = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
				if (bitmapImage.CanFreeze)
				{
					bitmapImage.Freeze();
				}
				_imageSources.Add(imageFileName, bitmapImage);
			}
			return _imageSources[imageFileName];
		}
	}

	public sealed class SimulatorStatusHelperForControls : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private static SimulatorStatusHelperForControls _default;

		private bool _isRunning;

		private bool _isSimulationRunCompleted;

		private bool _isSimulationRunFailed;

		private bool _isPausedByUser;

		public static SimulatorStatusHelperForControls Default
		{
			get
			{
				if (_default == null)
				{
					_default = new SimulatorStatusHelperForControls();
				}
				return _default;
			}
		}

		public bool IsRunning
		{
			get
			{
				return _isRunning;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (_isRunning != value)
				{
					_isRunning = value;
					OnIsRunningChanged();
					OnPropertyChanged("IsRunning");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsSimulationRunCompleted
		{
			get
			{
				return _isSimulationRunCompleted;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (_isSimulationRunCompleted != value)
				{
					_isSimulationRunCompleted = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsSimulationRunCompleted");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsSimulationRunFailed
		{
			get
			{
				return _isSimulationRunFailed;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (_isSimulationRunFailed != value)
				{
					_isSimulationRunFailed = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsSimulationRunFailed");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsPausedByUser
		{
			get
			{
				return _isPausedByUser;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (_isPausedByUser != value)
				{
					_isPausedByUser = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsPausedByUser");
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler IsRunningChanged;

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		private SimulatorStatusHelperForControls()
		{
		}

		public void UpdateStatuses(bool isRunning, bool isSimulationRunCompleted, bool isSimulationRunFailed, bool isPausedByUser = false)
		{
			IsRunning = isRunning;
			IsSimulationRunCompleted = isSimulationRunCompleted;
			IsSimulationRunFailed = isSimulationRunFailed;
			IsPausedByUser = isPausedByUser;
		}

		private void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}

		private void OnIsRunningChanged()
		{
			if (this.IsRunningChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsRunningChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}
	}
	public class MaskingAndValidatingHelper
	{
		private static Regex _validDoubleRegex;

		private static Regex _maskDoubleRegex;

		private static Regex _validIntWithExponentRegex;

		private static Regex _maskIntWithExponentRegex;

		private static Regex _validPercentageRegex;

		private static Regex _maskPercentageRegex;

		private static Regex _validIntegerRegex;

		private static Regex _maskIntegerRegex;

		private static Regex _validComponentIDRegex;

		private static Regex _validComponentNameRegex;

		private static Regex _validIdRegex;

		private static Regex _validFortranVariableRegex;

		private static Regex _validSubroutineRegex;

		private static Regex _maskSubroutineRegex;

		private static Regex _validFortranExpressionRegex;

		public static Regex validDoubleRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validDoubleRegex == null)
				{
					_validDoubleRegex = new Regex("^$|^[+-]?\\d*(\\.\\d*)?([Ee][+-]?\\d+)?$");
				}
				return _validDoubleRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskDoubleRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_maskDoubleRegex == null)
				{
					_maskDoubleRegex = new Regex("^[+-]?\\d*(\\.)?\\d*([eE][+-]?\\d*)?$");
				}
				return _maskDoubleRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex validIntWithExponentRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validIntWithExponentRegex == null)
				{
					_validIntWithExponentRegex = new Regex("^$|^[+-]?\\d+([Ee][+]?\\d+)?$");
				}
				return _validIntWithExponentRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskIntWithExponentRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_maskIntWithExponentRegex == null)
				{
					_maskIntWithExponentRegex = new Regex("^[+-]?\\d*([eE][+]?\\d*)?$");
				}
				return _maskIntWithExponentRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex validPercentageRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validPercentageRegex == null)
				{
					_validPercentageRegex = new Regex("^$|^[+-]?\\d*(\\.\\d*)?([Ee][+-]?\\d+)?%?$");
				}
				return _validPercentageRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskPercentageRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_maskPercentageRegex == null)
				{
					_maskPercentageRegex = new Regex("^[+-]?\\d*(\\.)?\\d*([eE][+-]?\\d*)?%?$");
				}
				return _maskPercentageRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex validIntegerRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validIntegerRegex == null)
				{
					_validIntegerRegex = new Regex("^[+-]?\\d*$");
				}
				return _validIntegerRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskIntegerRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_maskIntegerRegex == null)
				{
					_maskIntegerRegex = new Regex("^[+-]?\\d*$");
				}
				return _maskIntegerRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex validComponentIDRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validComponentIDRegex == null)
				{
					_validComponentIDRegex = new Regex("^$|^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9\\$\\+\\*\\.\\(\\)\\[\\{\\,\\:\\-\\/\\=\\&\\]\\}\\<\\>]*$");
				}
				return _validComponentIDRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskComponentIDRegex => validComponentIDRegex;

		public static Regex validComponentNameRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validComponentNameRegex == null)
				{
					_validComponentNameRegex = new Regex("^$|^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9\\$\\+\\*\\.\\(\\)\\[\\{\\,\\:\\-\\/\\=\\&\\]\\}\\<\\>\\x20\\u0020]*$");
				}
				return _validComponentNameRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskComponentNameRegex => validComponentNameRegex;

		public static Regex validIdRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validIdRegex == null)
				{
					_validIdRegex = new Regex("^$|^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9\\,\\$\\+\\-\\:]*$");
				}
				return _validIdRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskIdRegex => validIdRegex;

		public static Regex validFortranVariableRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validFortranVariableRegex == null)
				{
					_validFortranVariableRegex = new Regex("^$|^[zZ][a-hj-zA-HJ-Z0-9]?$|^[zZ][a-hj-zA-HJ-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9]*$|^[iI][a-yA-Y0-9]?$|^[iI][a-yA-Y0-9][a-zA-Z0-9]*$|^[a-hj-yA-HJ-Y][a-zA-Z0-9]*$|^[a-zA-Z][a-hj-yA-HJ-Y0-9]*$");
				}
				return _validFortranVariableRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskFortranVariableRegex => validFortranVariableRegex;

		public static Regex validSubroutineRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validSubroutineRegex == null)
				{
					_validSubroutineRegex = new Regex("^$|^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9\\$\\+\\*\\.\\(\\)\\[\\{\\,\\:\\-\\/\\=\\&\\]\\}\\<\\>\\x20\\u0020]*$");
				}
				return _validSubroutineRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskSubroutineRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_maskSubroutineRegex == null)
				{
					_maskSubroutineRegex = new Regex("^$|^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9\\$\\+\\*\\.\\(\\)\\[\\{\\,\\:\\-\\/\\=\\&\\]\\}\\<\\>]*$");
				}
				return _maskSubroutineRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex validFortranExpressionRegex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_validFortranExpressionRegex == null)
				{
					_validFortranExpressionRegex = new Regex("^[a-zA-Z0-9\\+\\-\\*\\/\\(\\)\\.\\,\\$]*$");
				}
				return _validFortranExpressionRegex;
			}
		}

		public static Regex maskFortranExpressionRegex => validFortranExpressionRegex;

		public static bool maskDoubleValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			testString = testString.Replace(GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
			if (!maskDoubleRegex.IsMatch(testString))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString) && (testString.Contains("e") || testString.Contains("E")))
			{
				if (double.TryParse(testString, out var result))
				{
					if (result < -1E+300 || result > 1E+300)
					{
						return false;
					}
					int num = result.ToString().IndexOf("e", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
					if (num > -1 && num > 0 && int.TryParse(result.ToString().Substring(num + 1), out var result2) && (result2 < -300 || result2 > 300))
					{
						return false;
					}
					num = testString.IndexOf("e", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
					if (result == 0.0 && double.TryParse(testString.Substring(0, num), out var result3) && result3 != 0.0)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				else if (!double.TryParse(testString, out result) && validDoubleRegex.IsMatch(testString))
				{
					int num2 = testString.IndexOf("e", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
					if (num2 > -1 && int.TryParse(testString.Substring(num2 + 1), out var result4) && result4 >= -300 && result4 <= 300)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskIntWithExponentValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			testString = testString.Replace(GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
			if (!maskIntWithExponentRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString) && (testString.Contains("e") || testString.Contains("E")))
			{
				if (double.TryParse(testString, out var result))
				{
					if (result > 2147483647.0 || result < -2147483648.0)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				else if (!double.TryParse(testString, out result) && validIntWithExponentRegex.IsMatch(testString))
				{
					int num = testString.IndexOf("e", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
					if (num > -1 && int.TryParse(testString.Substring(num + 1), out var result2) && result2 <= 9)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskPercentageValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			testString = testString.Replace(GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
			if (!maskPercentageRegex.IsMatch(testString) || (MaxLength != 0 && testString.Length > MaxLength))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString) && (testString.Contains("e") || testString.Contains("E")))
			{
				if (double.TryParse(testString, out var result))
				{
					int num = result.ToString().IndexOf("e", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
					if (num > -1 && num > 0 && int.TryParse(result.ToString().Substring(num + 1), out var result2) && (result2 < -300 || result2 > 300))
					{
						return false;
					}
					num = testString.IndexOf("e", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
					if (result == 0.0 && num > -1 && double.TryParse(testString.Substring(0, num), out var result3) && result3 != 0.0)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				else if (validPercentageRegex.IsMatch(testString))
				{
					int num2 = testString.IndexOf("e", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
					if (num2 > -1 && int.TryParse(testString.Substring(num2 + 1).Replace("%", string.Empty), out var result4) && result4 >= -300 && result4 <= 300)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskIntegerValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!maskIntegerRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskComponentIDValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!maskComponentIDRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskComponentNameValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!maskComponentNameRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskAsciiCharValue(string testString, int maxLength)
		{
			if (ArePureAsciiCharacters(testString))
			{
				return testString.Length <= maxLength;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool maskIdValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!maskIdRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskFortranVariableValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!maskFortranVariableRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskSubroutineValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!maskSubroutineRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool maskFortranExpressionValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!maskFortranExpressionRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validDoubleValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			string input = testString.Replace(GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
			if (!validDoubleRegex.IsMatch(input))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString) && !double.TryParse(testString, out var _))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validIntWithExponentValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validIntWithExponentRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString) && (!double.TryParse(testString, out var result) || result < -2147483648.0 || result > 2147483647.0))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validPercentageValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			string text = testString.Replace(GetSystemDecimalSymbol(), ".");
			if (!validPercentageRegex.IsMatch(text) || (MaxLength != 0 && text.Length > MaxLength))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString) && !double.TryParse(testString.Replace("%", string.Empty), out var _))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validIntegerValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validIntegerRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString) && !int.TryParse(testString, out var _))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validComponentIDValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validComponentIDRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validComponentNameValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validComponentNameRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validIdValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validIdRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validFortranVariableValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validFortranVariableRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validSubroutineValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validSubroutineRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool validFortranExpressionValue(string testString, int MaxLength)
		{
			if (!validFortranExpressionRegex.IsMatch(testString) || testString.Length > MaxLength)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static string GetSystemDecimalSymbol()
		{
			return 0.1.ToString().Replace("0", string.Empty).Replace("1", string.Empty);
		}

		public static string GetPreviewInputStringWhenAddText(TextBox tb, string previewAddingText)
		{
			string text = tb.Text;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (text != null)
			{
				int selectionStart = tb.SelectionStart;
				selectionStart = ((selectionStart > text.Length) ? text.Length : selectionStart);
				int selectionLength = tb.SelectionLength;
				int num = selectionStart + selectionLength;
				num = ((num > tb.Text.Length) ? tb.Text.Length : num);
				string obj = ((selectionStart == 0) ? string.Empty : text.Substring(0, selectionStart));
				string text2 = ((num >= text.Length) ? string.Empty : text.Substring(selectionStart + selectionLength));
				return obj + previewAddingText + text2;
			}
			return previewAddingText;
		}

		public static string GetPreviewInputStringWhenDeleteText(TextBox tb, Key key)
		{
			string text = tb.Text;
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (text != null)
			{
				int selectionStart = tb.SelectionStart;
				selectionStart = ((selectionStart > text.Length) ? text.Length : selectionStart);
				int selectionLength = tb.SelectionLength;
				int num = selectionStart + selectionLength;
				num = ((num > tb.Text.Length) ? tb.Text.Length : num);
				string obj = ((selectionStart == 0) ? string.Empty : text.Substring(0, selectionStart - ((key == Key.Back && selectionLength == 0) ? 1 : 0)));
				string text2 = ((num == text.Length) ? string.Empty : text.Substring(selectionStart + ((key == Key.Delete && selectionLength == 0) ? 1 : selectionLength)));
				result = obj + text2;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static string GetProductName()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			if (!Application.Current.ToString().Contains("Plus"))
			{
				return "Aspen Properties";
			}
			return "Aspen Plus";
		}

		private static string GetProductI18nName()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			if (!(GetProductName() == "Aspen Plus"))
			{
				return AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ProdName_AspenProperties;
			}
			return AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ProdName_AspenPlus;
		}

		public static void PopupInvalidValueWarningMsg(string invalidValue)
		{
			AesMessageBox.Show(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.InvalidValueWarningMsgPre + invalidValue + AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.InvalidValueWarningMsgTear, GetProductI18nName(), AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage.Error);
		}

		public static void PopupInvalidValueWarningMsg(string invalidValue, AesMaskOption Mask)
		{
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentId)
			{
				AesMessageBox.Show("\"" + invalidValue + AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.InvalidCompIDWarningMsg, GetProductI18nName(), AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage.Error);
			}
			if (Mask == AesMaskOption.ComponentName)
			{
				AesMessageBox.Show("\"" + invalidValue + AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.InvalidCompNameWarningMsg, GetProductI18nName(), AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage.Error);
			}
		}

		public static void PopupExceedMaxLengthWarningMsg(int maxLength)
		{
			AesMessageBox.Show(string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.ExceedMaxLengthWarningMsg, maxLength), GetProductI18nName(), AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage.Error);
		}

		public static void PopupOutOfWarningMsg(double? UpperLimit, double? LowerLimit, bool bGreaterThanLowerLimit = false)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (UpperLimit.HasValue && LowerLimit.HasValue)
			{
				text = ((!bGreaterThanLowerLimit) ? string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.OutOfRangeWarningMsgForBetween, LowerLimit.Value, UpperLimit.Value) : string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.OutOfRangeWarningMsgForHOHC, LowerLimit.Value, UpperLimit.Value));
			}
			else if (!UpperLimit.HasValue && LowerLimit.HasValue)
			{
				text = string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.OutOfRangeWarningMsgForLower, LowerLimit.Value);
			}
			else if (UpperLimit.HasValue && !LowerLimit.HasValue)
			{
				text = string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.OutOfRangeWarningMsgForUpper, UpperLimit.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				AesMessageBox.Show(text, GetProductI18nName(), AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		public static bool CanTextMaskable(string text, int maxLength, AesMaskOption mask, bool ignoreMaxLength = false)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result = false;
			switch (mask)
			{
			case AesMaskOption.None:
				result = true;
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Integer:
				result = maskIntegerValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Double:
				if (ignoreMaxLength)
				{
					maxLength = 0;
				}
				result = maskDoubleValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Id:
				result = maskIdValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.FortranVariable:
				result = maskFortranVariableValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Subroutine:
				result = maskSubroutineValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.FortranExpression:
				result = maskFortranExpressionValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.ComponentId:
				result = maskComponentIDValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Percentage:
				if (ignoreMaxLength)
				{
					maxLength = 0;
				}
				result = maskPercentageValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.ComponentName:
				result = maskComponentNameValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.AsciiChar:
				result = maskAsciiCharValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool CanTextValiable(string text, int maxLength, AesMaskOption mask, bool ignoreMaxLength = false)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result = false;
			switch (mask)
			{
			case AesMaskOption.None:
				result = true;
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Integer:
				result = validIntegerValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Double:
				if (ignoreMaxLength)
				{
					maxLength = 0;
				}
				result = validDoubleValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Id:
				result = validIdValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.FortranVariable:
				result = validFortranVariableValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Subroutine:
				result = validSubroutineValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.FortranExpression:
				result = validFortranExpressionValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.ComponentId:
				result = validComponentIDValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.Percentage:
				if (ignoreMaxLength)
				{
					maxLength = 0;
				}
				result = validPercentageValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.ComponentName:
				result = validComponentNameValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			case AesMaskOption.AsciiChar:
				result = IsValidAsciiCharValue(text, maxLength);
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static bool IsValidAsciiCharValue(string text, int maxLength)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (ArePureAsciiCharacters(text))
			{
				return text.Length <= maxLength;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool ArePureAsciiCharacters(string str)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(str))
			{
				return true;
			}
			char[] array = str.ToCharArray();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if ((uint)array[i] > 255u)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static void PopupNoneAsciiCharMsg()
		{
			AesMessageBox.Show(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.CannotInputNonASCIIChar, GetProductI18nName(), AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
		}

		public static void PopupInvalidValue(string value)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				AesMessageBox.Show(string.Format(AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Properties.Resources.InvalidValueMsg, value), GetProductI18nName(), AesMessageBoxButton.OK, AesMessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
			}
		}
	}
	public class RoutedEventArgs<T> : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public T Value { get; private set; }

		public RoutedEventArgs(T value, RoutedEvent routedEvent, object source)
			: base(routedEvent, source)
		{
			Value = value;
		}
	}
	public class SimulatorStatusBox : ContentControl, ISimulatorStatusReceiver
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty CheckSimulatorStatusProperty;

		public SimulatorStatusHelperForControls SimulatorStatus => SimulatorStatusHelperForControls.Default;

		static SimulatorStatusBox()
		{
			CheckSimulatorStatusProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("CheckSimulatorStatus", typeof(bool), typeof(SimulatorStatusBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnCheckSimulatorStatusPropertyChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SimulatorStatusBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(SimulatorStatusBox)));
		}

		public static bool GetCheckSimulatorStatus(UIElement target)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			return (bool)target.GetValue(CheckSimulatorStatusProperty);
		}

		public static void SetCheckSimulatorStatus(UIElement target, bool value)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			target.SetValue(CheckSimulatorStatusProperty, value);
		}

		private static void OnCheckSimulatorStatusPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is UIElement target)
			{
				if ((bool)e.NewValue)
				{
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(target, UIElement.IsEnabledProperty, new Binding
					{
						Source = SimulatorStatusHelperForControls.Default,
						Path = new PropertyPath("IsRunning"),
						Converter = new BooleanInverseConverter(),
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					});
				}
				else
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(target, UIElement.IsEnabledProperty);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(bool), typeof(bool))]
	public class BooleanInverseConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value as bool? != true;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value as bool? != true;
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.WpfControls.Engineering.Internal

	internal static class Helpers
	{
		public static Binding UpdateVariableRelativeBinding(PropertyDescriptorCollection sourceProperties, DependencyObject element, DependencyProperty targetProperty, string variableSourceProperty, string variableProperty, BindingMode bindingMode, UpdateSourceTrigger trigger, Binding existingBinding, IValueConverter converter)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = sourceProperties?.Find(variableProperty, ignoreCase: false);
			if (propertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				if (bindingMode != BindingMode.OneWay && propertyDescriptor.IsReadOnly)
				{
					bindingMode = BindingMode.OneWay;
				}
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(variableSourceProperty + ".");
				stringBuilder.Append(variableProperty);
				string text = stringBuilder.ToString();
				if (existingBinding != null && (existingBinding.Mode != bindingMode || existingBinding.Path.Path != text || existingBinding.UpdateSourceTrigger != trigger))
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(element, targetProperty);
					existingBinding = null;
				}
				if (existingBinding == null)
				{
					existingBinding = new Binding
					{
						RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
						Path = new PropertyPath(text),
						Mode = bindingMode,
						UpdateSourceTrigger = trigger,
						Converter = converter
					};
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(element, targetProperty, existingBinding);
				}
			}
			else if (existingBinding != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(element, targetProperty);
				existingBinding = null;
			}
			return existingBinding;
		}

		internal static bool PropertyAndBindingMatch(DependencyObject element, DependencyProperty property, DependencyProperty testProperty, BindingBase testBinding)
		{
			if (element == null || property != testProperty || testBinding == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return BindingOperations.GetBindingBase(element, property) == testBinding;
		}

		internal static void TriggerCommands(this ValueEditor editor, KeyboardEventArgs args, bool onlyWhenReadonly = true)
		{
			if (!onlyWhenReadonly || editor.IsReadOnly)
			{
				TryTriggerCommand(editor, ApplicationCommands.Cut, args);
				TryTriggerCommand(editor, ApplicationCommands.Copy, args);
				TryTriggerCommand(editor, ApplicationCommands.Paste, args);
			}
		}

		private static void TryTriggerCommand(IInputElement element, RoutedCommand command, InputEventArgs args)
		{
			foreach (object inputGesture in command.InputGestures)
			{
				if (inputGesture is KeyGesture keyGesture && keyGesture.Matches(element, args))
				{
					command.Execute(null, element);
					args.Handled = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public static void ProcessKeyInput(AesXamValueEdit control, KeyEventArgs keyEventArgs)
		{
			ProcessKeyInputInternal(control.Mask, keyEventArgs);
		}

		public static void ProcessKeyInput(AesXamComboEdit control, KeyEventArgs keyEventArgs)
		{
			ProcessKeyInputInternal(control.Mask, keyEventArgs);
		}

		public static void ProcessKeyInput(AesTextEdit control, KeyEventArgs keyEventArgs)
		{
			ProcessKeyInputInternal(control.Mask, keyEventArgs);
		}

		private static void ProcessKeyInputInternal(AesMaskOption mask, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key != Key.Escape && e.Key != Key.Tab && e.Key != Key.Return && (e.Key < Key.F1 || e.Key > Key.F24) && e.Key != Key.Up && e.Key != Key.Down && e.Key != Key.Left && e.Key != Key.Right)
			{
				bool? flag = null;
				if (mask == AesMaskOption.Integer)
				{
					flag = ((e.Key != Key.OemMinus && e.Key != Key.OemPlus && e.Key != Key.Add && e.Key != Key.Subtract && (e.Key < Key.D0 || e.Key > Key.D9) && (e.Key < Key.NumPad0 || e.Key > Key.NumPad9)) ? new bool?(false) : new bool?(true));
				}
				if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				if (e.Key == Key.Decimal && MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol() != "." && e.OriginalSource is IInputElement inputElement)
				{
					inputElement.RaiseEvent(new TextCompositionEventArgs(e.Device, new TextComposition(InputManager.Current, inputElement, MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol()))
					{
						RoutedEvent = UIElement.TextInputEvent
					});
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class DoubleValueDisplayedAsStringConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value.ToString()) && value.GetType() == typeof(string))
			{
				string systemDecimalSymbol = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
				if (systemDecimalSymbol != ".")
				{
					return value.ToString().Replace(".", systemDecimalSymbol);
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value.ToString()) && value.GetType() == typeof(string))
			{
				string systemDecimalSymbol = MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
				if (systemDecimalSymbol != ".")
				{
					return value.ToString().Replace(systemDecimalSymbol, ".");
				}
			}
			return value;
		}
	}
